# v2.13 The Bench Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** a builder's one project goes on a public, filterable leaderboard (the Bench) as soon as EarnKit confirms it is theirs; their agent posts one short update a week that EarnKit pins its own checked facts to; the best updates make a public feed; and every reply points at money (grants that fit, or a raise through a tokens.fun coin). The builder bonus is gone from everything a builder sees.

**Architecture:**
- **Tasks 1 to 4 are the engine:** the `EARNKIT_BENCH` switch, the additive schema, the new event types and pure ISO-week helpers (T1); one project at `find` (`identity.project`, `builder_profiles.main_project_key`, the proof line in the same turn) (T2); reads by name only, no person query, commit counts per week (T3); the weekly facts engine, the one-project score, `builder_projects.chains/streak/weeks_shipping/ship_weeks` and the cron's weekly step (T4).
- **Tasks 5 to 9 are the builder side:** `tell update/updates` and the 280-character text rule (T5); feed points, the owner's `feed:pin`/`feed:hide` and `/feed` (T6); the percentile rule and per-chain pools, with the copy-rule change (T7); the reply rewrite ("Ways to get funded", the last-line order, 2a and 2b, `schedule_weekly_update`) (T8); raising (T9).
- **Tasks 10 to 12 are the public surfaces:** `/bench` with URL filters and the `/leaderboard` redirect (T10); the profile redesign and the card (T11); the landing page (T12).
- **Tasks 13 and 14 remove the builder bonus:** EarnKit side, gated where a builder can see it, plus fee claiming that reads both splits (T13); the tokens.fun split change in its own repo (T14).
- **Tasks 15 and 16 close:** docs and the acceptance runbook (T15); the journey test that walks acceptance steps 1 to 7 on fakes (T16).

**Tech Stack:** Next.js 16 app router (route handlers, `next/og`, `unstable_cache`, `redirect`), postgres.js, zod v4, React 19 `renderToStaticMarkup` for markup tests, node:test via `tsx --test` (EarnKit); vitest via `bunx vitest run` (tokens.fun). No new dependency, no new service.

**Spec:** docs/design-docs-and-implementation-plans/v2.13-the-bench.md

Read with it: `v2.12.1-where-you-stand-plan.md` (house style, rulings S1 to S16 still hold unless a ruling below replaces them), `docs/codebase-context/architecture-guide.md` sections "v2.12.1 Where you stand" and "v2.12.2, a reported login's own repos". Owner memories: `builder-copy-style`, `cost-discipline`, `targets-effort-only`, `pr-branch-from-origin`.

## Global Constraints

v2.12.1's Global Constraints carry over (additive schema, the event log's append-only and never-in-the-way rules, fail strict on verification, test hygiene, the shared-DB rules). v2.13 adds, verbatim from the spec where quoted:

- **Branch:** `v2.13` in `/Users/jenil/earnkit-v213` (EarnKit) and `earnkit-fee-split` in `/Users/jenil/tokensfun-fee-split` (tokens.fun, T14 only). Never push, merge or deploy without the owner's go. Never run a bare `git stash`. Every commit ends with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- **Flag:** `EARNKIT_BENCH` (`benchEnabled()` = `EARNKIT_BUILDER_SCORE=on` and `EARNKIT_BENCH=on`). "off = production as it is today, v2.12.2 with `EARNKIT_BUILDER_SCORE=on`": flag off must equal today's production behaviour exactly; every user-visible change is gated.
- **Copy rules:** short, plain words; no em dashes; no all-caps labels; no jargon: "outcome", "percentile", "signal"; chain names allowed only in rank lines and chips; no "written by an agent" label (R6); the builder bonus is never mentioned.
- **Percentile:** `P = 100 * rank / total`; "Top P%" only when `total >= 20` and `P <= 50`; P of 1 or more rounded up to a whole number; P under 1 rounded up to one decimal, never below 0.1; under 20 ranked: "#N of M", only in the top half; outside the top half: no rank line. A chain chip (and a chain rank line) only when 10 or more ranked projects are on that chain.
- **Feed points:** `went_live` 40; `won` 40; `users_up` (n people) `min(30, 6 * log2(1 + n))`; `earned` ($d) `min(20, 5 * log2(1 + d / 10))`; `shipped_code` 10; `streak` (w weeks) `min(10, w)`. Ties: the higher Bench score first. Updates with no facts never reach the feed.
- **Updates:** "One paragraph, at most 280 characters, plain words. Links are allowed only to the project's own live URL or repo." "One update per project per ISO week: a second `tell update` in the same week replaces the text."
- **Chains:** shown only Base, Robinhood Chain and Arc, as logos with the name as alt text and a tooltip.
- **Cost flat:** no new paid service; no person GraphQL query (`readPersonGithub`) under the flag, on the reply path or the daily read; GitHub is one share of a 25-repo by-name call per builder per day; the cron gains only the weekly facts step (Mondays) and the feed rank step (once a day), inside the existing hourly run.
- **schema.sql must stay rerunnable:** `pnpm db:migrate` reruns the whole file before every deploy, and any CHECK re-added more than once lists identical values every time (`schema-rerun.test.ts`).
- **Sponsor campaigns untouched:** `campaigns`, escrow, the sponsor flow and EarnKit Campaign #1 are a separate product; no line of theirs changes.
- **Tools stay five** (`find`, `prepare`, `confirm`, `tell`, `score`), each description under 800 characters, the server instructions under 800 (`mcp.test.ts`). With the flag off, every schema and description is byte for byte today's.
- **Commands** (EarnKit, repo root `/Users/jenil/earnkit-v213`):
  - one file: `pnpm exec tsx --test --import ./apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/fixtures/test-guard.ts apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/<name>.test.ts`
  - DB-gated cases: prefix `DATABASE_URL=postgres://postgres@127.0.0.1:55442/postgres?sslmode=disable`, after `pnpm db:migrate` on that URL (run it twice: the second run proves the schema reruns).
  - before each commit: the task's test files, then `pnpm --filter frontend exec tsc --noEmit`. End of every task: `pnpm -s test` (with the local `DATABASE_URL`), `pnpm db:check`, `pnpm lint`. T10, T11, T12 also `pnpm build`.
  - tokens.fun (T14): `cd /Users/jenil/tokensfun-fee-split && bunx vitest run <files>`, then `bunx tsc --noEmit`.
- **Test hygiene:** `fixtures/test-guard.ts` clears `EARNKIT_BENCH` (T1), so every v2.13 test file sets `process.env.EARNKIT_BUILDER_SCORE = 'on'; process.env.EARNKIT_BENCH = 'on';` at the top and every flag-off parity case deletes `EARNKIT_BENCH` inside the case and restores it after. Rows with unique keys across people (project keys, X ids, handles) carry a per-run suffix; rank text from the shared DB is asserted by shape unless the call is limited with `only`.

## Review Focus

1. **Percentile boundaries.** P exactly 1 ("Top 1%"), P between 0 and 0.1 ("Top 0.1%", never "Top 0%"), P exactly 50 (shown) and 50.0001 (not), 19 ranked ("#N of M", top half only) against 20 ranked (percent), a chain with 9 ranked projects (no chip, no chain line) against 10. Pinned by `rank.test.ts` 7.1 to 7.3 and `bench-rank.test.ts` 7.4 in T7.
2. **An update that smuggles a link or a secret.** A link to anything but the project's own live origin or GitHub repo (`https://evil.example`, `github.com/someone-else/x`, `recipe.app.evil.example`, a bare `evil.example`, a defanged `evil[.]example`), a private key, a GitHub token, a seed phrase or an address is refused with a plain agent note and nothing is stored; 281 characters is refused; a second post in the same ISO week replaces the text instead of adding a row. Pinned by `update-text.test.ts` 5.1 to 5.4 and `updates.test.ts` 5.6 in T5.
3. **An agent that names a repo it does not own.** The project shows "Confirming", is never ranked, never on the Bench, never in the feed, and `tell update` for it is refused, until the proof line pushed by the repo owner is read; a different project sent later never replaces the first one. Pinned by `main-project.test.ts` 2.4 and 2.5 in T2 and `updates.test.ts` 5.7 in T5.
4. **Flag off is production.** With `EARNKIT_BENCH` unset: `find`, `tell` and `score` schemas and descriptions are byte for byte today's, the reply heading is "Ways to earn right now", `/bench` and `/feed` are 404, `/leaderboard` is today's board, the landing hero is "Earn rewards for building" with the builder bonus pill, and the cron runs no weekly or feed step. Pinned by `bench-flag.test.ts` 1.2 (T1), `mcp.test.ts` 2.7 (T2), `v213-find.test.ts` 8.9 (T8), `bench-page.test.ts` 10.6 (T10), `landing.test.ts` 12.4 (T12), `cron.test.ts` 4.8 (T4).
5. **Week edges.** An update posted Sunday 23:59 UTC and another Monday 00:00 UTC land in two ISO weeks; ISO week 53 (2026-12-28 is in 2026-W53) and the year turn (2027-01-03 is still 2026-W53) give the right Monday; a streak survives a Monday with nothing posted yet (it counts back from last week) and breaks on a skipped week; `hide: "2026-10-14"` (a Wednesday) hides the week of Monday 2026-10-12. Pinned by `weeks.test.ts` 1.4 to 1.6 (T1) and `updates.test.ts` 5.8 (T5).

---

## Plan rulings (spec ambiguities and conflicts with the code, resolved)

- **B1, the flag needs the score flag.** `benchEnabled()` is on only with both `EARNKIT_BUILDER_SCORE=on` and `EARNKIT_BENCH=on`: the Bench is built on v2.12.1's tables and readers.
- **B2, where the project comes from when the agent is old.** `identity.project` wins; else `identity.projects[0]`; else nothing. For a person who already has projects and whose agent never sends one, `main_project_key` is picked once by the first rescore under the flag: the confirmed project with the most people, then the most recently active (stand-view's order). Once set it never changes on its own: a different project sent later is stored in `identity` but does not replace it (R1; a second project is out of scope). The owner can clear the column by hand.
- **B3, the proof line in the same turn.** The `find` reply under the flag carries `proof: { file: 'earnkit.txt', line, agent_note }` (the v2.9 `tokenLineFor` line, recorded with `noteLineIssued` so commits after it count) whenever the main project has a GitHub repo and is not confirmed. `proofLineLogin` needs `installations.github_user` to equal the repo owner: when it is empty, `find` fills it with the owner of `identity.project.repo` (only when `identity.github_login` is absent or equals that owner), which proves nothing alone (the push must come from that account). With `agent_can_build: false` the reply carries the GitHub sign-in link instead (`createVerifyLink`, purpose `claim`).
- **B4, "within one more reply".** While the main project is unconfirmed and a proof line was issued, the on-demand refresh may run every 60 seconds (`CONFIRMING_REFRESH_MS`), not 10 minutes, and its proof-line check runs for the main repo alone with a 4-second floor (`MAIN_PROOF_MIN_MS`) instead of v2.12.2's 15 seconds. The cron keeps checking hourly as today.
- **B5, ranked means confirmed.** "The project goes on the Bench as soon as the proof line is read." Under the flag `ranked` = the main project is confirmed (a ship is not required), the ring is known (v2.12.1 rule), and the listed rules (claimed X, not hidden, not held). The score is v2 computed on the main project's inputs only.
- **B6, facts EarnKit can date.** `shipped_code`: `commit_counts[week]` of the main repo's newest reading. `went_live`: the first week ever that the site answered, an EarnKit hosting or a deploy happened. `users_up`: the counted people (anti-farming weight above 0, real use only) whose first touch fell in the week. `earned`: creator fee claims logged in `fee_claims` (role `creator`, the coin's creator wallet) in the week, priced with that wallet's `token_fees` USD per WETH, plus announced prizes captured in the week. `won`: announced wins captured in the week. `streak`: the run of ISO weeks with `shipped_code`, `went_live` or an EarnKit deploy that ends at that week (shown when 2 or more). `raising`: the project is raising at the time the card renders (not stored per week).
- **B7, one more internal column and the grid.** `builder_projects.ship_weeks text[]` (the ISO weeks that count for the streak, last 53) is added next to the spec's `chains`, `streak` and `weeks_shipping`, so the 52-week grid needs no event scan. Internal only.
- **B8, two hides.** The builder's hide (`tell update { hide }`) sets `project_updates.hidden` (leaves the profile and the feed). The owner's `pnpm feed:hide` sets a separate `feed_hidden` column (leaves the feed, stays on the profile). `text` is nullable: a week with facts and no update is a row with `text IS NULL` ("weeks with facts but no update show the chips alone").
- **B9, "Started tracking".** The first rescore after `main_project_key` is set writes the week's row with `facts.started = { live, weeks, people }`; the profile renders it as its own card ("Started tracking Recipe planner.") as the oldest card, and that week's update text, if any, as a separate card. It never reaches the feed.
- **B10, knowing whether the agent can schedule.** `tell updates { on: true, scheduled: true }` records `builder_profiles.updates_scheduled_at` (one internal column). Ask 4 ("post this week's update") is used only when updates are on, the week has no text, and `updates_scheduled_at` is null.
- **B11, the last line drops v2.12.1's other steps.** Under the flag the last line is only the spec's six (build, go_live, updates, post_update, coin or raise, enter). count_past, confirm, tag_future and payment are not offered; their handlers stay for flag off.
- **B12, the 2b "ideas" wording.** "Arc pays up to $10K for payment apps." needs a short funder name and a "for" phrase the program files do not have. A new optional program-file field `pitch: { who, for }` (at most 20 and 60 characters) supplies them (`arc-microgrants`: `{ "who": "Arc", "for": "payment apps" }`, `base-builder-grants`: `{ "who": "Base", "for": "a live app" }`); a program without it reads "<title> pays <prize> for a small app.". `who` is a name (exempt from the copy rule, like a program title). The build ask names the starter by a new `Starter.offer` ("a tip jar on Arc").
- **B13, the raise line and the wallet.** The spec's line is used as written when no fee wallet is known. With a known wallet the v2.8.1 rule (the wallet is shown before the yes) adds ", paid to 0x12ab…9f3e" before the final period. `streak_five` is a new `coin_moments.moment`; a coin launched before v2.13 is offered "Want to show you're raising with $X?" once (recorded in `standing_seen.raise_asked`).
- **B14, the X photo.** The photo is written at every X sign-in that reaches `bindX` (the claim, a second agent's claim of the same X). `bindX` keeps the stored picture when X returns none. A stale picture falls back to the initials in the browser (`onError`). No new sign-in surface and no X read outside a sign-in (no paid X API).
- **B15, chain logos.** `public/images/chains/base.svg` is drawn in the plan (Base's mark is a blue square). Arc and Robinhood Chain ship as monogram SVGs with the name as alt text until the owner drops the brand SVGs into the same paths (go-live step).
- **B16, the builder bonus's fee engine stays.** `bonus-ledger.ts` also claims creator and MINI fees and sweeps the Safe; it keeps running. Under the flag the cron stops traction scoring and the rounds step (so every deposit books to the Safe, "its 20% of coin fees goes to the company treasury"). With the flag off everything is reachable in production, so nothing is deleted in v2.13; T13 lists the modules to delete once the flag is retired.
- **B17, chain names and "commits" in chips.** The copy test strips rank lines ("Top 1% on Base", "#3 of 12 on Arc") and fact chips ("12 commits") before the banned-word check; everywhere else chain names and "commit" stay banned.
- **B18, old steps under the flag.** `schedule_daily_check` is not sent under the flag; `schedule_weekly_update` is sent once with the updates yes (and once more in a later `find` if the agent never confirmed `scheduled: true`).

### Task 1: The switch, the schema, the event types, the week helpers

Everything later writes into these. Nothing here writes a row while the flag is off.

**Files:**
- Modify: `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/builder-score-flag.ts` (add `BENCH_FLAG`, `benchEnabled`)
- Modify: `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/fixtures/test-guard.ts` (`CLEARED` gains `'EARNKIT_BENCH'`)
- Modify: `apps/frontend/db/schema.sql` (both existing `builder_events_type_check` lists, at about lines 1194 and 1352, gain the seven types; one v2.13 block appended at the end)
- Modify: `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/events.ts` (`EVENT_TYPES`)
- Modify: `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/reserved-routes.ts` (`'bench'`, `'feed'`)
- Modify: `apps/frontend/scripts/events-redact.mts` (the profile clear removes `project_updates` rows and the new columns)
- Create: `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/weeks.ts`
- Test: `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/bench-flag.test.ts`, `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/weeks.test.ts`, `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/v213-schema.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `builderScoreEnabled` (builder-score-flag.ts).
- Produces:
  - `BENCH_FLAG = 'EARNKIT_BENCH'`; `benchEnabled(env?: Record<string, string | undefined>): boolean`
  - `EventType` gains `'shipped_code' | 'went_live' | 'users_up' | 'update_posted' | 'update_hidden' | 'raising_on' | 'raising_off'`
  - weeks.ts: `isoWeekOf(d: Date): string` ('2026-W42'), `mondayOf(d: Date): string` ('2026-10-12'), `mondayOfWeek(week: string): string | null`, `weekOfMonday(monday: string): string | null`, `lastFinishedMonday(now: Date): string`, `weekLabel(monday: string): string` ('Week of Oct 12'), `streakOf(weeks: Iterable<string>, now: Date): number`, `weeksShipping(weeks: Iterable<string>, now: Date): number`, `last52(now: Date): string[]` (oldest first)
  - tables and columns: `builder_profiles.main_project_key text`, `updates_on_at timestamptz`, `updates_scheduled_at timestamptz`; `project_updates`; `builder_projects.chains text[]`, `streak int`, `weeks_shipping int`, `ship_weeks text[]`; `projects.raising_at timestamptz`, `coin_tx text`, `coin_chain_id int`; `coin_moments.moment` allows `'streak_five'`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`bench-flag.test.ts`:

```ts
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { BENCH_FLAG, benchEnabled } from './builder-score-flag';

test('1.1 the Bench is on only with the score flag and EARNKIT_BENCH=on', () => {
  assert.equal(BENCH_FLAG, 'EARNKIT_BENCH');
  assert.equal(benchEnabled({}), false);
  assert.equal(benchEnabled({ EARNKIT_BENCH: 'on' }), false, 'needs the score flag');
  assert.equal(benchEnabled({ EARNKIT_BUILDER_SCORE: 'on' }), false);
  assert.equal(benchEnabled({ EARNKIT_BUILDER_SCORE: 'on', EARNKIT_BENCH: 'yes' }), false, 'exactly "on"');
  assert.equal(benchEnabled({ EARNKIT_BUILDER_SCORE: 'on', EARNKIT_BENCH: 'on' }), true);
});

test('1.2 the test guard clears the switch (flag off is the default in every test)', async () => {
  const src = (await import('node:fs')).readFileSync(new URL('./fixtures/test-guard.ts', import.meta.url), 'utf8');
  assert.match(src, /'EARNKIT_BENCH'/);
  assert.equal(process.env.EARNKIT_BENCH, undefined);
});
```

`weeks.test.ts`:

```ts
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { isoWeek } from './source-readers';
import { isoWeekOf, last52, lastFinishedMonday, mondayOf, mondayOfWeek, streakOf, weekLabel, weekOfMonday, weeksShipping } from './weeks';

const d = (s: string) => new Date(s);

test('1.3 isoWeekOf matches the readers\' isoWeek on every day of two years', () => {
  for (let t = Date.UTC(2025, 0, 1); t < Date.UTC(2027, 0, 10); t += 86_400_000) assert.equal(isoWeekOf(new Date(t)), isoWeek(new Date(t)));
});

test('1.4 Sunday 23:59 and Monday 00:00 UTC are two weeks; Mondays come back from any day', () => {
  assert.equal(isoWeekOf(d('2026-10-18T23:59:59Z')), '2026-W42');
  assert.equal(isoWeekOf(d('2026-10-19T00:00:00Z')), '2026-W43');
  assert.equal(mondayOf(d('2026-10-14T12:00:00Z')), '2026-10-12');
  assert.equal(mondayOf(d('2026-10-18T23:59:59Z')), '2026-10-12');
  assert.equal(mondayOf(d('2026-10-19T00:00:00Z')), '2026-10-19');
});

test('1.5 week 53 and the year turn', () => {
  assert.equal(isoWeekOf(d('2026-12-28T00:00:00Z')), '2026-W53');
  assert.equal(isoWeekOf(d('2027-01-03T10:00:00Z')), '2026-W53');
  assert.equal(isoWeekOf(d('2027-01-04T00:00:00Z')), '2027-W01');
  assert.equal(mondayOfWeek('2026-W53'), '2026-12-28');
  assert.equal(mondayOfWeek('2027-W01'), '2027-01-04');
  assert.equal(mondayOfWeek('2026-W54'), null);
  assert.equal(mondayOfWeek('nope'), null);
  assert.equal(weekOfMonday('2026-12-28'), '2026-W53');
  assert.equal(weekOfMonday('2026-12-29'), null, 'a Tuesday is not a week key');
  assert.equal(weekLabel('2026-10-12'), 'Week of Oct 12');
  assert.equal(lastFinishedMonday(d('2026-10-14T09:00:00Z')), '2026-10-05');
  assert.equal(lastFinishedMonday(d('2026-10-19T00:00:00Z')), '2026-10-12');
});

test('1.6 the streak counts back from this week, or from last week while this one is empty', () => {
  const now = d('2026-10-19T08:00:00Z'); // Monday of 2026-W43
  assert.equal(streakOf(['2026-W40', '2026-W41', '2026-W42'], now), 3, 'nothing yet this Monday: from last week');
  assert.equal(streakOf(['2026-W40', '2026-W41', '2026-W42', '2026-W43'], now), 4);
  assert.equal(streakOf(['2026-W39', '2026-W41', '2026-W42'], now), 2, 'a skipped week breaks it');
  assert.equal(streakOf(['2026-W40'], now), 0, 'two weeks back is no streak');
  assert.equal(streakOf(['2026-W52', '2026-W53', '2027-W01'], d('2027-01-05T00:00:00Z')), 3, 'across week 53');
  assert.equal(streakOf([], now), 0);
  assert.equal(weeksShipping(['2025-W40', '2026-W01', '2026-W42', '2026-W42', 'junk'], now), 2, 'last 52 weeks, distinct, valid only');
  const grid = last52(now);
  assert.equal(grid.length, 52);
  assert.equal(grid.at(-1), '2026-W43');
  assert.equal(grid[0], '2025-W44');
});
```

```ts
after(async () => { if (process.env.DATABASE_URL) await sql().end(); });

test('1.7 the new event types are listed in events.ts and in every builder_events_type_check', () => {

test('1.9 the v2.13 columns and project_updates exist; one row per project and week; week is a Monday; 280 characters', dbTest, async () => {
  const cols = async (t: string) => (await sql()<{ c: string }[]>`SELECT column_name AS c FROM information_schema.columns WHERE table_name = ${t}`).map((r) => r.c);
  for (const c of ['main_project_key', 'updates_on_at', 'updates_scheduled_at']) assert.ok((await cols('builder_profiles')).includes(c), c);
  for (const c of ['chains', 'streak', 'weeks_shipping', 'ship_weeks']) assert.ok((await cols('builder_projects')).includes(c), c);
  for (const c of ['raising_at', 'coin_tx', 'coin_chain_id']) assert.ok((await cols('projects')).includes(c), c);
  for (const c of ['id', 'builder_id', 'project_key', 'week', 'text', 'facts', 'points', 'pinned', 'hidden', 'feed_hidden', 'created_at', 'edited_at'])
    assert.ok((await cols('project_updates')).includes(c), c);
  const key = `t1/${process.pid}-${Date.now()}`;
  await sql().begin(async (tx) => {
    await tx`INSERT INTO project_updates (builder_id, project_key, week, text) VALUES (1, ${key}, '2026-10-12', 'shipped')`;
    await assert.rejects(tx.savepoint((s) => s`INSERT INTO project_updates (builder_id, project_key, week) VALUES (1, ${key}, '2026-10-12')`), /duplicate|unique/i);
    throw new Error('rollback');
  }).catch((e: Error) => assert.equal(e.message, 'rollback'));
  await assert.rejects(sql()`INSERT INTO project_updates (builder_id, project_key, week) VALUES (1, ${key}, '2026-10-13')`, /check/i);
  await assert.rejects(sql()`INSERT INTO project_updates (builder_id, project_key, week, text) VALUES (1, ${key}, '2026-10-12', ${'x'.repeat(281)})`, /check/i);
  await sql().begin(async (tx) => {
    await tx`INSERT INTO coin_moments (builder_id, project_key, moment) VALUES (2100990000, ${key}, 'streak_five')`;
    throw new Error('rollback');
  }).catch((e: Error) => assert.equal(e.message, 'rollback'));
});
```

The duplicate-row case runs inside a postgres.js `savepoint`, so the outer transaction can still be rolled back as a whole.

`weeks.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * v2.13: ISO weeks (Monday to Sunday, UTC). A week's key is '2026-W42'; a row's `week` column is its
 * Monday ('2026-10-12'). The streak counts back from this week, or from last week while this one
 * has nothing yet (a Monday never breaks a streak). Pure and import-free (pages use it).
 */
const DAY = 86_400_000;
const WEEK = /^(\d{4})-W(\d{2})$/;
const MONDAY = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/;
const MONTHS = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'];

/** Same rule as source-readers.ts isoWeek (weeks.test.ts 1.3 keeps them equal). */
export function isoWeekOf(d: Date): string {
  const t = new Date(Date.UTC(d.getUTCFullYear(), d.getUTCMonth(), d.getUTCDate()));
  const day = t.getUTCDay() || 7;
  t.setUTCDate(t.getUTCDate() + 4 - day);
  const year = t.getUTCFullYear();
  const week = Math.ceil(((t.getTime() - Date.UTC(year, 0, 1)) / DAY + 1) / 7);
  return `${year}-W${String(week).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}
const mondayTime = (d: Date) => {
  const t = Date.UTC(d.getUTCFullYear(), d.getUTCMonth(), d.getUTCDate());
  return t - ((new Date(t).getUTCDay() + 6) % 7) * DAY;
};
const ymd = (t: number) => new Date(t).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
export const mondayOf = (d: Date): string => ymd(mondayTime(d));
export function mondayOfWeek(week: string): string | null {
  const m = WEEK.exec(week);
  if (!m) return null;
  const jan4 = Date.UTC(Number(m[1]), 0, 4);
  const t = mondayTime(new Date(jan4)) + (Number(m[2]) - 1) * 7 * DAY;
  return isoWeekOf(new Date(t)) === week ? ymd(t) : null;
}
export function weekOfMonday(monday: string): string | null {
  if (!MONDAY.test(monday)) return null;
  const t = Date.parse(`${monday}T00:00:00Z`);
  if (!Number.isFinite(t) || new Date(t).getUTCDay() !== 1) return null;
  return isoWeekOf(new Date(t));
}
export const lastFinishedMonday = (now: Date): string => ymd(mondayTime(now) - 7 * DAY);
export const weekLabel = (monday: string): string => {
  const t = new Date(`${monday}T00:00:00Z`);
  return `Week of ${MONTHS[t.getUTCMonth()]} ${t.getUTCDate()}`;
};
/** The 52 week keys ending with this week, oldest first. */
export function last52(now: Date): string[] {
  const end = mondayTime(now);
  return Array.from({ length: 52 }, (_, i) => isoWeekOf(new Date(end - (51 - i) * 7 * DAY)));
}
export function streakOf(weeks: Iterable<string>, now: Date): number {
  const have = new Set([...weeks].filter((w) => WEEK.test(w)));
  let t = mondayTime(now);
  if (!have.has(isoWeekOf(new Date(t)))) t -= 7 * DAY;
  let n = 0;
  while (have.has(isoWeekOf(new Date(t)))) {
    n++;
    t -= 7 * DAY;
  }
  return n;
}
export function weeksShipping(weeks: Iterable<string>, now: Date): number {
  const window = new Set(last52(now));
  return new Set([...weeks].filter((w) => window.has(w))).size;
}
```

Append at the end of `schema.sql`:

```sql
-- v2.13 (the Bench). Additive. Nothing writes these while EARNKIT_BENCH is off.
-- The one project (spec R1): its builder_projects key, set once (plan B2). The weekly-update yes (R5)
-- and whether the agent scheduled the weekly task (plan B10).
ALTER TABLE builder_profiles ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS main_project_key text;
ALTER TABLE builder_profiles ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS updates_on_at timestamptz;
ALTER TABLE builder_profiles ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS updates_scheduled_at timestamptz;
-- The ship log: one row per project and ISO week (week = its Monday). text NULL: a week with facts
-- and no update (plan B8). hidden: the builder's hide; feed_hidden: the owner's feed:hide.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS project_updates (
  id          bigserial PRIMARY KEY,
  builder_id  integer NOT NULL,
  project_key text NOT NULL,
  week        date NOT NULL CHECK (extract(isodow FROM week) = 1),
  text        text CHECK (text IS NULL OR char_length(text) <= 280),
  facts       jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  points      integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  pinned      boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  hidden      boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  feed_hidden boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  edited_at   timestamptz,
  UNIQUE (project_key, week)
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS project_updates_builder ON project_updates (builder_id, week DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS project_updates_feed ON project_updates (week, points DESC) WHERE NOT hidden AND NOT feed_hidden;
-- Computed by the rescore (T4): chains the project is on (base, robinhood, arc), the streak, weeks
-- shipping in the last 52, and those weeks themselves for the profile's grid (plan B7).
ALTER TABLE builder_projects ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS chains text[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}';
ALTER TABLE builder_projects ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS streak integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;
ALTER TABLE builder_projects ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS weeks_shipping integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;
ALTER TABLE builder_projects ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS ship_weeks text[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}';
-- Raising (R8): set on the yes, cleared on "stop raising"; the coin's launch tx and chain (tokens.fun's txHash, chainId).
ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS raising_at timestamptz;
ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS coin_tx text;
ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS coin_chain_id integer;
-- The fifth week in a row is a coin moment too (spec "Optional: raise"). Re-added once only.
ALTER TABLE coin_moments DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS coin_moments_moment_check;
ALTER TABLE coin_moments ADD CONSTRAINT coin_moments_moment_check
  CHECK (moment IN ('built_live','countable','first_five','has_people','streak_five'));
```

### Task 2: One project at `find`, and its proof in the same turn

Spec "2a. They have a project" and R1, R2: the agent sends the project it is in; EarnKit stores it as the person's one project, proposes its sources, and hands back the proof line to commit and push now.

**Files:**
- Modify: `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/identity-input.ts` (extract `projectOf`, add `benchProject`, export `projectKeyOf`)
- Create: `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/main-project.ts`
- Modify: `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/discovery.ts` (`proofLineCheck` gains `minMs`)
- Modify: `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/person-score.ts` (`refreshDiscovery`: the fast proof check while confirming)
- Modify: `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/stand-view.ts` (`startRefresh` passes `minAgeMs`)
- Modify: `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/mcp.ts` (`FIND_DESCRIPTION_BENCH`, the `identity` field's words under the flag)
- Modify: `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/tools.ts` (`listProgramsInput` returns `project`; `list_programs` under the flag records the one project and attaches `proof`)
- Test: `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/main-project.test.ts`, `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/mcp.test.ts` (new cases 2.7, 2.8)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `benchEnabled` (T1); `identityInput`, `noteIdentity` (identity-input.ts); `tokenLineFor` (token-gate.ts); `noteLineIssued` (github-verify.ts); `createVerifyLink`, `githubVerifyEnabled` (github-verify.ts); `refreshPerson`, `REFRESH_MS` (person-score.ts); `discoverPerson`, `proofLineCheck` (discovery.ts).
- Produces:
  - identity-input.ts: `projectOf(raw: unknown): IdentityProject | null`; `benchProject(identity: unknown): IdentityProject | null`; `projectKeyOf(p: IdentityProject): string`
  - main-project.ts:
    - `CONFIRMING_REFRESH_MS = 60_000`, `MAIN_PROOF_MIN_MS = 4_000`
    - `PROOF_NOTE: string`, `SIGN_IN_PROOF_NOTE: string`
    - `mainProjectOf(builderId: number, db?: Db): Promise<string | null>`
    - `setMainProject(builderId: number, p: IdentityProject): Promise<{ key: string; set: boolean }>` (set: this call wrote it)
    - `mainConfirmed(builderId: number): Promise<boolean>`
    - `confirming(builderId: number): Promise<boolean>` (main set, not confirmed, a proof line issued for it to one of the group's installations)
    - `fillGithubUser(installationId: number, repoKey: string, reported: string | null): Promise<void>`
    - `proofFor(installationId: number, builderId: number, o: { canPush: boolean; now: Date }): Promise<{ file: 'earnkit.txt'; line: string; agent_note: string } | { sign_in: string; agent_note: string } | null>`
    - `pickMainProject(builderId: number, db: Db): Promise<string | null>` (plan B2, used by T4's rescore)
  - discovery.ts: `proofLineCheck(d, deadline, o: { skipShort?: boolean; onSkip?: () => void; minMs?: number })`
  - stand-view.ts: `startRefresh(id, o: { now: Date; start: number; reader?: ReaderDeps; minAgeMs?: number })`
  - mcp.ts: `FIND_DESCRIPTION_BENCH: string`
  - tools.ts `find` reply under the flag gains `proof?: { file: 'earnkit.txt'; line: string; agent_note: string } | { sign_in: string; agent_note: string }`

`main-project.test.ts`:

```ts
import { after, test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import { sql } from './db';
import { benchProject, noteIdentity, identityInput, projectKeyOf } from './identity-input';
import { MAIN_PROOF_MIN_MS, PROOF_NOTE, confirming, fillGithubUser, mainProjectOf, pickMainProject, proofFor, setMainProject } from './main-project';
import { proofLineCheck } from './discovery';
import type { ReaderDeps } from './source-readers';

process.env.EARNKIT_BUILDER_SCORE = 'on';
process.env.EARNKIT_BENCH = 'on';
process.env.EARNKIT_AUTH_SECRET ||= 'v213-main-secret';
const dbTest = { skip: !process.env.DATABASE_URL && 'no DATABASE_URL' };
after(async () => { if (process.env.DATABASE_URL) await sql().end(); });
const RUN = randomBytes(3).toString('hex');

async function person(): Promise<number> {
  const [{ id }] = await sql()<{ id: number }[]>`INSERT INTO installations (token_hash) VALUES (${`v213-main-${randomBytes(8).toString('hex')}`}) RETURNING id`;
  await sql()`INSERT INTO builder_profiles (builder_id, slug) VALUES (${id}, ${`v213m-${id}`})`;
  return id;
}

test('2.1 the one project: identity.project wins, else the first of an old agent\'s projects[]; the rest are ignored', () => {
  const one = benchProject({ project: { name: 'Recipe planner', what: 'a recipe planner', repo: 'https://github.com/Maya/recipes.git', live_url: 'https://recipes.app/x', chain: 'base' }, projects: [{ name: 'Other', repo: 'maya/other' }] });
  assert.deepEqual(one, { name: 'Recipe planner', what: 'a recipe planner', repo: 'maya/recipes', live_url: 'https://recipes.app', package: null, contracts: [] });
  assert.equal(projectKeyOf(one!), 'maya/recipes');
  const old = benchProject({ projects: [{ name: 'First', repo: 'maya/first' }, { name: 'Second', repo: 'maya/second' }] });
  assert.equal(old?.repo, 'maya/first');
  assert.equal(benchProject({ project: [{ name: 'a list', repo: 'maya/a' }] }), null, 'a list is not the project');
  assert.equal(benchProject({ project: { name: 'No links' } }), null, 'nothing to read');
  assert.equal(benchProject('junk'), null);
  assert.equal(benchProject(undefined), null);
});

test('2.2 the project is set once: a later, different project never replaces it (R1)', dbTest, async () => {
  const id = await person();
  const a = await setMainProject(id, { name: 'A', what: null, repo: `maya${RUN}/a`, live_url: null, package: null, contracts: [] });
  assert.deepEqual(a, { key: `maya${RUN}/a`, set: true });
  const b = await setMainProject(id, { name: 'B', what: null, repo: `maya${RUN}/b`, live_url: null, package: null, contracts: [] });
  assert.deepEqual(b, { key: `maya${RUN}/a`, set: false });
  assert.equal(await mainProjectOf(id), `maya${RUN}/a`);
});

test('2.3 under the flag noteIdentity proposes the one project\'s sources only, never verified', dbTest, async () => {
  const id = await person();
  const i = identityInput({ github_login: `maya${RUN}`, projects: [{ name: 'A', repo: `maya${RUN}/a`, live_url: 'https://a.example.com' }, { name: 'B', repo: `maya${RUN}/b` }] });
  const one = benchProject({ projects: i.projects })!;
  await noteIdentity(id, { ...i, projects: [one] }, new Date());
  const rows = await sql()<{ ref: string; state: string }[]>`SELECT ref, state FROM builder_sources WHERE builder_id = ${id} ORDER BY ref`;
  assert.deepEqual(rows.map((r) => r.ref), ['https://a.example.com', `maya${RUN}/a`]);
  assert.ok(rows.every((r) => r.state === 'proposed'), 'an agent report never confirms');
});

test('2.4 the proof line: issued for an unconfirmed repo, recorded, github_user filled with the owner; none once confirmed', dbTest, async () => {
  const id = await person();
  const key = `maya${RUN}/proof`;
  await setMainProject(id, { name: 'P', what: null, repo: key, live_url: null, package: null, contracts: [] });
  await fillGithubUser(id, key, null);
  assert.equal((await sql()`SELECT github_user FROM installations WHERE id = ${id}`)[0].github_user, `maya${RUN}`);
  await fillGithubUser(id, key, 'someone-else');
  assert.equal((await sql()`SELECT github_user FROM installations WHERE id = ${id}`)[0].github_user, `maya${RUN}`, 'never overwritten');
  const p = await proofFor(id, id, { canPush: true, now: new Date() });
  assert.ok(p && 'line' in p);
  assert.match(p.line, /^token: [0-9a-f]{10,}$/);
  assert.equal(p.file, 'earnkit.txt');
  assert.equal(p.agent_note, PROOF_NOTE);
  assert.equal(await confirming(id), true);
  const [issued] = await sql()`SELECT 1 FROM proof_line_issues WHERE installation_id = ${id} AND repo_key = ${key}`;
  assert.ok(issued, 'commits after this moment count (v2.11 R4 b)');
  await sql()`INSERT INTO builder_projects (builder_id, project_key, name, kind, confirmed, shipped) VALUES (${id}, ${key}, 'P', 'code', true, false)`;
  assert.equal(await proofFor(id, id, { canPush: true, now: new Date() }), null);
  assert.equal(await confirming(id), false);
});

test('2.5 a repo the agent does not own stays Confirming: proposed, unranked, and the cron proof check names no one (review focus 3)', dbTest, async () => {
  const id = await person();
  const key = `notmaya${RUN}/stolen`;
  await setMainProject(id, { name: 'S', what: null, repo: key, live_url: null, package: null, contracts: [] });
  await noteIdentity(id, { github_login: `maya${RUN}`, projects: [{ name: 'S', what: null, repo: key, live_url: null, package: null, contracts: [] }], wallets: [] }, new Date());
  await fillGithubUser(id, key, `maya${RUN}`);
  // github_login reported as maya; the repo's owner is notmaya: github_user is not set to either (B3).
  assert.equal((await sql()`SELECT github_user FROM installations WHERE id = ${id}`)[0].github_user, null);
  // A reader whose activity log says someone else pushed the line: no login, nothing verified.
  const reader = { now: new Date(), ghFetch: async () => new Response('[]', { status: 200 }), ghSpent: () => false } as unknown as ReaderDeps;
  const check = proofLineCheck(reader, Date.now() + 10_000, { minMs: MAIN_PROOF_MIN_MS });
  assert.equal(await check(id, key), null);
  const [src] = await sql()<{ state: string }[]>`SELECT state FROM builder_sources WHERE builder_id = ${id} AND ref = ${key}`;
  assert.equal(src.state, 'proposed');
});

test('2.6 the lazy pick for a person with projects and no main one: confirmed, most people, then newest (B2)', dbTest, async () => {
  const id = await person();
  const k = (s: string) => `p${RUN}/${s}`;
  for (const [key, confirmed, people, days] of [[k('old'), true, 3, 9], [k('busy'), true, 40, 30], [k('mine'), false, 99, 1]] as const)
    await sql()`INSERT INTO builder_projects (builder_id, project_key, name, kind, confirmed, shipped, people, last_active_at)
      VALUES (${id}, ${key}, ${key}, 'app', ${confirmed}, true, ${people}, now() - ${`${days} days`}::interval)`;
  assert.equal(await pickMainProject(id, sql()), k('busy'));
  assert.equal(await mainProjectOf(id), k('busy'), 'stored once');
  await sql()`UPDATE builder_projects SET people = 500 WHERE builder_id = ${id} AND project_key = ${k('old')}`;
  assert.equal(await pickMainProject(id, sql()), k('busy'), 'never moves on its own');
});
```

```ts
/** The project key a reported project is stored under (projectSources' key). Pure. */
export const projectKeyOf = (p: IdentityProject): string => p.repo ?? p.live_url ?? p.package ?? p.contracts[0];

/**
 * v2.13 spec "2a": the one project the agent is in: `identity.project` (an object), else the first

 */
export function benchProject(identity: unknown): IdentityProject | null {
  const i = o(identity);
  if (i.project !== undefined) return Array.isArray(i.project) ? null : projectOf(i.project);

}
```

`main-project.ts`:

```ts
import { sql, type Db } from './db';
import { createVerifyLink, githubVerifyEnabled, noteLineIssued } from './github-verify';
import { projectKeyOf, type IdentityProject } from './identity-input';
import { tokenLineFor } from './token-gate';

/**
 * v2.13 spec R1, "2a": the person's one project (builder_profiles.main_project_key), set once (plan
 * B2), and the proof that it is theirs: the v2.9 earnkit.txt line, committed and pushed by their
 * agent in the same turn (plan B3), or GitHub sign-in when the agent can't push.
 */
export const CONFIRMING_REFRESH_MS = 60_000;
export const MAIN_PROOF_MIN_MS = 4_000;
export const PROOF_NOTE =
  "Add proof.line as its own line in earnkit.txt at the repo root, commit it to the default branch and push now, from the builder's own GitHub account. Do it in this turn; never ask the builder to edit files. Don't show this note.";
export const SIGN_IN_PROOF_NOTE = "You can't push, so the reply carries a link that shows the project is theirs. Show show_to_builder as written. Don't show this note.";
const REPO_KEY = /^[a-z0-9](?:[a-z0-9]|-(?=[a-z0-9])){0,38}\/(?!\.\.?$)[a-z0-9._-]{1,100}$/;

export async function mainProjectOf(builderId: number, db: Db = sql()): Promise<string | null> {
  const [p] = await db<{ key: string | null }[]>`SELECT main_project_key AS key FROM builder_profiles WHERE builder_id = ${builderId}`;
  return p?.key ?? null;
}

/** Set once (plan B2): a different project later is stored in identity only. `set`: this call wrote it. */
export async function setMainProject(builderId: number, p: IdentityProject): Promise<{ key: string; set: boolean }> {
  const key = projectKeyOf(p);
  const [r] = await sql()<{ key: string; was: string | null }[]>`
    WITH old AS (SELECT main_project_key AS was FROM builder_profiles WHERE builder_id = ${builderId} FOR UPDATE)
    UPDATE builder_profiles SET main_project_key = COALESCE(main_project_key, ${key}), updated_at = now()
    FROM old WHERE builder_id = ${builderId}
    RETURNING main_project_key AS key, old.was`;
  return r ? { key: r.key, set: r.was === null } : { key, set: false };
}
```

```ts
export async function mainConfirmed(builderId: number): Promise<boolean> {
  const [r] = await sql()`
    SELECT 1 FROM builder_profiles p JOIN builder_projects b ON b.builder_id = p.builder_id AND lower(b.project_key) = lower(p.main_project_key)
    WHERE p.builder_id = ${builderId} AND b.confirmed`;
  return Boolean(r);
}

export async function confirming(builderId: number): Promise<boolean> {
  const [r] = await sql()`
    SELECT 1 FROM builder_profiles p
    WHERE p.builder_id = ${builderId} AND p.main_project_key IS NOT NULL
      AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM builder_projects b WHERE b.builder_id = p.builder_id AND lower(b.project_key) = lower(p.main_project_key) AND b.confirmed)
      AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM proof_line_issues l JOIN installations i ON i.id = l.installation_id
        WHERE COALESCE(i.builder_id, i.id) = ${builderId} AND l.repo_key = lower(p.main_project_key))`;
  return Boolean(r);
}

/** Plan B3: github_user is the repo's owner when empty and the reported login (if any) is that owner. Never overwritten. */
export async function fillGithubUser(installationId: number, repoKey: string, reported: string | null): Promise<void> {
  const owner = repoKey.toLowerCase().split('/')[0];
  if (!REPO_KEY.test(repoKey.toLowerCase()) || (reported && reported.toLowerCase() !== owner)) return;
  await sql()`UPDATE installations SET github_user = ${owner} WHERE id = ${installationId} AND github_user IS NULL`;
}

export async function proofFor(installationId: number, builderId: number, o: { canPush: boolean; now: Date }) {
  const key = (await mainProjectOf(builderId))?.toLowerCase() ?? null;
  if (!key || !REPO_KEY.test(key) || (await mainConfirmed(builderId))) return null;
  const url = `https://github.com/${key}`;
  if (!o.canPush) {
    if (!githubVerifyEnabled()) return null;
    const link = await createVerifyLink({ installationId, projectId: null, repoKey: key, purpose: 'claim' }).catch(() => null);
    return link ? { sign_in: link, agent_note: SIGN_IN_PROOF_NOTE } : null;
  }
  const line = tokenLineFor(installationId, url);
  if (!line) return null;
  await noteLineIssued(installationId, url, o.now);
  return { file: 'earnkit.txt' as const, line, agent_note: PROOF_NOTE };
}

/** Plan B2: a person with projects and no main one gets one, once: confirmed first, then most people, then newest. */
export async function pickMainProject(builderId: number, db: Db): Promise<string | null> {
  const [r] = await db<{ key: string | null }[]>`
    UPDATE builder_profiles p SET main_project_key = COALESCE(p.main_project_key, (
      SELECT b.project_key FROM builder_projects b WHERE b.builder_id = p.builder_id
      ORDER BY b.confirmed DESC, b.people DESC, b.last_active_at DESC NULLS LAST, b.project_key LIMIT 1))
    WHERE p.builder_id = ${builderId} RETURNING p.main_project_key AS key`;
  return r?.key ?? null;
}
```

Spec R2, "Reads" and "Cost": under the flag the person GraphQL query (`readPersonGithub`) and the v2.12.2 reported-login listing (`readOwnedRepos`) are never called; repos are read by name, 25 a call across builders; each repo reading keeps `commit_counts` per ISO week from the dates already read.

- Modify: `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/source-readers.ts` (`RepoSignals.commit_counts?`)

```ts
import { after, test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { sql } from './db';
import { PERSON_GITHUB_QUERY, OWNED_REPOS_QUERY } from './person-github';
import { readPersonLive } from './person-score';
import { readSources, withPastCounts } from './sources';
import { discover } from './discovery';
import type { ReaderDeps } from './source-readers';

process.env.EARNKIT_BUILDER_SCORE = 'on';
process.env.EARNKIT_BENCH = 'on';
const dbTest = { skip: !process.env.DATABASE_URL && 'no DATABASE_URL' };
after(async () => { if (process.env.DATABASE_URL) await sql().end(); });
const NOW = new Date();
const T = 2_131_300_000 + (process.pid % 1000) * 20;

const node = (key: string) => ({
  nameWithOwner: key, description: null, homepageUrl: null, isFork: false, isTemplate: false, isPrivate: false,
  createdAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z', pushedAt: NOW.toISOString(), stargazerCount: 0, forkCount: 0, parent: null, templateRepository: null,
  owner: { __typename: 'User', login: key.split('/')[0] },
  stargazers: { edges: [] }, forks: { nodes: [] },
  defaultBranchRef: { target: { history: { nodes: [{ committedDate: NOW.toISOString() }] } } },
});
/** A reader that answers by-name repo queries and records every query; any other read throws. */
function reader(queries: string[]): ReaderDeps {
  const no = async () => { throw new Error('unexpected read'); };
  return {
    gh: no, probe: no, fetchText: no, json: no, baseTxs: no, tokenTransfers: no, contractOrigin: no, arcLogs: no, arcHead: no, arcSender: no, usdPerNative: no, walletCreations: no, now: NOW,
    ghGraphql: async <R>(q: string, v: Record<string, unknown>) => {
      queries.push(q);
      const data: Record<string, unknown> = {};
      for (let i = 0; `o${i}` in v; i++) data[`r${i}`] = node(`${v[`o${i}`]}/${v[`n${i}`]}`);
      return { data } as R;
    },
  } as ReaderDeps;
}
async function personWithRepos(id: number, login: string, n: number) {
  await sql()`DELETE FROM source_live WHERE source_id IN (SELECT id FROM builder_sources WHERE builder_id = ${id})`;
  await sql()`DELETE FROM source_readings WHERE source_id IN (SELECT id FROM builder_sources WHERE builder_id = ${id})`;
  for (const t of ['builder_profiles', 'builder_sources', 'builder_projects']) await sql()`DELETE FROM ${sql()(t)} WHERE builder_id = ${id}`;
  await sql()`DELETE FROM installations WHERE id = ${id}`;
  await sql()`INSERT INTO installations (id, token_hash) VALUES (${id}, ${`v213-reads-${id}`})`;
  await sql()`INSERT INTO builder_profiles (builder_id, slug, github_login) VALUES (${id}, ${`v213r-${id}`}, ${login})`;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++)
    await sql()`INSERT INTO builder_sources (builder_id, kind, ref, project_key, state, proof, proposed_by)
      VALUES (${id}, 'repo', ${`${login}/r${i}`}, ${`${login}/r${i}`}, 'verified', 'repo_owner', 'server')`;
}

test('3.1 withPastCounts keeps the larger count per week, drops weeks past 52 and junk', () => {
  const now = new Date('2026-10-14T00:00:00Z');
  assert.deepEqual(
    withPastCounts({ '2026-W42': 3, '2026-W41': 1 }, { commit_counts: { '2026-W41': 4, '2026-W30': 2, '2025-W30': 9, junk: 1, '2026-W29': -1 } }, now),
    { '2026-W30': 2, '2026-W41': 4, '2026-W42': 3 },
  );
  assert.deepEqual(withPastCounts({ '2026-W42': 1 }, null, now), { '2026-W42': 1 });
});

test('3.2 the refresh reads a person\'s repos by name only, never the person query, and keeps commit_counts', dbTest, async () => {
  const id = T + 1;
  await personWithRepos(id, `lee${T}`, 3);
  const queries: string[] = [];
  const r = await readPersonLive(id, NOW, { deadline: Date.now() + 10_000, reader: reader(queries) });
  assert.equal(r.read, 3);
  assert.equal(queries.length, 1, 'one by-name call for 3 repos');
  assert.ok(!queries.some((q) => q === PERSON_GITHUB_QUERY || q === OWNED_REPOS_QUERY));
  const [live] = await sql()<{ signals: { commit_counts?: Record<string, number> } }[]>`
    SELECT l.signals FROM source_live l JOIN builder_sources s ON s.id = l.source_id WHERE s.builder_id = ${id} LIMIT 1`;
  assert.equal(Object.values(live.signals.commit_counts ?? {}).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0), 1);
});

test('3.3 the daily read batches 25 repos a call across builders (30 repos of 2 people: 2 calls)', dbTest, async () => {
  await personWithRepos(T + 2, `ann${T}`, 20);
  await personWithRepos(T + 3, `bob${T}`, 10);
  const queries: string[] = [];
  const r = await readSources(NOW, { deadline: Date.now() + 20_000, reader: reader(queries), only: [T + 2, T + 3] });
  assert.equal(r.read, 30);
  assert.equal(queries.length, 2);
  assert.ok(!queries.includes(PERSON_GITHUB_QUERY));
});

test('3.4 discovery under the flag never lists every repo or org of a proven login', dbTest, async () => {
  const id = T + 4;
  const login = `cat${T}`;
  await personWithRepos(id, login, 0);
  await sql()`INSERT INTO builder_sources (builder_id, kind, ref, project_key, state, proposed_by) VALUES (${id}, 'repo', ${`${login}/main`}, ${`${login}/main`}, 'proposed', 'agent')`;
  await sql()`UPDATE builder_profiles SET main_project_key = ${`${login}/main`} WHERE builder_id = ${id}`;
  await sql()`INSERT INTO discovery_queue (builder_id, reason) VALUES (${id}, 'first_find') ON CONFLICT (builder_id) DO UPDATE SET done_at = NULL`;
  const paths: string[] = [];
  const d = reader([]);
  d.gh = async <R>(path: string) => {
    paths.push(path);
    if (path === `/users/${login}`) return { blog: null, twitter_username: null } as R;
    if (path === `/repos/${login}/main`) return { full_name: `${login}/main`, owner: { login, type: 'User' }, fork: false, is_template: false } as R;
    return (path.includes('/git/trees/') ? {} : []) as R;
  };
  await discover(NOW, { deadline: Date.now() + 20_000, reader: d, login: async () => ({ login, basis: 'signin' }), only: [id] });
  assert.ok(!paths.some((p) => p.startsWith(`/users/${login}/repos`) || p.endsWith('/orgs')), paths.join(', '));
  const [src] = await sql()<{ state: string; proof: string | null }[]>`SELECT state, proof FROM builder_sources WHERE builder_id = ${id} AND ref = ${`${login}/main`}`;
  assert.deepEqual(src, { state: 'verified', proof: 'repo_owner' });
});

test('3.5 flag off: the refresh still reads a person\'s own repos through the person query (parity)', dbTest, async () => {
  const id = T + 5;
  await personWithRepos(id, `dan${T}`, 2);
  const queries: string[] = [];
  const d = reader(queries);
  const inner = d.ghGraphql;
  d.ghGraphql = async <R>(q: string, v: Record<string, unknown>) => {
    if (q === PERSON_GITHUB_QUERY) {
      queries.push(q);
      return { data: { user: { login: `dan${T}`, twitterUsername: null, websiteUrl: null, repositories: { nodes: [node(`dan${T}/r0`), node(`dan${T}/r1`)] } } } } as R;
    }
    return inner<R>(q, v);
  };
  delete process.env.EARNKIT_BENCH;
  try {
    await readPersonLive(id, NOW, { deadline: Date.now() + 10_000, reader: d });
    assert.deepEqual(queries, [PERSON_GITHUB_QUERY]);
  } finally {
    process.env.EARNKIT_BENCH = 'on';
  }
});
```

Append to `person-github.ts`:

```ts
/** v2.13 (R2): the person's GitHub profile only, for the X field match; no repos (about 0.2 s). */
export const GITHUB_USER_QUERY = `query($login: String!) { user(login: $login) { login twitterUsername websiteUrl } }`;
export async function readGithubUser(login: string, d: ReaderDeps): Promise<PersonGithub | null> {
  if (!LOGIN.test(login)) throw new Error('not a GitHub login');
  return personGithubFrom(await d.ghGraphql(GITHUB_USER_QUERY, { login }), d.now);
}
```

(`personGithubFrom` already returns an empty `repos` map when `repositories` is absent.)

`sources.ts`:

```ts
/** v2.13: a reading's commit counts merged with the previous reading's: the larger per week, last 52 weeks only. Pure. */
export function withPastCounts(counts: Record<string, number>, prev: unknown, now: Date): Record<string, number> {
  const old = (prev as { commit_counts?: unknown } | null)?.commit_counts;
  const floor = isoWeek(new Date(now.getTime() - 52 * 7 * DAY_MS));
  const out: Record<string, number> = {};
  const add = (w: string, n: unknown) => {
    if (!WEEK.test(w) || w < floor || typeof n !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(n) || n <= 0) return;
    out[w] = Math.max(out[w] ?? 0, Math.floor(n));
  };
  for (const [w, n] of Object.entries(counts)) add(w, n);
  if (old && typeof old === 'object' && !Array.isArray(old)) for (const [w, n] of Object.entries(old)) add(w, n);
  return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(out).sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b)));
}
```

In `readAll`, every place that writes a repo reading (`ok(s, { ...r, contributors, commit_weeks: withPastWeeks(...) })`) also writes `commit_counts: withPastCounts(r.commit_counts ?? {}, prev, now)` (read `prev` once: `const prev = await previousSignals(s.id)`). Then, under the flag, replace the per-person loop with one pass across builders:

```ts
  if (benchEnabled()) {
    // v2.13 (R2, "Cost"): no person query; every due repo by name, REPOS_MAX a call across builders.
    const repos = due.filter((s) => s.kind === 'repo');
    for (const s of repos) if (!REPO_REF.test(s.ref)) await fail(s, 'not_a_repo');
    const named = repos.filter((s) => REPO_REF.test(s.ref));
    const found = new Map<string, PersonRepo>();
    for (let i = 0; i < named.length; i += REPOS_MAX) {
      if (Date.now() >= o.deadline) return { read, failed };
      const batch = named.slice(i, i + REPOS_MAX);
      const refs = [...new Set(batch.map((s) => s.ref.toLowerCase()))];
      const go = await attempt(batch, async () => {
        for (const [k, r] of await readGithubRepos(refs, o.reader)) found.set(k, r);
      });
      if (!go) return { read, failed };
    }
    for (const s of named) await writeRepo(s, found.get(s.ref.toLowerCase()));
  } else {
    // ... today's per-person loop, unchanged, with its write moved into writeRepo
  }
```

where `writeRepo(s, r)` is the existing tail (not found: `fail(s, 'not_found')` unless stalled or settled; an organization's contributors REST call; `ok(...)` with `commit_weeks` and `commit_counts`), extracted unchanged so both branches share it. A batch of 25 may hold the same ref twice (two people proposed it): `refs` is de-duplicated and every source with that ref gets the same reading.

`person-score.ts readPersonLive`: wrap the `mine` block in `if (!benchEnabled()) { ... }` (so under the flag `mine` is empty and every repo goes through the by-name loop), and in the `put(s, { ok: true, signals: { ...r, contributors, commit_weeks: ... } })` add `commit_counts: withPastCounts(r.commit_counts ?? {}, prev, now)`.

`discovery.ts`:
- `provenLogin`: replace `readPersonGithub(p.reported, o.reader)` with `(benchEnabled() ? readGithubUser : readPersonGithub)(p.reported, o.reader)`.
- `discoverOne`, Clue 2: under the flag skip the `/users/${L}/repos` listing and the org loop: wrap them in `if (!benchEnabled()) { ... }`; the agent remotes loop (which reads `/repos/<key>` for each proposed agent repo) stays, so the main project (an agent remote) is still checked against the proven login.
- `discoverOne`, the v2.12.2 reported-login block: change `if (!proof) {` to `if (!proof && !benchEnabled()) {`.

`week-facts.test.ts`:

```ts
import { after, test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { sql } from './db';
import { EVENT_LOG_FLAG } from './events';
import { factChips, hasFacts, startedChips, startedLine, weekFactsFor, weeklyFactsStep } from './week-facts';
import { rescorePerson } from './person-score';
import { isoWeekOf, mondayOf } from './weeks';
import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import { ProgramFile } from './program-file';
import { upsertProgram } from './programs';
import { committedProgram } from './fixtures/program-file';

process.env.EARNKIT_BUILDER_SCORE = 'on';
process.env.EARNKIT_BENCH = 'on';
process.env[EVENT_LOG_FLAG] = 'on';
const dbTest = { skip: !process.env.DATABASE_URL && 'no DATABASE_URL' };
after(async () => { if (process.env.DATABASE_URL) await sql().end(); });
const DAY = 86_400_000;
const T = 2_131_400_000 + (process.pid % 1000) * 20;
const NOW = new Date();
const wk = (daysAgo: number) => isoWeekOf(new Date(NOW.getTime() - daysAgo * DAY));
/** A copy of a committed program under a fresh id (the shared DB may not have run programs:sync). */
async function program(name: string): Promise<{ id: string; title: string }> {
  const p = { ...committedProgram(name), id: `v213-${name}-${randomBytes(3).toString('hex')}`, status: 'active' };
  await upsertProgram(ProgramFile.parse(p));
  return { id: p.id, title: p.title };
}

async function clear(id: number) {
  await sql()`DELETE FROM source_live WHERE source_id IN (SELECT id FROM builder_sources WHERE builder_id = ${id})`;
  await sql()`DELETE FROM source_readings WHERE source_id IN (SELECT id FROM builder_sources WHERE builder_id = ${id})`;
  for (const t of ['builder_profiles', 'builder_sources', 'builder_live', 'builder_projects', 'project_updates']) await sql()`DELETE FROM ${sql()(t)} WHERE builder_id = ${id}`;
  await sql()`DELETE FROM projects WHERE installation_id = ${id}`;
  await sql()`DELETE FROM contract_deploys WHERE installation_id = ${id}`;
  await sql()`DELETE FROM installations WHERE id = ${id}`;
}
/** A person whose main project `key` has a verified repo with these commit counts, read today. */
async function person(id: number, key: string, counts: Record<string, number>, o: { confirmed?: boolean; other?: string } = {}) {
  await clear(id);
  await sql()`INSERT INTO installations (id, token_hash) VALUES (${id}, ${`v213-wf-${id}`})`;
  await sql()`INSERT INTO builder_profiles (builder_id, slug, main_project_key, ring) VALUES (${id}, ${`v213wf-${id}`}, ${key}, ${sql().json({ ring: [], ringOwner: [] })})`;
  const state = o.confirmed === false ? 'proposed' : 'verified';
  const [{ sid }] = await sql()<{ sid: number }[]>`
    INSERT INTO builder_sources (builder_id, kind, ref, project_key, state, proof, proposed_by)
    VALUES (${id}, 'repo', ${key}, ${key}, ${state}, ${state === 'verified' ? 'proof_line' : null}, 'agent') RETURNING id::int AS sid`;
  await sql()`INSERT INTO source_live (source_id, signals, ok, read_at) VALUES (${sid}, ${sql().json({
    owner: key.split('/')[0], owner_type: 'User', created_at: null, fork: false, template: false, stargazers: [], forkers: [], issue_authors: [], contributors: [],
    commit_weeks: Object.keys(counts), commit_counts: counts, partial: false, private: false,
  } as never)}, true, now())`;
  if (o.other)
    await sql()`INSERT INTO builder_sources (builder_id, kind, ref, project_key, state, proof, proposed_by)
      VALUES (${id}, 'site', ${o.other}, ${o.other}, 'verified', 'earnkit_hosted', 'server')`;
}

test('4.1 the chips, in the order the card shows them, in plain words', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(
    factChips({ shipped_code: { n: 12 }, went_live: true, users_up: { n: 31 }, earned: { usd: 120 }, won: [{ title: 'Base Builder Grants' }], streak: { w: 6 } }, { raising: 'RECIPE' }),
    ['Went live', 'Won Base Builder Grants', '+31 people', '$120 earned', '12 commits', '6 weeks in a row', 'Raising $RECIPE'],
  );
  assert.deepEqual(factChips({ shipped_code: { n: 1 }, users_up: { n: 1 } }), ['+1 person', '1 commit']);
  assert.deepEqual(factChips({ streak: { w: 1 } }), [], 'a streak of one is not a chip');
  assert.equal(startedLine('Recipe planner'), 'Started tracking Recipe planner.');
  assert.deepEqual(startedChips({ live: true, weeks: 6, people: 31 }), ['Live', '6 weeks shipping', '31 people use it']);
  assert.deepEqual(startedChips({ live: false, weeks: 1, people: 1 }), ['1 week shipping', '1 person uses it']);
  assert.deepEqual(startedChips({ live: false, weeks: 0, people: 0 }), []);
});

test('4.2 a fact is shipped code, going live, new people, money or a win; a streak or the start alone is not', () => {
  assert.equal(hasFacts({ shipped_code: { n: 1 } }), true);
  assert.equal(hasFacts({ won: [] }), false);
  assert.equal(hasFacts({ streak: { w: 4 } }), false);
  assert.equal(hasFacts({ started: { live: true, weeks: 3, people: 2 } }), false);
  assert.equal(hasFacts({ checked: true }), false);
});

test('4.3 the rescore scores the one project, stores its streak, weeks shipping, shipping weeks and chains, and writes the Started row once', dbTest, async () => {
  const id = T + 1;
  const key = `maya${T}/recipes`;
  await person(id, key, { [wk(14)]: 2, [wk(7)]: 5, [wk(0)]: 3, [wk(35)]: 1 }, { other: `https://other${T}.example.com` });
  // An EarnKit deploy on Base for the project, and an entry in a program on Arc.
  await sql()`INSERT INTO contract_deploys (installation_id, builder_key, repo_key, chain, tx_hash, address, status, cost_micro_usd)
    VALUES (${id}, ${`k${id}`}, ${key}, 'base', ${`0x${'a'.repeat(64)}`}, ${`0x${'b'.repeat(40)}`}, 'deployed', 1)`;
  const arc = await program('arc-microgrants');
  await sql()`INSERT INTO projects (program_id, name, repo_url, repo_key, installation_id) VALUES (${arc.id}, 'R', ${`https://github.com/${key}`}, ${key}, ${id})`;
  await rescorePerson(id, NOW);
  const [p] = await sql()<{ streak: number; weeks_shipping: number; ship_weeks: string[]; chains: string[] }[]>`
    SELECT streak, weeks_shipping, ship_weeks, chains FROM builder_projects WHERE builder_id = ${id} AND project_key = ${key}`;
  assert.equal(p.streak, 3);
  assert.equal(p.weeks_shipping, 4);
  assert.deepEqual(new Set(p.ship_weeks), new Set([wk(35), wk(14), wk(7), wk(0)]));
  assert.deepEqual([...p.chains].sort(), ['arc', 'base']);
  const [live] = await sql()<{ ranked: boolean }[]>`SELECT ranked FROM builder_live WHERE builder_id = ${id}`;
  assert.equal(live.ranked, true, 'confirmed is enough under the flag (B5)');
  const started = await sql()<{ week: string; facts: { started?: unknown } }[]>`SELECT week::text, facts FROM project_updates WHERE builder_id = ${id}`;
  assert.equal(started.length, 1);
  assert.equal(started[0].week, mondayOf(NOW));
  assert.ok(started[0].facts.started);
  await rescorePerson(id, new Date(NOW.getTime() + 8 * DAY));
  assert.equal((await sql()`SELECT 1 FROM project_updates WHERE builder_id = ${id} AND facts ? 'started'`).length, 1, 'never twice');
});

test('4.4 an unconfirmed main project is not ranked, though another confirmed project would have been (B5)', dbTest, async () => {
  const id = T + 2;
  await person(id, `nope${T}/theirs`, { [wk(0)]: 1 }, { confirmed: false, other: `https://mine${T}.example.com` });
  await rescorePerson(id, NOW);

});

  const id = T + 3;
  const key = `ann${T}/shop`;
  const monday = mondayOf(new Date(NOW.getTime() - 7 * DAY));
  const inWeek = new Date(Date.parse(`${monday}T12:00:00Z`) + DAY);

  await sql()`INSERT INTO builder_projects (builder_id, project_key, name, kind, confirmed, shipped, ship_weeks) VALUES (${id}, ${key}, 'Shop', 'app', true, true, ${[isoWeekOf(new Date(inWeek.getTime() - 7 * DAY)), isoWeekOf(inWeek)]})`;
  await sql()`INSERT INTO contract_deploys (installation_id, builder_key, repo_key, chain, tx_hash, address, status, cost_micro_usd, created_at)
    VALUES (${id}, ${`k${id}`}, ${key}, 'base', ${`0x${'c'.repeat(64)}`}, ${`0x${'d'.repeat(40)}`}, 'deployed', 1, ${inWeek})`;

    INSERT INTO projects (program_id, name, repo_url, repo_key, installation_id, creator_wallet, coin_address, token_status, token_url)
    VALUES (${base.id}, 'Shop', ${`https://github.com/${key}`}, ${key}, ${id}, ${wallet}, ${`0x${(T % 1e9).toString(16).padStart(40, '0')}`}, 'launched', 'https://tokens.fun/x') RETURNING id AS pid`;
  await sql()`INSERT INTO token_fees (wallet, weth, usd, read_at) VALUES (${wallet}, '1000000000000000000', 3000, now()) ON CONFLICT (wallet) DO UPDATE SET weth = EXCLUDED.weth, usd = EXCLUDED.usd`;
  await sql()`INSERT INTO fee_claims (tx_hash, owner, role, weth, gas_wei, at) VALUES (${`0x${(T + 3).toString(16).padStart(64, '0')}`}, ${wallet}, 'creator', '40000000000000000', 0, ${inWeek})
    ON CONFLICT (tx_hash) DO NOTHING`;
  await sql()`INSERT INTO entry_outcomes (project_id, program_id, round_label, result, prize_usd, basis, captured_at) VALUES (${pid}, ${base.id}, 'r1', 'won', 500, 'announcement', ${inWeek})`;
  const f = await weekFactsFor(id, monday, NOW);
  assert.deepEqual(f.shipped_code, { n: 12 });
  assert.equal(f.went_live, true);
  assert.deepEqual(f.earned, { usd: 620 }, '0.04 WETH at $3,000 plus a $500 prize');
  assert.deepEqual(f.won?.map((w) => w.title), [base.title]);
  assert.deepEqual(f.streak, { w: 2 });
  await sql()`UPDATE builder_projects SET confirmed = false WHERE builder_id = ${id}`;
  assert.deepEqual(await weekFactsFor(id, monday, NOW), {});
});
```

`person-score.ts`:

1. `rank.ts` (append; T7 adds the percentile rule beside it):

```ts
/** v2.13 R14: the chains a project can be shown on (logos), in the Bench's order. */
export const BENCH_CHAINS = ['base', 'robinhood', 'arc'] as const;
export type BenchChain = (typeof BENCH_CHAINS)[number];
export const CHAIN_LABEL: Record<BenchChain, string> = { base: 'Base', robinhood: 'Robinhood Chain', arc: 'Arc' };
```

   and in `person-score.ts`: `import { BENCH_CHAINS, type BenchChain } from './rank';` plus `const CHAIN_IDS: Record<number, BenchChain> = { 8453: 'base', 4663: 'robinhood' };`.

2. `ProjectRow` gains `chains: BenchChain[]; streak: number; weeks_shipping: number; ship_weeks: string[];`.

3. `personInputsV2(id, now, personRing?, db = sql(), o: { scope?: string | null } = {})`. New reads after `events`:

```ts
  // v2.13: chains from program entries and launched coins (any chain; coin_address is Base only).
  const entries = await db<{ repo_key: string | null; chains: string[] | null }[]>`
    SELECT COALESCE(p.repo_key, p.token_repo_key) AS repo_key, g.chains FROM projects p JOIN programs g ON g.id = p.program_id
    WHERE p.installation_id IN ${db(group)} AND p.program_id <> ${COIN_PROGRAM} AND p.vetoed_at IS NULL`;
  const launched = await db<{ repo_key: string | null; chain_id: number | null; coin: string | null }[]>`
    SELECT COALESCE(token_repo_key, repo_key) AS repo_key, coin_chain_id AS chain_id, lower(coin_address) AS coin
    FROM projects WHERE installation_id IN ${db(group)} AND token_status = 'launched'`;
```

and change the `events` read to `SELECT at, lower(repo_key) AS repo_key, type ...`. Inside the per-key loop:
- `const inScope = !o.scope || lowKey(o.scope) === low;` Every push into `input` (`input.ships.push`, `input.touches.push`, `input.packages.push`, `input.earned.push`, and `addWeek`) happens only when `inScope` (wrap them: `if (inScope) input.ships.push(...ships)`, the `addWeek` helper takes the key: `addWeek(at, inScope)`). The project row itself is built for every key as today (rows stay, unshown: spec "Rows stay in the database").
- Shipping weeks for this key: `const ship = new Set<string>()`; add `isoWeek` of each commit week string from the repo readings (the `commit_weeks` loop: `ship.add(wk)`), of each `hostedHere.deployed_at`, each `deploysHere.created_at`, the first-up observation of each site, and each EarnKit `built`/`deployed`/`hosted` event whose `repo_key` equals `low`. Store `shipWeeks.set(key, [...ship].sort())`.
- New people: for each counted actor (weight above 0, kind not star or fork) take `firstSource.get(actor).at`; `newPeople.set(key, counts by isoWeek)`.
- Chains: `const chains = new Set<BenchChain>()`; a verified contract source `base:`/`arc:` (state verified) adds its prefix; each `deploysHere` adds its `chain`; each `launched` row with this repo key adds `CHAIN_IDS[chain_id] ?? (coin ? 'base' : null)`; each `entries` row with this repo key adds every `chains` value that is in `BENCH_CHAINS`. `chainsOf.set(key, BENCH_CHAINS.filter((c) => chains.has(c)))`.
- The row gets `chains`, `ship_weeks: [...ship].filter((w) => last53.has(w)).sort()` (with `const last53 = new Set([...last52(now), isoWeekOf(new Date(now.getTime() - 52 * WEEK_MS))])`), `streak: streakOf(ship, now)`, `weeks_shipping: weeksShipping(ship, now)`.
- The EarnKit events loop at the end (`for (const e of events) addWeek(e.at)`) becomes `for (const e of events) addWeek(e.at, !o.scope || e.repo_key === lowKey(o.scope))`.
- Return adds `newPeople`, `shipWeeks`, `chains: chainsOf`.
