`week-facts.ts`:

```ts
import { sql, type Db } from './db';
import { noteEvent } from './events';
import { money } from './page-data';
import { personInputsV2 } from './person-score';
import { COIN_PROGRAM } from './policy-constants';
import { isoWeekOf, lastFinishedMonday, mondayOf, streakOf, weekOfMonday } from './weeks';

/**
 * v2.13 spec "The facts": what EarnKit checked itself about one project in one ISO week, pinned to
 * that week's project_updates row (plan B6). The update's text never sets them.
 */
export type WeekFacts = {
  shipped_code?: { n: number }; went_live?: true; users_up?: { n: number }; earned?: { usd: number };
  won?: { title: string }[]; streak?: { w: number }; started?: { live: boolean; weeks: number; people: number }; checked?: true;
};
export const WEEKLY_HOUR_UTC = 15;
export const WEEKLY_PER_RUN = 200;
const DAY = 86_400_000;
const plural = (n: number, one: string, many: string) => `${n.toLocaleString('en-US')} ${n === 1 ? one : many}`;

export function factChips(f: WeekFacts, o: { raising?: string | null } = {}): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  if (f.went_live) out.push('Went live');
  for (const w of f.won ?? []) out.push(`Won ${w.title}`);
  if (f.users_up && f.users_up.n > 0) out.push(`+${plural(f.users_up.n, 'person', 'people')}`);
  if (f.earned && f.earned.usd >= 1) out.push(`${money(f.earned.usd)} earned`);
  if (f.shipped_code && f.shipped_code.n > 0) out.push(plural(f.shipped_code.n, 'commit', 'commits'));
  if (f.streak && f.streak.w >= 2) out.push(`${f.streak.w} weeks in a row`);
  if (o.raising) out.push(`Raising $${o.raising}`);
  return out;
}
export const startedLine = (name: string) => `Started tracking ${name}.`;
export function startedChips(s: { live: boolean; weeks: number; people: number }): string[] {
  return [
    ...(s.live ? ['Live'] : []),
    ...(s.weeks > 0 ? [plural(s.weeks, 'week shipping', 'weeks shipping')] : []),
    ...(s.people > 0 ? [plural(s.people, 'person uses it', 'people use it')] : []),
  ];
}
export const hasFacts = (f: WeekFacts): boolean =>
  Boolean((f.shipped_code?.n ?? 0) > 0 || f.went_live || (f.users_up?.n ?? 0) > 0 || (f.earned?.usd ?? 0) >= 1 || (f.won?.length ?? 0) > 0);
```

`weekFactsFor(builderId, monday, now)`:

```ts
export async function weekFactsFor(builderId: number, monday: string, now: Date): Promise<WeekFacts> {
  const week = weekOfMonday(monday);
  if (!week) return {};
  const db = sql();
  const [p] = await db<{ key: string; ship_weeks: string[] }[]>`
    SELECT b.project_key AS key, b.ship_weeks FROM builder_profiles f JOIN builder_projects b ON b.builder_id = f.builder_id AND lower(b.project_key) = lower(f.main_project_key)
    WHERE f.builder_id = ${builderId} AND b.confirmed`;
  if (!p) return {};
  const start = new Date(`${monday}T00:00:00Z`);
  const end = new Date(start.getTime() + 7 * DAY);
  const f: WeekFacts = {};
  // shipped_code: the main repo's newest good reading (live or daily), its count for the week.
  const [repo] = await db<{ counts: Record<string, number> | null }[]>`
    SELECT x.signals->'commit_counts' AS counts FROM builder_sources s
    JOIN LATERAL ((SELECT l.signals, l.read_at AS at FROM source_live l WHERE l.source_id = s.id AND l.ok)
      UNION ALL (SELECT r.signals, r.day::timestamptz AS at FROM source_readings r WHERE r.source_id = s.id AND r.ok ORDER BY r.day DESC LIMIT 1)
      ORDER BY at DESC LIMIT 1) x ON true
    WHERE s.builder_id = ${builderId} AND s.kind = 'repo' AND s.state = 'verified' AND lower(s.project_key) = lower(${p.key})`;
  const n = Number(repo?.counts?.[week] ?? 0);
  if (n > 0) f.shipped_code = { n };
  // went_live: the first live moment ever falls in this week, and no week was said to before.
  const [live] = await db<{ first: Date | null; said: boolean }[]>`
    SELECT LEAST(
      (SELECT min(h.deployed_at) FROM hosted_sites h JOIN installations i ON i.id = h.installation_id WHERE COALESCE(i.builder_id, i.id) = ${builderId} AND lower(h.repo_key) = lower(${p.key})),
      (SELECT min(d.created_at) FROM contract_deploys d JOIN installations i ON i.id = d.installation_id WHERE COALESCE(i.builder_id, i.id) = ${builderId} AND d.status = 'deployed' AND lower(d.repo_key) = lower(${p.key})),
      (SELECT min(r.day)::timestamptz FROM source_readings r JOIN builder_sources s ON s.id = r.source_id
        WHERE s.builder_id = ${builderId} AND s.kind = 'site' AND s.state = 'verified' AND lower(s.project_key) = lower(${p.key}) AND r.ok AND r.signals->>'up' = 'true')
    ) AS first,
    EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM builder_events e WHERE e.type = 'went_live' AND lower(e.repo_key) = lower(${p.key})) AS said`;
  if (live?.first && !live.said && live.first >= start && live.first < end) f.went_live = true;
  // users_up: counted people whose first touch fell in the week (the one project's inputs).
  const { newPeople } = await personInputsV2(builderId, now, undefined, db, { scope: p.key });
  const up = newPeople.get(p.key)?.[week] ?? 0;
  if (up > 0) f.users_up = { n: up };
  // earned: creator fee claims in the week, priced at the wallet's own USD per WETH; prizes captured in the week.
  const [fees] = await db<{ usd: string | null }[]>`
    SELECT sum(c.weth / 1e18 * (t.usd / NULLIF(t.weth::numeric / 1e18, 0)))::text AS usd
    FROM fee_claims c JOIN token_fees t ON lower(t.wallet) = lower(c.owner)
    WHERE c.role = 'creator' AND c.at >= ${start} AND c.at < ${end} AND lower(c.owner) IN (
      SELECT lower(x.creator_wallet) FROM projects x JOIN installations i ON i.id = x.installation_id
      WHERE COALESCE(i.builder_id, i.id) = ${builderId} AND x.token_status = 'launched' AND lower(COALESCE(x.token_repo_key, x.repo_key)) = lower(${p.key}))`;
  const outcomes = await db<{ title: string; usd: string | null; result: string }[]>`
    SELECT g.title, o.prize_usd::text AS usd, o.result FROM entry_outcomes o JOIN projects x ON x.id = o.project_id JOIN programs g ON g.id = o.program_id
    JOIN installations i ON i.id = x.installation_id
    WHERE COALESCE(i.builder_id, i.id) = ${builderId} AND o.basis = 'announcement' AND o.captured_at >= ${start} AND o.captured_at < ${end}
      AND x.program_id <> ${COIN_PROGRAM} AND lower(COALESCE(x.repo_key, x.token_repo_key)) = lower(${p.key}) ORDER BY o.captured_at`;
  const usd = Number(fees?.usd ?? 0) + outcomes.filter((x) => ['won', 'placed', 'finalist'].includes(x.result)).reduce((a, x) => a + Number(x.usd ?? 0), 0);
  if (usd >= 1) f.earned = { usd: Math.round(usd) };
  const won = outcomes.filter((x) => x.result === 'won' || x.result === 'placed').map((x) => ({ title: x.title }));
  if (won.length > 0) f.won = won;
  // streak: this week shipped, and the run ending here is 2 or more.
  if (p.ship_weeks.includes(week)) {
    const w = streakOf(p.ship_weeks.filter((x) => x <= week), new Date(start.getTime() + DAY));
    if (w >= 2) f.streak = { w };
  }
  return f;
}
```

(4.5 checks `earned` = 0.04 × 3000 + 500 = 620.)

```ts
/** The week's row for the project: facts merged in, text, pins and hides kept. */
export async function upsertWeekFacts(builderId: number, projectKey: string, monday: string, f: WeekFacts): Promise<void> {
  await sql()`
    INSERT INTO project_updates (builder_id, project_key, week, facts) VALUES (${builderId}, ${projectKey}, ${monday}, ${sql().json(f as never)})
    ON CONFLICT (project_key, week) DO UPDATE SET facts = project_updates.facts || EXCLUDED.facts
    WHERE project_updates.builder_id = EXCLUDED.builder_id`;
}

/** Plan B9: the "Started tracking" facts on the week the project entered, once per project. */
export async function ensureStartedRow(db: Db, builderId: number, key: string, at: Date, s: { live: boolean; weeks: number; people: number }): Promise<void> {
  const [had] = await db`SELECT 1 FROM project_updates WHERE lower(project_key) = lower(${key}) AND facts ? 'started' LIMIT 1`;
  if (had) return;
  await db`
    INSERT INTO project_updates (builder_id, project_key, week, facts) VALUES (${builderId}, ${key}, ${mondayOf(at)}, ${db.json({ started: s } as never)})
    ON CONFLICT (project_key, week) DO UPDATE SET facts = project_updates.facts || EXCLUDED.facts
    WHERE project_updates.builder_id = EXCLUDED.builder_id AND NOT (project_updates.facts ? 'started')`;
}

/**
 * spec "Cost": Mondays, after the digest (from 15:00 UTC), the finished week's facts for every one
 * project not done yet (facts.checked), WEEKLY_PER_RUN a run while `deadline` allows; then one dated
 * event per fact and project and week (dedupe key `<type>:<project_key>:<week>`). Shared clients never.
 */
export async function weeklyFactsStep(now: Date, o: { deadline: number; only?: number[] }): Promise<{ step: 'skipped' } | { people: number; rows: number }> {
  if (now.getUTCDay() !== 1 || now.getUTCHours() < WEEKLY_HOUR_UTC) return { step: 'skipped' };
  const monday = lastFinishedMonday(now);
  const week = weekOfMonday(monday)!;
  const db = sql();
  const due = await db<{ id: number; key: string }[]>`
    SELECT p.builder_id AS id, p.main_project_key AS key FROM builder_profiles p
    WHERE p.main_project_key IS NOT NULL
      AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM installations i WHERE COALESCE(i.builder_id, i.id) = p.builder_id AND i.shared_client)
      AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM project_updates u WHERE lower(u.project_key) = lower(p.main_project_key) AND u.week = ${monday} AND u.facts ? 'checked')
      ${o.only ? db`AND p.builder_id IN ${db(o.only.length ? o.only : [-1])}` : db``}
    ORDER BY p.builder_id LIMIT ${WEEKLY_PER_RUN}`;
  let people = 0;
  let rows = 0;
  for (const { id, key } of due) {
    if (Date.now() >= o.deadline) break;
    const f = await weekFactsFor(id, monday, now);
    await upsertWeekFacts(id, key, monday, { ...f, checked: true });
    people++;
    rows++;
    const at = new Date(`${monday}T12:00:00Z`);
    const ev = (type: 'shipped_code' | 'went_live' | 'users_up', detail: Record<string, unknown>) =>
      noteEvent(type, () => ({ type, dedupe_key: `${type}:${key.toLowerCase()}:${week}`, installation_id: id, repo_key: key.toLowerCase(), at, detail }));
    if (f.shipped_code) await ev('shipped_code', { n: f.shipped_code.n, week });
    if (f.went_live) await ev('went_live', { week });
    if (f.users_up) await ev('users_up', { n: f.users_up.n, week });
  }
  return { people, rows };
}
```

(The `repo_key` field of an event must be at most 300 characters and is lowercased by `recordEvent`; a non-repo key, a site origin, is stored the same way.)

`update-text.test.ts`:

```ts
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { UPDATE_EMPTY_NOTE, UPDATE_LINK_NOTE, UPDATE_SECRET_NOTE, checkUpdate, updateLongNote } from './update-text';

const OWN = { live: 'https://recipes.app', repo: 'maya/recipes' };
const ok = (raw: string) => {
  const r = checkUpdate(raw, OWN);
  assert.ok(r.ok, `${raw}: ${'note' in r ? r.note : ''}`);
  return r.text;
};
const refused = (raw: string, note: string) => assert.deepEqual(checkUpdate(raw, OWN), { ok: false, note }, raw);

test('5.1 one plain paragraph is kept; the project\'s own live site and repo may be linked', () => {
  assert.equal(ok('Added fridge photo import, so it plans a week of meals from a picture.'), 'Added fridge photo import, so it plans a week of meals from a picture.');
  assert.equal(ok('Shipped  login.\n\nTry it at https://recipes.app/new'), 'Shipped login. Try it at https://recipes.app/new');
  assert.equal(ok('Code: https://github.com/Maya/recipes/pull/3'), 'Code: https://github.com/Maya/recipes/pull/3');
  assert.equal(ok('github.com/maya/recipes has the new parser'), 'github.com/maya/recipes has the new parser');
  assert.equal(ok('Faster search — and a dark mode'), 'Faster search, and a dark mode', 'no em dashes in public text');
  assert.equal(ok('Moved to Next.js 16'), 'Moved to Next.js 16', 'a library name is not a link');
  assert.equal(ok('x'.repeat(280)).length, 280);
});

test('5.2 any other link is refused, however it is written (review focus 2)', () => {
  for (const raw of [
    'See https://evil.example/claim', 'see evil.example', 'see evil[.]example', 'see evil(.)example',
    'https://recipes.app.evil.example', 'https://recipes.app@evil.example', 'http://recipes.app/x',
    'https://github.com/someone-else/recipes', 'https://github.com/maya/recipes-evil', 'https://gist.github.com/maya/1',
    'javascript:alert(1)', 'Free money at bit.ly/xyz',
  ])
    refused(raw, UPDATE_LINK_NOTE);
});

test('5.3 keys, tokens, seed phrases and addresses are refused (review focus 2)', () => {
  for (const raw of [
    `key 0x${'ab'.repeat(32)}`, 'token ghp_0123456789abcdefghijABCDEFGHIJ0123', 'sk-0123456789abcdefghij0123456789', 'AKIAABCDEFGHIJKLMNOP',
    'my seed phrase is in here', 'the 12 words are safe', `send to 0x${'1'.repeat(40)}`, '-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----',
  ])
    refused(raw, UPDATE_SECRET_NOTE);
});

test('5.4 at most 280 characters after cleaning; empty is refused', () => {
  refused('x'.repeat(281), updateLongNote(281));
  refused('   ', UPDATE_EMPTY_NOTE);
  assert.deepEqual(checkUpdate(42, OWN), { ok: false, note: UPDATE_EMPTY_NOTE });
  assert.match(updateLongNote(281), /281 characters/);
});
```

```ts
import { after, test } from 'node:test';

import { HIDE_DATE_NOTE, HIDE_NONE_NOTE, NOT_CONFIRMED_NOTE, UPDATE_LINES, WEEKLY_UPDATE_NOTE, hideUpdate, postUpdate, setUpdates } from './updates';
import { UPDATE_LINK_NOTE } from './update-text';

process.env.EARNKIT_BUILDER_SCORE = 'on';

const dbTest = { skip: !process.env.DATABASE_URL && 'no DATABASE_URL' };

const RUN = randomBytes(3).toString('hex');

async function person(o: { confirmed?: boolean; handle?: string | null } = {}): Promise<{ id: number; key: string }> {
  const [{ id }] = await sql()<{ id: number }[]>`INSERT INTO installations (token_hash) VALUES (${`v213-upd-${randomBytes(8).toString('hex')}`}) RETURNING id`;
  const key = `maya${RUN}/r${id}`;
  await sql()`INSERT INTO builder_profiles (builder_id, slug, main_project_key, x_user_id, x_handle) VALUES (${id}, ${`v213u-${id}`}, ${key},
    ${o.handle ? `x${id}` : null}, ${o.handle ?? null})`;
  await sql()`INSERT INTO builder_projects (builder_id, project_key, name, link, kind, confirmed, shipped)
    VALUES (${id}, ${key}, 'Recipe planner', 'https://recipes.app', 'app', ${o.confirmed ?? true}, true)`;
  return { id, key };
}
const rows = async (key: string) => sql()<{ week: string; text: string | null; hidden: boolean; edited_at: Date | null }[]>`
  SELECT week::text, text, hidden, edited_at FROM project_updates WHERE project_key = ${key} ORDER BY week`;

test('5.5 a post lands on this week\'s row with the facts EarnKit checked, and one update_posted event', dbTest, async () => {
  const { id, key } = await person({ handle: `maya_${RUN}` });
  const r = await postUpdate(id, 'Added fridge photo import.', new Date('2026-10-14T10:00:00Z'));
  assert.equal(r.show_to_builder, UPDATE_LINES.postedAt(`earnkit.com/maya_${RUN}`));
  assert.deepEqual((await rows(key)).map((x) => [x.week, x.text]), [['2026-10-12', 'Added fridge photo import.']]);
  assert.equal((await sql()`SELECT 1 FROM builder_events WHERE dedupe_key = ${`update_posted:${key}:2026-W42`}`).length, 1);
});

test('5.6 a second post in the same ISO week replaces the text; Sunday 23:59 and Monday 00:00 are two weeks (review focus 2, 5)', dbTest, async () => {
  const { id, key } = await person();
  await postUpdate(id, 'First.', new Date('2026-10-12T00:00:00Z'));
  const again = await postUpdate(id, 'Second.', new Date('2026-10-18T23:59:59Z'));
  assert.equal(again.show_to_builder, UPDATE_LINES.changed);
  let r = await rows(key);
  assert.equal(r.length, 1);
  assert.equal(r[0].text, 'Second.');
  assert.ok(r[0].edited_at);
  await postUpdate(id, 'Next week.', new Date('2026-10-19T00:00:00Z'));
  r = await rows(key);
  assert.deepEqual(r.map((x) => x.week), ['2026-10-12', '2026-10-19']);
  const bad = await postUpdate(id, 'see https://evil.example', new Date('2026-10-19T01:00:00Z'));
  assert.deepEqual(bad, { show_to_builder: '', agent_note: UPDATE_LINK_NOTE });
  assert.equal((await rows(key))[1].text, 'Next week.', 'a refused post changes nothing');
});

test('5.7 no post for a project not confirmed as theirs (review focus 3)', dbTest, async () => {

test('5.8 hide takes any day of the week; a bad date or an empty week is a note (review focus 5)', dbTest, async () => {

  await postUpdate(id, 'Shipped.', new Date('2026-10-13T10:00:00Z'));
  assert.equal((await hideUpdate(id, '2026-10-14')).show_to_builder, UPDATE_LINES.hidden);
  assert.equal((await rows(key))[0].hidden, true);
  assert.equal((await sql()`SELECT 1 FROM builder_events WHERE dedupe_key = ${`update_hidden:${key}:2026-W42`}`).length, 1);
  assert.deepEqual(await hideUpdate(id, 'last week'), { show_to_builder: '', agent_note: HIDE_DATE_NOTE });
  assert.deepEqual(await hideUpdate(id, '2026-02-30'), { show_to_builder: '', agent_note: HIDE_DATE_NOTE });
  assert.deepEqual(await hideUpdate(id, '2025-01-01'), { show_to_builder: '', agent_note: HIDE_NONE_NOTE });

test('5.9 updates on records the yes once and sends the weekly task; scheduled is remembered; off clears both', dbTest, async () => {
  const { id } = await person();
  const on = await setUpdates(id, { on: true }, new Date());
  assert.equal(on.show_to_builder, UPDATE_LINES.on);
  assert.equal(on.schedule_weekly_update, WEEKLY_UPDATE_NOTE);
  const sched = await setUpdates(id, { on: true, scheduled: true }, new Date());
  assert.equal(sched.schedule_weekly_update, undefined);
  let [p] = await sql()<{ on_at: Date | null; sched: Date | null }[]>`SELECT updates_on_at AS on_at, updates_scheduled_at AS sched FROM builder_profiles WHERE builder_id = ${id}`;
  assert.ok(p.on_at && p.sched);
  assert.equal((await setUpdates(id, { on: false }, new Date())).show_to_builder, UPDATE_LINES.off);
  [p] = await sql()<{ on_at: Date | null; sched: Date | null }[]>`SELECT updates_on_at AS on_at, updates_scheduled_at AS sched FROM builder_profiles WHERE builder_id = ${id}`;
  assert.deepEqual(p, { on_at: null, sched: null });
});

});
```

`hosting.ts`: rename the `SEED` constant to an exported `SEED_WORDS` (same pattern) and use it where `SEED` was used.

`update-text.ts`:

```ts
import { SEED_WORDS } from './hosting';
import { UntrustedTextError, cleanText } from './untrusted-text';

/**
 * v2.13 spec "The text": one paragraph, at most 280 characters, plain words; links only to the
 * project's own live URL or repo; the same link, entity and secret checks as other agent text
 * (untrusted-text.ts cleanText). Pure.
 */
export const UPDATE_MAX = 280;
export const UPDATE_EMPTY_NOTE = "Write one plain paragraph about what shipped this week, then call tell with update: { text }. Don't show this note.";
export const UPDATE_LINK_NOTE = "Links are allowed only to the project's own live site or its GitHub repo. Remove the others and send it again. Don't show this note.";
export const UPDATE_SECRET_NOTE = "The update looks like it holds a key, a token, a seed phrase or an address. Never post those; write it again in plain words. Don't show this note.";
export const updateLongNote = (n: number) => `The update is ${n} characters; keep it to one plain paragraph of at most ${UPDATE_MAX}. Send it again with tell update. Don't show this note.`;
export type UpdateCheck = { ok: true; text: string } | { ok: false; note: string };

const SECRET = /\b0x[0-9a-f]{40,}\b|\b(?:ghp|gho|ghs|ghu|github_pat)_[A-Za-z0-9_]{20,}|\bsk-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{16,}|\bAKIA[0-9A-Z]{16}\b|-----BEGIN [A-Z ]*PRIVATE KEY-----/i;
/** A link with a scheme, or a GitHub link without one. Anything else that looks like a domain is cleanText's to refuse. */
const LINK = /\b(?:[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:\/\/\S+|(?:www\.)?github\.com\/\S+)/gi;
const TRAILING = /[).,;:!?'"]+$/;

/** The link points at the project's own live origin, or inside its own GitHub repo. */
function ownLink(raw: string, own: { live: string | null; repo: string | null }): boolean {
  const s = raw.replace(TRAILING, '');
  const u = URL.canParse(s) ? new URL(s) : URL.canParse(`https://${s}`) ? new URL(`https://${s}`) : null;
  if (!u || u.username || u.password || u.port || u.protocol !== 'https:') return false;
  if (own.live && URL.canParse(own.live) && u.origin === new URL(own.live).origin) return true;
  if (!own.repo || !/^(www\.)?github\.com$/i.test(u.hostname)) return false;
  const path = u.pathname.toLowerCase();
  const repo = `/${own.repo.toLowerCase()}`;
  return path === repo || path.startsWith(`${repo}/`);
}

export function checkUpdate(raw: unknown, own: { live: string | null; repo: string | null }): UpdateCheck {
  if (typeof raw !== 'string' || !raw.trim()) return { ok: false, note: UPDATE_EMPTY_NOTE };
  if (SECRET.test(raw) || SEED_WORDS.test(raw)) return { ok: false, note: UPDATE_SECRET_NOTE };
  const links: string[] = [];
  let bad = false;
  const masked = raw.replace(LINK, (m) => {
    const tail = TRAILING.exec(m)?.[0] ?? '';
    const link = m.slice(0, m.length - tail.length);
    if (!ownLink(link, own)) bad = true;
    links.push(link);
    return `ekupdlink${links.length - 1}${tail}`;
  });
  if (bad) return { ok: false, note: UPDATE_LINK_NOTE };
  let text: string;
  try {
    text = cleanText(masked.replace(/\s*[–—]\s*/g, ', '), { max: 4 * UPDATE_MAX, singleLine: true, field: 'update' });
  } catch (err) {
    if (err instanceof UntrustedTextError) return { ok: false, note: /characters/.test(err.message) ? updateLongNote(raw.length) : UPDATE_LINK_NOTE };
    throw err;
  }
  text = text.replace(/ekupdlink(\d+)/g, (_, i: string) => links[Number(i)] ?? '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  if (!text) return { ok: false, note: UPDATE_EMPTY_NOTE };
  const n = [...text].length;
  return n > UPDATE_MAX ? { ok: false, note: updateLongNote(n) } : { ok: true, text };
}
```

Notes for the implementer, from the cases: `cleanText` already refuses bare domains (`evil.example`, `bit.ly/xyz`), defanged forms (`evil[.]example`, `evil(.)example`), HTML entities and URI schemes left in the text, and lets library names (`Next.js`) through (`TECH_WORDS`). `http://` links fail `ownLink` (https only). `https://recipes.app@evil.example` has a username, so it fails. The placeholder `ekupdlinkN` is letters and digits only, so `cleanText` keeps it. If `cleanText`'s single-line mode collapses the double space and the newlines (5.1's second case), the last `replace(/\s+/g, ' ')` is a no-op; if it does not, that replace does it.

`updates.ts`:

```ts
import { sql } from './db';
import { noteEvent } from './events';
import { mainConfirmed, mainProjectOf } from './main-project';
import { pageUrl } from './reserved-routes';
import { checkUpdate } from './update-text';
import { upsertWeekFacts, weekFactsFor } from './week-facts';
import { mondayOf, weekOfMonday } from './weeks';

export type Reply = { show_to_builder: string; agent_note?: string; schedule_weekly_update?: string };
export const UPDATE_LINES = {
  posted: "Posted this week's update.",
  postedAt: (link: string) => `Posted this week's update: ${link}`,
  changed: "Changed this week's update.",
  hidden: 'Hidden from your page.',
  on: "Done. I'll post what you ship here every week.",
  off: "Okay, I've stopped posting updates.",
} as const;
export const NOT_CONFIRMED_NOTE = "The project isn't confirmed as theirs yet, so there is nothing to post to. Push the proof line from find first. Don't show this note.";
export const HIDE_DATE_NOTE = "hide takes a date, YYYY-MM-DD, any day of that week. Don't show this note.";
export const HIDE_NONE_NOTE = "There is no update that week. Don't show this note.";
export const WEEKLY_UPDATE_NOTE =
  'Once, if you can run scheduled tasks: schedule a weekly task (Mondays) in this project folder that reads git log for the past week and what you deployed, ' +
  'writes one plain paragraph (at most 280 characters, links only to the live site or the repo) and calls tell with update: { text }. ' +
  "Then call tell with updates: { on: true, scheduled: true }. Never ask the builder to write it. Don't show this note.";
const DATE = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/;
const REPO = /^[a-z0-9-]+\/[a-z0-9._-]+$/i;

export async function ownLinks(builderId: number): Promise<{ live: string | null; repo: string | null }> {
  const key = await mainProjectOf(builderId);
  const [p] = key ? await sql()<{ link: string | null }[]>`SELECT link FROM builder_projects WHERE builder_id = ${builderId} AND lower(project_key) = lower(${key})` : [];
  return { live: p?.link ?? null, repo: key && REPO.test(key) ? key.toLowerCase() : null };
}

export async function postUpdate(builderId: number, text: unknown, now: Date): Promise<Reply> {
  const key = await mainProjectOf(builderId);
  if (!key || !(await mainConfirmed(builderId))) return { show_to_builder: '', agent_note: NOT_CONFIRMED_NOTE };
  const checked = checkUpdate(text, await ownLinks(builderId));
  if (!checked.ok) return { show_to_builder: '', agent_note: checked.note };
  const monday = mondayOf(now);
  const week = weekOfMonday(monday)!;
  const facts = await weekFactsFor(builderId, monday, now).catch(() => ({}));
  const [r] = await sql()<{ was: string | null }[]>`
    WITH old AS (SELECT text AS was FROM project_updates WHERE project_key = ${key} AND week = ${monday})
    INSERT INTO project_updates (builder_id, project_key, week, text, facts) VALUES (${builderId}, ${key}, ${monday}, ${checked.text}, ${sql().json(facts as never)})
    ON CONFLICT (project_key, week) DO UPDATE SET text = EXCLUDED.text, facts = project_updates.facts || EXCLUDED.facts, hidden = false,
      edited_at = CASE WHEN project_updates.text IS NULL THEN project_updates.edited_at ELSE now() END
    WHERE project_updates.builder_id = EXCLUDED.builder_id
    RETURNING (SELECT was FROM old) AS was`;
  if (!r) return { show_to_builder: '', agent_note: NOT_CONFIRMED_NOTE };
  await noteEvent('update_posted', () => ({
    type: 'update_posted', dedupe_key: `update_posted:${key.toLowerCase()}:${week}`, installation_id: builderId, repo_key: key.toLowerCase(), detail: { week },
  }));
  if (r.was !== null) return { show_to_builder: UPDATE_LINES.changed };
  const [p] = await sql()<{ handle: string | null }[]>`SELECT x_handle AS handle FROM builder_profiles WHERE builder_id = ${builderId} AND x_user_id IS NOT NULL`;
  return { show_to_builder: p?.handle ? UPDATE_LINES.postedAt(pageUrl(p.handle)) : UPDATE_LINES.posted };
}

export async function hideUpdate(builderId: number, day: unknown): Promise<Reply> {
  const t = typeof day === 'string' && DATE.test(day) ? new Date(`${day}T00:00:00Z`) : null;
  if (!t || !Number.isFinite(t.getTime()) || t.toISOString().slice(0, 10) !== day) return { show_to_builder: '', agent_note: HIDE_DATE_NOTE };
  const monday = mondayOf(t);
  const key = await mainProjectOf(builderId);
  const [r] = key
    ? await sql()<{ key: string }[]>`
        UPDATE project_updates SET hidden = true WHERE builder_id = ${builderId} AND project_key = ${key} AND week = ${monday} AND text IS NOT NULL
        RETURNING project_key AS key`
    : [];
  if (!r) return { show_to_builder: '', agent_note: HIDE_NONE_NOTE };
  const week = weekOfMonday(monday)!;
  await noteEvent('update_hidden', () => ({
    type: 'update_hidden', dedupe_key: `update_hidden:${r.key.toLowerCase()}:${week}`, installation_id: builderId, repo_key: r.key.toLowerCase(), detail: { week },
  }));
  return { show_to_builder: UPDATE_LINES.hidden };
}

export async function setUpdates(builderId: number, u: { on: boolean; scheduled?: boolean }, now: Date): Promise<Reply> {
  if (!u.on) {
    await sql()`UPDATE builder_profiles SET updates_on_at = NULL, updates_scheduled_at = NULL WHERE builder_id = ${builderId}`;
    return { show_to_builder: UPDATE_LINES.off };
  }
  const [p] = await sql()<{ sched: Date | null }[]>`
    UPDATE builder_profiles SET updates_on_at = COALESCE(updates_on_at, ${now}),
      updates_scheduled_at = CASE WHEN ${u.scheduled === true} THEN COALESCE(updates_scheduled_at, ${now}) ELSE updates_scheduled_at END
    WHERE builder_id = ${builderId} RETURNING updates_scheduled_at AS sched`;
  return { show_to_builder: UPDATE_LINES.on, ...(!p?.sched && { schedule_weekly_update: WEEKLY_UPDATE_NOTE }) };
}
```

(5.6: the second post's `was` is 'First.', so the reply is `changed`; `edited_at` is set. The Started row of T4 has `text IS NULL`, so the first real post that week is "posted", not "changed".)

### Task 6: The feed: points, the daily rank step, the owner's pin and hide, `/feed`

Spec "The feed (R7)": every week's visible updates with at least one fact, ranked by progress points; the top 20 of the last finished ISO week, then older weeks; the owner can pin or hide; `/feed` shows long cards.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/feed.ts`
- Create: `apps/frontend/scripts/feed.mts`
- Modify: `package.json` (repo root: `feed:pin`, `feed:hide` scripts)
- Modify: `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/builder-score.ts` (`runScoreSteps`: the `feed` step under the flag, once a day)
- Create: `apps/frontend/app/components/ui/UpdateCard.tsx`, `apps/frontend/app/feed/page.tsx`, `apps/frontend/app/feed/FeedView.tsx`
- Test: `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/feed.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `benchEnabled` (T1); `lastFinishedMonday`, `weekLabel` (T1); `WeekFacts`, `factChips`, `hasFacts`, `startedLine`, `startedChips` (T4); `listedRanked` (person-score.ts); `avatarUrl`, `displayName` (page-data.ts); `pagePath` (reserved-routes.ts).
- Produces:
  - `pointsOf(f: WeekFacts): number` (pure)
  - `FEED_WEEK_MAX = 20`, `FEED_OLDER_WEEKS = 3`, `CAROUSEL_MAX = 12`
  - `rankFeed(now: Date, o?: { weeks?: number }): Promise<{ rows: number }>` (writes `points` for the last `weeks` Mondays, default 5)
  - `feedRankStep(now: Date): Promise<{ step: 'skipped' } | { rows: number }>` (once a UTC day)
  - `type FeedCard = { id: number; handle: string; name: string; avatar: string | null; project: string; week: string; text: string | null; chips: string[]; pinned: boolean }`
  - `feedWeeks(now: Date, o?: { only?: number[] }): Promise<{ monday: string; label: string; cards: FeedCard[] }[]>`
  - `carouselCards(now: Date, o?: { only?: number[] }): Promise<FeedCard[]>` (top `CAROUSEL_MAX`)
  - `setFeedFlag(id: number, what: 'pin' | 'hide', on: boolean): Promise<boolean>`
  - `UpdateCard({ card, shape }: { card: FeedCard | ProfileCard; shape: 'long' | 'square' })` (React)

```ts
import { after, test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import * as React from 'react';
import { renderToStaticMarkup } from 'react-dom/server';
import { sql } from './db';
import { CAROUSEL_MAX, FEED_WEEK_MAX, carouselCards, feedWeeks, pointsOf, rankFeed, setFeedFlag } from './feed';
import FeedView, { FEED_TITLE } from '../../app/feed/FeedView';
import UpdateCard from '../../app/components/ui/UpdateCard';

process.env.EARNKIT_BUILDER_SCORE = 'on';
process.env.EARNKIT_BENCH = 'on';
const dbTest = { skip: !process.env.DATABASE_URL && 'no DATABASE_URL' };
after(async () => { if (process.env.DATABASE_URL) await sql().end(); });
(globalThis as { React?: typeof React }).React = React;
const RUN = randomBytes(3).toString('hex');
const NOW = new Date('2026-10-21T12:00:00Z'); // a Wednesday; the last finished week is 2026-10-12

test('6.1 the points table, exactly', () => {
  assert.equal(pointsOf({ went_live: true }), 40);
  assert.equal(pointsOf({ won: [{ title: 'A' }, { title: 'B' }] }), 40, 'a win is 40, however many');
  assert.equal(pointsOf({ users_up: { n: 1 } }), 6);
  assert.equal(pointsOf({ users_up: { n: 31 } }), 30, 'min(30, 6 * log2(32)) = 30');
  assert.equal(pointsOf({ users_up: { n: 7 } }), 18);
  assert.equal(pointsOf({ earned: { usd: 10 } }), 5);
  assert.equal(pointsOf({ earned: { usd: 150 } }), 20, 'min(20, 5 * log2(16))');
  assert.equal(pointsOf({ shipped_code: { n: 12 } }), 10);
  assert.equal(pointsOf({ streak: { w: 6 } }), 6);
  assert.equal(pointsOf({ streak: { w: 14 } }), 10);
  assert.equal(pointsOf({ started: { live: true, weeks: 6, people: 31 } }), 0, 'the start is not progress');
  assert.equal(pointsOf({ went_live: true, users_up: { n: 31 }, shipped_code: { n: 1 }, streak: { w: 3 } }), 83);
});

/** A listed person (claimed, ranked, shown) with one confirmed main project and rows by week. */
async function listed(score: number, weeks: { monday: string; text: string | null; facts: Record<string, unknown> }[]): Promise<{ id: number; key: string; rowIds: number[] }> {
  const [{ id }] = await sql()<{ id: number }[]>`INSERT INTO installations (token_hash) VALUES (${`v213-feed-${randomBytes(8).toString('hex')}`}) RETURNING id`;
  const key = `f${RUN}/p${id}`;
  await sql()`INSERT INTO builder_profiles (builder_id, slug, main_project_key, x_user_id, x_handle, x_name) VALUES (${id}, ${`v213f-${id}`}, ${key}, ${`fx${id}`}, ${`f${RUN}${id}`}, 'Maya')`;
  await sql()`INSERT INTO builder_live (builder_id, score, parts, ranked, shipped, people, formula, calibration, computed_at) VALUES (${id}, ${score}, '{}'::jsonb, true, 1, 0, 'v2', 'v2-launch', now())`;
  await sql()`INSERT INTO builder_projects (builder_id, project_key, name, kind, confirmed, shipped) VALUES (${id}, ${key}, 'Recipe planner', 'app', true, true)`;
  const rowIds: number[] = [];
  for (const w of weeks) {
    const [{ rid }] = await sql()<{ rid: number }[]>`INSERT INTO project_updates (builder_id, project_key, week, text, facts) VALUES (${id}, ${key}, ${w.monday}, ${w.text}, ${sql().json(w.facts as never)}) RETURNING id::int AS rid`;
    rowIds.push(rid);
  }
  return { id, key, rowIds };
}

test('6.2 the feed ranks a finished week by points, ties by the Bench score; no facts never reaches it; a hidden update leaves it', dbTest, async () => {
  const a = await listed(40, [{ monday: '2026-10-12', text: 'Went live today.', facts: { went_live: true } }]);
  const b = await listed(90, [{ monday: '2026-10-12', text: 'Pushed code.', facts: { shipped_code: { n: 3 } } }]);
  const c = await listed(10, [{ monday: '2026-10-12', text: 'Also went live.', facts: { went_live: true } }]);
  const d = await listed(99, [{ monday: '2026-10-12', text: 'Thinking about it.', facts: {} }]);
  const e = await listed(99, [{ monday: '2026-10-12', text: 'Hidden one.', facts: { went_live: true } }]);
  await sql()`UPDATE project_updates SET hidden = true WHERE id = ${e.rowIds[0]}`;
  const only = [a.id, b.id, c.id, d.id, e.id];
  await rankFeed(NOW);
  const [week] = await feedWeeks(NOW, { only });
  assert.equal(week.monday, '2026-10-12');
  assert.equal(week.label, 'Week of Oct 12');
  assert.deepEqual(week.cards.map((x) => x.text), ['Went live today.', 'Also went live.', 'Pushed code.'], 'a 40 before a 10; the tie by score (40 over 10)');
  assert.deepEqual(week.cards[0].chips, ['Went live']);
});

test('6.3 the owner\'s pin leads its week; the owner\'s hide leaves the feed and stays on the profile; facts-only weeks show their chips', dbTest, async () => {
  const a = await listed(10, [{ monday: '2026-10-12', text: 'Small thing.', facts: { shipped_code: { n: 1 } } }]);
  const b = await listed(90, [{ monday: '2026-10-12', text: 'Big thing.', facts: { went_live: true, users_up: { n: 31 } } }]);
  const c = await listed(50, [{ monday: '2026-10-12', text: null, facts: { shipped_code: { n: 9 } } }]);
  await rankFeed(NOW);
  assert.equal(await setFeedFlag(a.rowIds[0], 'pin', true), true);
  assert.equal(await setFeedFlag(b.rowIds[0], 'hide', true), true);
  assert.equal(await setFeedFlag(999_999_999, 'pin', true), false);
  const [week] = await feedWeeks(NOW, { only: [a.id, b.id, c.id] });
  assert.deepEqual(week.cards.map((x) => [x.text, x.pinned]), [['Small thing.', true], [null, false]]);
  const [row] = await sql()<{ hidden: boolean; feed_hidden: boolean }[]>`SELECT hidden, feed_hidden FROM project_updates WHERE id = ${b.rowIds[0]}`;
  assert.deepEqual(row, { hidden: false, feed_hidden: true });
});

test('6.4 the last finished week first, at most 20, then older weeks; the carousel is the top 12', dbTest, async () => {
  const people = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < FEED_WEEK_MAX + 2; i++)
    people.push(await listed(i, [{ monday: '2026-10-12', text: `Update ${i}.`, facts: { shipped_code: { n: 1 }, streak: { w: (i % 9) + 2 } } }, { monday: '2026-10-05', text: `Older ${i}.`, facts: { shipped_code: { n: 1 } } }]));
  const only = people.map((p) => p.id);
  await rankFeed(NOW);
  const weeks = await feedWeeks(NOW, { only });
  assert.deepEqual(weeks.map((w) => w.monday), ['2026-10-12', '2026-10-05']);
  assert.equal(weeks[0].cards.length, FEED_WEEK_MAX);
  assert.equal((await carouselCards(NOW, { only })).length, CAROUSEL_MAX);
  // The current week (not finished) is never in the feed.
  await listed(100, [{ monday: '2026-10-19', text: 'This week.', facts: { went_live: true } }]);
  assert.ok(!(await feedWeeks(NOW)).some((w) => w.monday === '2026-10-19'));
});

test('6.5 a person who is not listed (unclaimed, hidden or held) or a project not confirmed never reaches the feed', dbTest, async () => {
  const a = await listed(50, [{ monday: '2026-10-12', text: 'Hidden page.', facts: { went_live: true } }]);
  await sql()`UPDATE builder_profiles SET hidden = true WHERE builder_id = ${a.id}`;
  const b = await listed(50, [{ monday: '2026-10-12', text: 'Not mine.', facts: { went_live: true } }]);
  await sql()`UPDATE builder_projects SET confirmed = false WHERE builder_id = ${b.id}`;
  await rankFeed(NOW);
  const weeks = await feedWeeks(NOW, { only: [a.id, b.id] });
  assert.deepEqual(weeks, []);
});

test('6.6 the feed page: long cards, plain words, no "written by", no all-caps labels', () => {
  const card = { id: 1, handle: 'maya_makes', name: 'Maya Chen', avatar: null, project: 'Recipe planner', week: '2026-10-12', text: 'Added fridge photo import.', chips: ['Went live', '+31 people'], pinned: false };
  const html = renderToStaticMarkup(React.createElement(FeedView, { weeks: [{ monday: '2026-10-12', label: 'Week of Oct 12', cards: [card] }] }));

  assert.match(square, /data-shape="square"/);
  assert.equal(renderToStaticMarkup(React.createElement(FeedView, { weeks: [] })).includes('No updates yet'), true);
});
```

`feed.ts`:

```ts
import { sql } from './db';
import { hit } from './rate-limit';
import { listedRanked } from './person-score';
import { avatarUrl, displayName } from './page-data';
import { factChips, hasFacts, type WeekFacts } from './week-facts';
import { lastFinishedMonday, weekLabel } from './weeks';

/** v2.13 spec "The feed (R7)": progress points, the daily rank, the feed's weeks and the carousel. */
export const FEED_WEEK_MAX = 20;
export const FEED_OLDER_WEEKS = 3;
export const CAROUSEL_MAX = 12;
const log2 = (x: number) => Math.log(x) / Math.LN2;

export function pointsOf(f: WeekFacts): number {
  let p = 0;
  if (f.went_live) p += 40;
  if ((f.won?.length ?? 0) > 0) p += 40;
  if (f.users_up && f.users_up.n > 0) p += Math.min(30, 6 * log2(1 + f.users_up.n));
  if (f.earned && f.earned.usd > 0) p += Math.min(20, 5 * log2(1 + f.earned.usd / 10));
  if (f.shipped_code && f.shipped_code.n > 0) p += 10;
  if (f.streak && f.streak.w > 0) p += Math.min(10, f.streak.w);
  return Math.round(p);
}

/** The rank step: points for the last `weeks` Mondays' rows (0 for a row with no facts). */
export async function rankFeed(now: Date, o: { weeks?: number } = {}): Promise<{ rows: number }> {
  const from = new Date(`${lastFinishedMonday(now)}T00:00:00Z`);
  from.setUTCDate(from.getUTCDate() - 7 * ((o.weeks ?? 5) - 1));
  const rows = await sql()<{ id: number; facts: WeekFacts }[]>`SELECT id::int AS id, facts FROM project_updates WHERE week >= ${from}`;
  for (const r of rows) {
    const p = hasFacts(r.facts) ? pointsOf(r.facts) : 0;
    await sql()`UPDATE project_updates SET points = ${p} WHERE id = ${r.id} AND points <> ${p}`;
  }
  return { rows: rows.length };
}

/** Once a UTC day (spec "Cost"): the first cron run of the day ranks; the rate-limit window marks it (no new table). */
export async function feedRankStep(now: Date): Promise<{ step: 'skipped' } | { rows: number }> {
  if (!(await hit(`feedRank:${now.toISOString().slice(0, 10)}`, 1, 86_400, now)).allowed) return { step: 'skipped' };
  return rankFeed(now);
}
```

```ts
export type FeedCard = { id: number; handle: string; name: string; avatar: string | null; project: string; week: string; text: string | null; chips: string[]; pinned: boolean };
type Row = { id: number; week: string; text: string | null; facts: WeekFacts; pinned: boolean; handle: string; x_name: string | null; avatar: string | null; project: string };

/** Visible updates with facts, of listed people's confirmed one project, from `since` to before `until`. */
async function feedRows(since: string, until: string, limitPerWeek: number, only?: number[]): Promise<Row[]> {
  const db = sql();
  return db<Row[]>`
    SELECT * FROM (
      SELECT u.id::int AS id, u.week::text AS week, u.text, u.facts, u.pinned, p.x_handle AS handle, p.x_name, p.x_avatar_url AS avatar, b.name AS project,
        row_number() OVER (PARTITION BY u.week ORDER BY u.pinned DESC, u.points DESC, l.score DESC, u.id) AS n
      FROM project_updates u
      JOIN ${listedRanked(db, only)} l ON l.builder_id = u.builder_id
      JOIN builder_profiles p ON p.builder_id = u.builder_id AND lower(p.main_project_key) = lower(u.project_key)
      JOIN builder_projects b ON b.builder_id = u.builder_id AND lower(b.project_key) = lower(u.project_key) AND b.confirmed
      WHERE u.week >= ${since} AND u.week < ${until} AND NOT u.hidden AND NOT u.feed_hidden AND u.points > 0
    ) x WHERE x.n <= ${limitPerWeek} ORDER BY x.week DESC, x.n`;
}
const card = (r: Row): FeedCard => ({
  id: r.id, handle: r.handle, name: displayName(r.x_name, r.handle), avatar: avatarUrl(r.avatar), project: r.project, week: r.week,
  text: r.text, chips: factChips(r.facts), pinned: r.pinned,
});

export async function feedWeeks(now: Date, o: { only?: number[] } = {}) {
  const last = lastFinishedMonday(now);
  const since = new Date(`${last}T00:00:00Z`);
  since.setUTCDate(since.getUTCDate() - 7 * FEED_OLDER_WEEKS);
  const until = new Date(`${last}T00:00:00Z`);
  until.setUTCDate(until.getUTCDate() + 7);
  const rows = await feedRows(since.toISOString().slice(0, 10), until.toISOString().slice(0, 10), FEED_WEEK_MAX, o.only);
  const out = new Map<string, FeedCard[]>();
  for (const r of rows) out.set(r.week, [...(out.get(r.week) ?? []), card(r)]);
  return [...out].map(([monday, cards]) => ({ monday, label: weekLabel(monday), cards }));
}

/** The landing carousel: the feed's first CAROUSEL_MAX cards (the last finished week, then older ones). */
export async function carouselCards(now: Date, o: { only?: number[] } = {}): Promise<FeedCard[]> {
  return (await feedWeeks(now, o)).flatMap((w) => w.cards).slice(0, CAROUSEL_MAX);
}

export async function setFeedFlag(id: number, what: 'pin' | 'hide', on: boolean): Promise<boolean> {
  const rows = what === 'pin'
    ? await sql()`UPDATE project_updates SET pinned = ${on} WHERE id = ${id} RETURNING 1`
    : await sql()`UPDATE project_updates SET feed_hidden = ${on} WHERE id = ${id} RETURNING 1`;
  return rows.length > 0;
}
```

(6.2's tie: `Went live today.` (40, score 40) and `Also went live.` (40, score 10) both have 40 points, so the Bench score orders them; `Pushed code.` has 10. 6.3: `c`'s row has text NULL and a fact, so it shows (chips alone); a pinned row leads whatever its points.)

`scripts/feed.mts`:

```ts
import './env.mts';
import { sql } from '../lib/earnkit/db';
import { setFeedFlag } from '../lib/earnkit/feed';

/**
 * v2.13 spec "Owner control": pnpm feed:pin <update-id> [--off], pnpm feed:hide <update-id> [--off].
 * Owner-run only. Pin: the update leads its week in the feed. Hide: it leaves the feed and stays on the profile.
 */
const [what, raw, flag] = process.argv.slice(2);
const id = Number(raw);
if ((what !== 'pin' && what !== 'hide') || !Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0 || (flag !== undefined && flag !== '--off')) {
  console.error('Usage: pnpm feed:pin <update-id> [--off] | pnpm feed:hide <update-id> [--off]');
  process.exit(2);
}
const done = await setFeedFlag(id, what, flag !== '--off');
console.log(done ? `${what}${flag ? ' off' : ''}: update ${id}` : `No update ${id}.`);
await sql().end();
process.exit(done ? 0 : 1);
```

```tsx
import { notFound } from 'next/navigation';
import { unstable_cache } from 'next/cache';
import { benchEnabled } from '@/lib/earnkit/builder-score-flag';
import { feedWeeks } from '@/lib/earnkit/feed';
import FeedView, { FEED_LINE, FEED_TITLE } from './FeedView';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';
export const metadata = { title: `${FEED_TITLE} · EarnKit`, description: FEED_LINE };
const cached = unstable_cache(async () => feedWeeks(new Date()), ['feed-v213'], { revalidate: 300 });

/** v2.13 spec "The feed": long cards. A 404 with the flag off. */
export default async function FeedPage() {
  if (!benchEnabled()) notFound();
  return <FeedView weeks={await cached()} />;
}
```

```ts
import { BENCH_PERCENT_FROM, CHAIN_CHIP_MIN, benchPercent, benchRank, percentText } from './rank';

test('7.1 P = 100 * rank / total: whole numbers rounded up from 1, one decimal rounded up under 1, never below 0.1 (review focus 1)', () => {
  assert.equal(benchPercent(10, 1000), 1, 'exactly 1');
  assert.equal(benchPercent(4, 100), 4);
  assert.equal(benchPercent(18, 100), 18);
  assert.equal(benchPercent(101, 200), 51, '50.5 rounds up');
  assert.equal(benchPercent(3, 1000), 0.3);
  assert.equal(benchPercent(1, 300), 0.4, '0.33 rounds up to 0.4');
  assert.equal(benchPercent(1, 1000), 0.1);
  assert.equal(benchPercent(1, 5000), 0.1, '0.02 floors at 0.1');
  assert.equal(percentText(0.3), '0.3');
  assert.equal(percentText(4), '4');
  assert.equal(BENCH_PERCENT_FROM, 20);
  assert.equal(CHAIN_CHIP_MIN, 10);
});

test('7.2 "Top P%" only from 20 ranked and P <= 50; "#N of M" under 20 in the top half; otherwise no line (review focus 1)', () => {
  const s = (rank: number, total: number) => ({ ranked: true, rank, total });
  assert.deepEqual(benchRank(s(18, 100)), { short: 'Top 18% on the Bench', phrase: 'top 18% of builders' });
  assert.deepEqual(benchRank(s(3, 1000)), { short: 'Top 0.3% on the Bench', phrase: 'top 0.3% of builders' });
  assert.deepEqual(benchRank(s(1, 9000)), { short: 'Top 0.1% on the Bench', phrase: 'top 0.1% of builders' });
  assert.deepEqual(benchRank(s(10, 20)), { short: 'Top 50% on the Bench', phrase: 'top 50% of builders' }, 'P exactly 50 shows');
  assert.equal(benchRank(s(500_001, 1_000_000)), null, '50.0001 does not');
  assert.equal(benchRank(s(11, 20)), null);
  assert.deepEqual(benchRank(s(10, 19)), { short: '#10 of 19 on the Bench', phrase: '#10 of 19 builders' });
  assert.equal(benchRank(s(11, 19)), null, 'outside the top half');
  assert.deepEqual(benchRank(s(1, 1)), { short: '#1 of 1 on the Bench', phrase: '#1 of 1 builder' });
  for (const bad of [{ ranked: false, rank: 1, total: 30 }, s(0, 30), s(31, 30), s(Number.NaN, 30)]) assert.equal(benchRank(bad), null);
});
```

```ts
import { after, test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import { sql } from './db';
import { chainPools, rankLines } from './bench-rank';

process.env.EARNKIT_BUILDER_SCORE = 'on';
process.env.EARNKIT_BENCH = 'on';
const dbTest = { skip: !process.env.DATABASE_URL && 'no DATABASE_URL' };
after(async () => { if (process.env.DATABASE_URL) await sql().end(); });
const RUN = randomBytes(3).toString('hex');

async function listed(score: number, chains: string[]): Promise<number> {
  const [{ id }] = await sql()<{ id: number }[]>`INSERT INTO installations (token_hash) VALUES (${`v213-br-${randomBytes(8).toString('hex')}`}) RETURNING id`;
  const key = `b${RUN}/p${id}`;
  await sql()`INSERT INTO builder_profiles (builder_id, slug, main_project_key, x_user_id, x_handle) VALUES (${id}, ${`v213b-${id}`}, ${key}, ${`bx${id}`}, ${`b${RUN}${id}`})`;
  await sql()`INSERT INTO builder_live (builder_id, score, parts, ranked, shipped, people, formula, calibration, computed_at) VALUES (${id}, ${score}, '{}'::jsonb, true, 1, 0, 'v2', 'v2-launch', now())`;
  await sql()`INSERT INTO builder_projects (builder_id, project_key, name, kind, confirmed, shipped, chains) VALUES (${id}, ${key}, 'P', 'app', true, true, ${chains})`;
  return id;
}

test('7.4 a chain\'s line shows only from 10 ranked projects on it; the Bench line uses the whole pool (review focus 1)', dbTest, async () => {
  const base: number[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) base.push(await listed(50 + i, ['base']));
  const arc: number[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) arc.push(await listed(40 + i, ['arc']));
  const me = base[9]; // the top Base score (59)
  const only = [...base, ...arc];
  const pools = await chainPools({ only });
  assert.equal(pools.base, 10);
  assert.equal(pools.arc, 9);
  assert.equal(pools.robinhood, 0);
  const lines = await rankLines(me, { only });
  assert.equal(lines.bench, '#1 of 19 on the Bench');
  assert.deepEqual(lines.chains, [{ chain: 'base', label: 'Base', line: '#1 of 10 on Base' }]);
  const arcTop = arc[8];
  assert.deepEqual((await rankLines(arcTop, { only })).chains, [], 'arc has 9: no chip, no line');
  const low = base[0]; // score 50: behind the 9 higher Base scores, ahead of all 9 Arc scores
  assert.equal((await rankLines(low, { only })).bench, '#10 of 19 on the Bench', '10th of 19 is the top half\'s last place');
  assert.deepEqual((await rankLines(low, { only })).chains, [], '10th of 10 on Base is outside its top half');
});
```

```ts
/** Builder and public text added in v2.13, checked by the v2.13 rule (plan B17). Names (projects, programs, $TICKERS) are exempt. */

];

test('7.5 every v2.13 string passes the v2.13 copy rule', () => {

test('7.6 the rule: chain names only inside a rank line, "percentile", "signal", "outcome", the builder bonus and "written by" never', () => {
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`rank.ts` (append below T4's chain constants):

```ts
/**
 * v2.13 spec "Percentile (R13)": P = 100 * rank / total. "Top P%" only from BENCH_PERCENT_FROM ranked
 * and P <= TOP_PERCENT_MAX; P of 1 or more rounded up to a whole number, under 1 rounded up to one
 * decimal and never below 0.1. Under BENCH_PERCENT_FROM: "#N of M", only in the top half. A chain's
 * pool uses the same rule and names the chain; a chain's line shows only from CHAIN_CHIP_MIN ranked
 * projects on it (bench-rank.ts). The word "percentile" never appears. Pure.
 */
export const BENCH_PERCENT_FROM = 20;
export const CHAIN_CHIP_MIN = 10;
export function benchPercent(rank: number, total: number): number {
  const whole = Math.ceil((100 * rank) / total);
  if (whole > 1 || (100 * rank) / total === 1) return whole;
  return Math.max(1, Math.ceil((1000 * rank) / total)) / 10;
}
export const percentText = (p: number) => (p >= 1 ? String(p) : p.toFixed(1));
export function benchRank(s: Standing, o: { chain?: BenchChain } = {}): { short: string; phrase: string } | null {
  if (!s.ranked || !Number.isFinite(s.rank) || !Number.isFinite(s.total) || s.rank < 1 || s.total < 1 || s.rank > s.total) return null;
  const where = o.chain ? `on ${CHAIN_LABEL[o.chain]}` : 'on the Bench';
  if (s.total < BENCH_PERCENT_FROM) {
    if (s.rank > Math.ceil(s.total / 2)) return null;
    const of = `#${s.rank} of ${s.total}`;
    return { short: `${of} ${where}`, phrase: o.chain ? `${of} ${where}` : `${of} ${s.total === 1 ? 'builder' : 'builders'}` };
  }
  const p = benchPercent(s.rank, s.total);
  if ((100 * s.rank) / s.total > TOP_PERCENT_MAX) return null;
  const t = percentText(p);
  return { short: `Top ${t}% ${where}`, phrase: o.chain ? `top ${t}% ${where}` : `top ${t}% of builders` };
}
```

(`benchPercent(10, 1000)`: `whole` is 1 and the exact value is 1, so 1. `benchPercent(3, 1000)`: `whole` is 1 but the exact value is 0.3, so `ceil(3) / 10` = 0.3. `benchPercent(1, 5000)`: `ceil(0.2)` = 1, so 0.1. The "P <= 50" check uses the exact P, so 50.0001 is out and exactly 50 is in.)

`bench-rank.ts`:

```ts
import { sql } from './db';
import { listedRanked, standingOf } from './person-score';
import { BENCH_CHAINS, CHAIN_CHIP_MIN, CHAIN_LABEL, benchRank, type BenchChain } from './rank';

/**
 * v2.13 spec "Chains (R14)": a ranked project is on a chain when builder_projects.chains (T4) names
 * it. The pool of a chain is the listed ranked people whose one project is on it. A chain's line (and
 * its chip on the Bench) only from CHAIN_CHIP_MIN ranked projects in its pool.
 */
const pool = (only?: number[]) => {
  const db = sql();
  return db`(SELECT l.builder_id, l.score, b.chains FROM ${listedRanked(db, only)} l
    JOIN builder_profiles p ON p.builder_id = l.builder_id
    JOIN builder_projects b ON b.builder_id = l.builder_id AND lower(b.project_key) = lower(p.main_project_key))`;
};

export async function chainPools(o: { only?: number[] } = {}): Promise<Record<BenchChain, number>> {
  const rows = await sql()<{ chain: string; n: number }[]>`
    SELECT c AS chain, count(*)::int AS n FROM ${pool(o.only)} x, unnest(x.chains) c GROUP BY c`;
  const out = Object.fromEntries(BENCH_CHAINS.map((c) => [c, 0])) as Record<BenchChain, number>;
  for (const r of rows) if ((BENCH_CHAINS as readonly string[]).includes(r.chain)) out[r.chain as BenchChain] = r.n;
  return out;
}

export type RankLines = { bench: string | null; phrase: string | null; chains: { chain: BenchChain; label: string; line: string }[] };

export async function rankLines(builderId: number, o: { only?: number[] } = {}): Promise<RankLines> {
  const s = await standingOf(builderId, o);
  const words = benchRank(s);
  const out: RankLines = { bench: words?.short ?? null, phrase: words?.phrase ?? null, chains: [] };
  if (!s.ranked) return out;
  const [me] = await sql()<{ chains: string[]; score: number }[]>`
    SELECT b.chains, l.score FROM builder_profiles p JOIN builder_projects b ON b.builder_id = p.builder_id AND lower(b.project_key) = lower(p.main_project_key)
    JOIN builder_live l ON l.builder_id = p.builder_id WHERE p.builder_id = ${builderId}`;
  if (!me) return out;
  const pools = await chainPools(o);
  for (const chain of BENCH_CHAINS) {
    if (!me.chains.includes(chain) || pools[chain] < CHAIN_CHIP_MIN) continue;
    const [r] = await sql()<{ total: number; rank: number }[]>`
      SELECT (count(*) + CASE WHEN bool_or(x.builder_id = ${builderId}) THEN 0 ELSE 1 END)::int AS total,
        (1 + count(*) FILTER (WHERE x.score > ${me.score} AND x.builder_id <> ${builderId}))::int AS rank
      FROM ${pool(o.only)} x WHERE ${chain} = ANY(x.chains)`;
    const w = benchRank({ ranked: true, rank: r.rank, total: r.total }, { chain });
    if (w) out.chains.push({ chain, label: CHAIN_LABEL[chain], line: w.short });
  }
  return out;
}
```

(7.4: 19 listed; the top Base score is 59, above every Arc score (40 to 48): "#1 of 19 on the Bench" and "#1 of 10 on Base". Arc has 9: no line. The lowest Base score, 50, is 10th of 19 (the last place of the top half) and 10th of 10 on Base (outside that pool's top half).)

`fixtures/banned-words.ts` (append):

```ts
/**
 * v2.13 plan B17: a rank line ("Top 1% on Base", "#3 of 12 on Arc") may name a chain, and a fact
 * chip may count commits ("12 commits"); both are stripped before the check. BENCH_EXTRA: words the
 * v2.13 surfaces never use ("signal", "written by", the builder bonus).
 */
export const BENCH_ALLOWED: RegExp[] = [
  /\b(?:[Tt]op \d+(?:\.\d)?%|#\d+ of \d+(?: builders?)?) on (?:Base|Arc|Robinhood Chain)\b/g,
  /\b\d[\d,]* commits?\b/g,
];
export const BENCH_EXTRA: RegExp[] = [/\bsignals?\b/i, /written by/i, /\bbonus\b/i];
export function bannedInBench(text: string, exempt: string[]): string[] {
  let t = text;
  for (const r of BENCH_ALLOWED) t = t.replace(r, ' ');
  return [...bannedIn(t, exempt), ...BENCH_EXTRA.filter((r) => r.test(t)).map(String)];
}
```

```ts
  const coin: BenchAsk = { kind: 'coin', project_key: 'k', line: 'raise?' };
  const build: BenchAsk = { kind: 'build', project_key: null, line: buildAsk('a tip jar on Arc') };
  const enter: BenchAsk = { kind: 'enter', project_key: 'k', program_id: 'p', line: enterAsk('Base Builder Grants') };
  const base = { project: RECIPE, canHost: true, updatesOn: true, postedThisWeek: true, scheduled: true, coin, build, enter, declined: [] as string[] };
  assert.equal(chooseAsk({ ...base, project: null })?.kind, 'build', '1. no project');

  assert.deepEqual(chooseAsk({ ...base, project: { ...RECIPE, live: false } }), { kind: 'go_live', project_key: 'maya/recipes', line: goLiveAsk('Recipe planner') }, '2. not live');
  assert.equal(chooseAsk({ ...base, project: { ...RECIPE, live: false }, canHost: false })?.kind, 'coin', 'not live but nothing can host it');
  assert.deepEqual(chooseAsk({ ...base, updatesOn: false }), { kind: 'updates', project_key: 'maya/recipes', line: UPDATES_ASK }, '3. updates not on');
  assert.equal(chooseAsk({ ...base, updatesOn: false, project: { ...RECIPE, confirmed: false } })?.kind, 'coin', 'asked only once confirmed');
  assert.deepEqual(chooseAsk({ ...base, postedThisWeek: false, scheduled: false }), { kind: 'post_update', project_key: 'maya/recipes', line: POST_UPDATE_ASK }, '4.');
  assert.equal(chooseAsk({ ...base, postedThisWeek: false, scheduled: true })?.kind, 'coin', 'an agent that schedules is not asked');
  assert.equal(chooseAsk(base)?.kind, 'coin', '5. a coin moment');
  assert.equal(chooseAsk({ ...base, coin: null })?.kind, 'enter', '6. the best program');
  assert.equal(chooseAsk({ ...base, updatesOn: false, declined: ['updates:maya/recipes'] })?.kind, 'coin', 'a no keeps it away 30 days');
});
```

```ts
test('8.10 score under the flag: the same three parts, with rank_lines and the streak', dbTest, () =>
  benchOn(async () => {
    const id = await person({ main: 'mayas/recipes', claimed: 'mayas_x', ranked: true, score: 60, projects: [{ key: 'mayas/recipes', name: 'Recipe planner', confirmed: true, weeks_shipping: 3, streak: 3 }] });
    const r = (await tools.score({}, { installationId: id } as never)) as { show_to_builder: string; rank_lines: string[]; streak: number };
    assert.equal(r.show_to_builder.split('\n')[0], 'Where you stand');
    assert.equal(r.streak, 3);
    assert.ok(Array.isArray(r.rank_lines));
  }),
);
```

(`FRESH_ON` is the `freshDeps` the v2.11 journey uses to turn deploys and hosting on for `arc-tipjar`; copy it from `v211-journey.test.ts`. `fitsFor(programId, key)` gains the repo key argument.)

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`bench-reply.ts`:

```ts
import { pageUrl } from './reserved-routes';

/**
 * v2.13 spec "2a", "2b", "5. Ways to get funded", "The last line": the reply in three parts, ending
 * in at most one question. Plain words; chain names only inside a rank phrase; a funder's short
 * name and the starter's offer are names (plan B12). Pure.
 */
export const BENCH_HEADS = { stand: 'Where you stand', fund: 'Ways to get funded', up: 'How to move up' } as const;
export type BenchAskKind = 'build' | 'go_live' | 'updates' | 'post_update' | 'coin' | 'raise' | 'enter';
export type BenchAsk = { kind: BenchAskKind; project_key: string | null; line: string; program_id?: string };
export type FundLine =
  | { kind: 'fits'; title: string; prize: string; when: string }
  | { kind: 'idea'; who: string; prize: string; for: string }
  | { kind: 'rolling'; title: string; prize: string };
export type BenchProject = { key: string; name: string; confirmed: boolean; live: boolean; weeks: number };
export type BenchView = {
  project: BenchProject | null; rank: string | null; claimed: boolean; handle: string | null; claimLink: string | null;
  /** GitHub sign-in when the agent can't push the proof line (plan B3). */
  proofLink?: string | null;
  fund: FundLine[]; ask: BenchAsk | null;
  /** Money already on its way (v2.12.1 `due`: a deadline, an entry waiting on the builder's own submission). */
  due?: string[];
};

export const NO_PROJECT_LINE = "You're not on the Bench yet. Pick something to build and you're on it today.";
export const confirmingLine = (name: string) => `${name} joins the Bench as soon as I've confirmed it's yours (a minute or two).`;
export const showItsYoursLine = (name: string, link: string) => `${name} joins the Bench as soon as you show it's yours: ${link}`;
export const claimToJoinLine = (name: string, link: string) => `${name} goes on the Bench once you claim your page with X: ${link}`;
export const CONFIRMED_LINE = (name: string) => `${name} is confirmed as yours.`;
export function onBenchLine(name: string, rank: string | null, weeks: number): string {
  const w = weeks > 0 ? `${weeks} ${weeks === 1 ? 'week' : 'weeks'} shipping` : null;
  if (rank && w) return `${name} is on the Bench: ${rank}, ${w}.`;
  if (rank) return `${name} is on the Bench: ${rank}.`;
  return w ? `${name} is on the Bench, ${w}.` : `${name} is on the Bench.`;
}
/** `when` as stand-view earnWhen gives it: "open all year" or "closes Oct 14". */
export const fitsLine = (title: string, prize: string, name: string, when: string) =>
  `${title} pays ${prize} and fits ${name}; ${when === 'open all year' ? "it's open all year" : `it ${when}`}.`;
export const ideasLine = (ideas: { who: string; prize: string; for: string }[]) => ideas.map((i) => `${i.who} pays ${i.prize} for ${i.for}.`).join(' ');
export const rollingFundLine = (title: string, prize: string) => `${title} pays ${prize}; it's open all year.`;
export const UPDATES_ASK = 'Want me to post what you ship here every week? It keeps your streak going on the Bench.';
export const POST_UPDATE_ASK = "Want me to post this week's update?";
export const buildAsk = (offer: string) => `Want me to build ${offer} and put it online? It takes a few minutes and you won't have to do anything.`;
export const goLiveAsk = (name: string) => `Want me to put ${name} online? It takes a few minutes and you won't have to do anything.`;
export const enterAsk = (title: string) => `Want me to prepare an entry for ${title}?`;
export const benchStepKey = (kind: BenchAskKind, key: string | null) => `${kind}:${key ?? '*'}`;

export function chooseAsk(o: {
  project: BenchProject | null; canHost: boolean; updatesOn: boolean; postedThisWeek: boolean; scheduled: boolean;
  coin: BenchAsk | null; build: BenchAsk | null; enter: BenchAsk | null; declined: string[];
}): BenchAsk | null {
  const p = o.project;
  if (!p) return o.build;
  const off = (k: BenchAskKind) => o.declined.includes(benchStepKey(k, p.key));
  if (!p.live && o.canHost && !off('go_live')) return { kind: 'go_live', project_key: p.key, line: goLiveAsk(p.name) };
  if (p.confirmed && !o.updatesOn && !off('updates')) return { kind: 'updates', project_key: p.key, line: UPDATES_ASK };
  if (p.confirmed && o.updatesOn && !o.postedThisWeek && !o.scheduled && !off('post_update')) return { kind: 'post_update', project_key: p.key, line: POST_UPDATE_ASK };
  if (o.coin) return o.coin;
  return o.enter && !off('enter') ? o.enter : null;
}

function standLines(v: BenchView): string[] {
  const p = v.project;
  if (!p) return [NO_PROJECT_LINE];
  if (!p.confirmed) {
    const first = v.proofLink ? showItsYoursLine(p.name, v.proofLink) : confirmingLine(p.name);
    return [first, ...(!v.claimed && v.claimLink ? [`Claim your page with X: ${v.claimLink}`] : [])];
  }
  if (!v.claimed) return [v.claimLink ? claimToJoinLine(p.name, v.claimLink) : CONFIRMED_LINE(p.name)];
  return [onBenchLine(p.name, v.rank, p.weeks)];
}
function fundLines(v: BenchView): string[] {
  const name = v.project?.name ?? '';
  const ideas = v.fund.filter((f): f is Extract<FundLine, { kind: 'idea' }> => f.kind === 'idea');
  const rest = v.fund.filter((f) => f.kind !== 'idea').slice(0, 3).map((f) =>
    f.kind === 'fits' ? fitsLine(f.title, f.prize, name, f.when) : rollingFundLine(f.title, (f as { prize: string }).prize));
  return [...(v.due ?? []), ...rest, ...(ideas.length ? [ideasLine(ideas.slice(0, 2))] : [])];
}
export function benchReply(v: BenchView): string {
  const parts = [[BENCH_HEADS.stand, ...standLines(v)]];
  const fund = fundLines(v);
  if (fund.length) parts.push([BENCH_HEADS.fund, ...fund]);
  if (v.ask) parts.push([BENCH_HEADS.up, v.ask.line]);
  return parts.map((x) => x.join('\n')).join('\n\n');
}
export { pageUrl };
```

`starters.ts`: `Starter` gains `/** v2.13 (plan B12): the build ask's words, mid-sentence: 'a tip jar on Arc'. */ offer: string;` and `arc-tipjar` gets `offer: 'a tip jar on Arc'`.

`program-file.ts`: the program schema gains `pitch: z.object({ who: z.string().min(1).max(20), for: z.string().min(1).max(60) }).strict().optional()` (copy-rule plain words; no em dash, checked by `programs:check`'s existing text rules). `programs.ts` stores it in the existing program `details`/jsonb the card reads (follow how `fresh_build` reaches `Card`) and `present.ts Card` gains `pitch?: { who: string; for: string } | null`. Program files: `arc-microgrants.json` adds `"pitch": { "who": "Arc", "for": "payment apps" }`; `base-builder-grants.json` adds `"pitch": { "who": "Base", "for": "a live app" }`.

`bench-find.ts` (reads and writes; never throws on its own reads: each read falls back as `standFind` does):

```ts
export const BENCH_MOVE_UP_NOTE = 'A yes or no to the last line: call tell with move_up: { kind, project_key, answer } (kind and project_key as given here). Do the steps it returns yourself; never ask the builder to edit files.';
export const UPDATES_NOTE = BENCH_MOVE_UP_NOTE;
export const POST_UPDATE_NOTE = "Write this week's update now: one plain paragraph from git log for the week and what you deployed, at most 280 characters, links only to the live site or the repo. Call tell with update: { text }. Don't show this note.";
export const ENTER_NOTE = 'Prepare it: call prepare with program_id as given. Don\'t show this note.';
type Seen = { declined?: Record<string, string>; bench_ask?: { kind: BenchAskKind; key: string | null; program_id?: string } };
```

`benchFind(ctx, a)` takes the same `a` as `standFind` (`first`, `cards`, `repos`, `myEntries`, `lastCheck`, `pickup`, `ask`, `alerts`, `agentCanBuild`, `walletNote`, `programs`, `hide`) and:
1. `const id = ctx.installationId; const now = a.first.now;` reads `standBase(id, { fresh: ctx.freshDeps })` (claim link, claimed, handle, the `web` sign per project, `seen`), the main key (`mainProjectOf`), the main `builder_projects` row (`name, confirmed, shipped, kind, link, weeks_shipping, streak, people, has_coin`), `updates_on_at`, `updates_scheduled_at`, and this week's row (`SELECT text FROM project_updates WHERE lower(project_key) = lower(main) AND week = ${mondayOf(now)}`).
2. `project: BenchProject | null` = the main row as `{ key, name, confirmed, live: shipped && kind === 'app' || <a verified site is up>, weeks: weeks_shipping }`; when the main key is set but no row exists yet (the first find before the first rescore), `{ key, name: <identity name>, confirmed: false, live: false, weeks: 0 }`.
3. `rank` = `(await rankLines(id)).phrase` (only used once claimed and confirmed).
4. Fund: the rows `standFind` computes (`layout(m, entered).rows.filter(qualifies)`) filtered to `lower(r.key ?? r.repo) === lower(main)`, at most 3, as `{ kind: 'fits', title, prize: earnPrize(card), when: earnWhen(card) }`; none and a project: `moneyFallback(...)`'s rolling program as `{ kind: 'rolling', title, prize }`; no project: the build pick's program, then the biggest-prize other showable program, each as `{ kind: 'idea', who: card.pitch?.who ?? card.title, prize: earnPrize(card), for: card.pitch?.for ?? 'a small app' }`.
5. Asks: `build` = no project and a pick (`freshLead(a.first) ?? moneyFallback(...).pick`) → `{ kind: 'build', project_key: null, line: buildAsk(pick.starter.offer) }` with `build_offer` in the reply as today; `canHost` = `hostFor(a.agentCanBuild)(key)` and the project's `web` sign (stand-view); `enter` = the first fits row → `{ kind: 'enter', project_key: main, program_id, line: enterAsk(title) }`; `coin` = `coinAskBench(id, project, seen)`; `declined` = `declinedNow(seen, now)`. A pending join or an outcome question (`a.pickup`, `a.ask`) holds the ask as `standFind` does (no ask that reply).
6. The chosen ask is stored as `standing_seen.bench_ask = { kind, key, program_id }` (so the yes is answered as asked, never trusting the agent's kind).
7. News: `takeNews(id, read.seen, read.base, now, !asks)` as today, opened with `newsOpener(a.alerts.lines, [news])`.
8. Reply: `show_to_builder` = `[opener, benchReply(view), a.pickup?.question].filter(Boolean).join('\n\n')`; `note` = `['Show show_to_builder to the builder exactly as written.', EARN_NOTE]`; `move_up` = `{ kind, project_key, agent_note: kind === 'coin' || kind === 'raise' ? COIN_NOTE : kind === 'post_update' ? POST_UPDATE_NOTE : BENCH_MOVE_UP_NOTE }`; `lead` for the first fits row; `build_offer`; `proof` = `proofFor(id, id, { canPush: a.agentCanBuild, now })` (moved here from T2's temporary line); `proofLink` in the view = `proof.sign_in` when present; `claim` when the claim link shows; `schedule_weekly_update` = `WEEKLY_UPDATE_NOTE` when updates are on and `updates_scheduled_at` is null (B18; sent at most once more: record `standing_seen.weekly_note_at`); never `schedule_daily_check`; `ask_outcome`, `pick_up`, `matches`, `programs` and `working_on` as `standFind` returns them; `rank_lines` = `[bench, ...chains.map(c => c.line)]` filtered; `streak` = the main row's streak.

`coinAskBench(id, p, seen)`: when the project is confirmed, shipped, has people and no coin: `offerCoin(id, p.key, seen.people?.[p.key] !== undefined && seen.people[p.key] < 5 && p.people >= 5 ? 'first_five' : 'has_people', ...)` → `{ kind: 'coin', project_key: p.key, line: offer.line }`, else null (T9 adds `streak_five` and the raise ask for a coin launched before v2.13).

`benchMoveUp(ctx, m)`: reads `standing_seen.bench_ask`; a different kind or key than stored is `{ show_to_builder: 'Okay.' }`. A no stores `declined[benchStepKey(kind, key)] = now` (30 days, `DECLINE_MS`) and answers "Okay.". A yes:
- `updates` → `setUpdates(id, { on: true }, now)`;
- `post_update` → `{ show_to_builder: '', agent_note: POST_UPDATE_NOTE }`;
- `go_live` → the existing `goLiveYes(ctx, key, m.live_url)`;
- `build` → `{ show_to_builder: '', agent_note: BUILD_NOTE }` (the build path starts from `build_offer` as today);
- `enter` → `{ show_to_builder: '', lead: { program_id }, agent_note: ENTER_NOTE }`;
- `coin`, `raise` → the existing `coinAnswer` (T9 adds `raise`).

`tools.ts`:
- In `list_programs`, under the flag the matcher gets only the one project: when `benchEnabled()` and a main key or `project.repo` exists, call `matchProjects` with `repos: [project.repo ?? main]` and no `github_user` and no `local_repos` (the reported-login matching of v2.12.2 is skipped: spec "Program matching uses the one project only").
- Where `standFind` is called: `return benchEnabled() ? benchFind(ctx, args) : standFind(ctx, args)` (and T2's temporary `proof` line is removed).
- In `moveUpAnswer`: first line `if (benchEnabled()) return benchMoveUp(ctx, m);`.
- The build becomes the one project: at the `beforeSend: () => builtEvent(ctx, build, repoKey, proven.lineOwner)` call site, add after the event (under the flag): `await setMainProject(ctx.installationId, { name: build.name ?? repoKey.split('/')[1], what: build.description ?? null, repo: repoKey, live_url: null, package: null, contracts: [] }).catch(() => undefined);`.
- `score`: under the flag `scoreReply` delegates to `benchScore(ctx, now, o)`, which runs `ensureProfile`, `startRefresh(...).within()`, and builds the same view as `benchFind` with no matcher (fund from `scoreEarn`'s fits filtered to the main key, else its rolling line), returning `show_to_builder`, `move_up`, `rank_lines`, `streak`.

### Task 9: Raising through a tokens.fun coin

Spec "4. Optional: raise" and R8: offered at the coin moments and once at the fifth week in a row; the yes launches through the existing path and sets `raising_at`; "stop raising" clears it and the coin stays; a coin launched before v2.13 does not turn the pill on and gets "Want to show you're raising with $X?" once; the launch stores `coin_tx` and `coin_chain_id`.

**Files:**

- Create: `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/raising.ts`

**Interfaces:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`raising.test.ts`:

```ts
import { after, test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import { sql } from './db';
import { EVENT_LOG_FLAG } from './events';
import { RAISE_LINES, pillOf, raiseAsk, raisingCoin, setRaising, showRaisingLine } from './raising';
import { raiseLine } from './coin-moments';

process.env.EARNKIT_BUILDER_SCORE = 'on';
process.env.EARNKIT_BENCH = 'on';
process.env[EVENT_LOG_FLAG] = 'on';
const dbTest = { skip: !process.env.DATABASE_URL && 'no DATABASE_URL' };
after(async () => { if (process.env.DATABASE_URL) await sql().end(); });
const RUN = randomBytes(3).toString('hex');

async function withCoin(o: { launched?: boolean; raising?: boolean } = {}): Promise<{ id: number; key: string; pid: number }> {
  const [{ id }] = await sql()<{ id: number }[]>`INSERT INTO installations (token_hash) VALUES (${`v213-raise-${randomBytes(8).toString('hex')}`}) RETURNING id`;
  const key = `r${RUN}/p${id}`;
  await sql()`INSERT INTO builder_profiles (builder_id, slug, main_project_key) VALUES (${id}, ${`v213ra-${id}`}, ${key})`;
  const [{ pid }] = await sql()<{ pid: number }[]>`
    INSERT INTO projects (program_id, name, repo_url, repo_key, token_repo_key, installation_id, token_symbol, token_status, token_url, raising_at)
    VALUES ('earnkit-coin', 'Recipe planner', ${`https://github.com/${key}`}, ${key}, ${key}, ${id}, 'RECIPE', ${o.launched === false ? 'pending' : 'launched'},
      ${o.launched === false ? 'pending' : `https://tokens.fun/coin/${id}`}, ${o.raising ? new Date() : null}) RETURNING id AS pid`;
  return { id, key, pid };
}

test('9.1 the raise line names the coin before the yes, as the spec writes it; the wallet is shown when known (B13)', () => {
  assert.equal(raiseLine('Recipe planner', 'RECIPE', null), "Want to raise for Recipe planner? I'll launch $RECIPE on tokens.fun and you get 40% of its trading fees.");
  assert.equal(raiseLine('Recipe planner', 'RECIPE', '0x12ab000000000000000000000000000000009f3e'),
    "Want to raise for Recipe planner? I'll launch $RECIPE on tokens.fun and you get 40% of its trading fees, paid to 0x12ab…9f3e.");
  assert.equal(showRaisingLine('RECIPE'), "Want to show you're raising with $RECIPE?");
});

test('9.2 a coin launched before v2.13 does not show the pill until the builder says yes; stop raising removes it, the coin stays', dbTest, async () => {
  const { id, pid } = await withCoin();
  assert.equal(await pillOf(id), null, 'R8: not by itself');
  assert.deepEqual(await setRaising(id, true), { show_to_builder: RAISE_LINES.on('RECIPE') });
  assert.deepEqual(await pillOf(id), { symbol: 'RECIPE', url: `https://tokens.fun/coin/${id}` });
  assert.deepEqual(await setRaising(id, false), { show_to_builder: RAISE_LINES.off });
  assert.equal(await pillOf(id), null);
  const [p] = await sql()<{ token_status: string }[]>`SELECT token_status FROM projects WHERE id = ${pid}`;
  assert.equal(p.token_status, 'launched', 'the coin stays');
  assert.equal((await sql()`SELECT 1 FROM builder_events WHERE installation_id = ${id} AND type IN ('raising_on','raising_off')`).length, 2);
});

test('9.3 the pill shows only while the coin is launched and raising_at is set', dbTest, async () => {
  const { id } = await withCoin({ launched: false, raising: true });
  assert.equal(await pillOf(id), null);
  assert.deepEqual(await setRaising(id, true), { show_to_builder: RAISE_LINES.notLaunched });
});

test('9.4 the raise ask for an old coin comes once per project', dbTest, async () => {
  const { id, key } = await withCoin();
  const ask = await raiseAsk(id, key, {});
  assert.deepEqual(ask, { kind: 'raise', project_key: key, line: showRaisingLine('RECIPE') });
  assert.equal(await raiseAsk(id, key, { raise_asked: { [key]: new Date().toISOString() } }), null);
  await setRaising(id, true);
  assert.equal(await raiseAsk(id, key, {}), null, 'already raising');
});

test('9.5 tell coin { raising } through the tool; flag off it is ignored', dbTest, async () => {
  const { id } = await withCoin();
  const { tools } = await import('./tools');
  const on = (await tools.tell({ coin: { raising: true } }, { installationId: id } as never)) as { show_to_builder: string };
  assert.equal(on.show_to_builder, RAISE_LINES.on('RECIPE'));
  assert.ok(await raisingCoin(id));
});
```

`coin-moments.test.ts` (append):

```ts
test('9.7 under the flag every coin moment but built_live says the raise line; flag off the v2.12.1 lines stay', () => {
  process.env.EARNKIT_BUILDER_SCORE = 'on';
  const coin = { name: 'Recipe planner', symbol: 'RECIPE' };
  delete process.env.EARNKIT_BENCH;
  assert.equal(coinLine('has_people', 'Recipe planner', null, coin), "Recipe planner has people using it. Want to launch a coin, $RECIPE (Recipe planner), for it? You'd get 40% of its trading fees.");
  process.env.EARNKIT_BENCH = 'on';
  try {
    for (const m of ['countable', 'first_five', 'has_people', 'streak_five'] as const)
      assert.equal(coinLine(m, 'Recipe planner', null, coin), raiseLine('Recipe planner', 'RECIPE', null));
    assert.match(coinLine('built_live', 'Recipe planner', null, coin), /^It's live\./);
  } finally {
    delete process.env.EARNKIT_BENCH;
  }
});
```
