`coin-moments.ts`:

```ts
export type Moment = 'built_live' | 'countable' | 'first_five' | 'has_people' | 'streak_five';
/** v2.13 spec "4. Optional: raise": the coin is named before the yes; the wallet is shown when known (plan B13). Pure. */
export const raiseLine = (name: string, symbol: string, wallet: string | null) =>
  `Want to raise for ${name}? I'll launch $${symbol} on tokens.fun and you get 40% of its trading fees${wallet ? `, paid to ${shortWallet(wallet.toLowerCase())}` : ''}.`;
```

In `coinLine`, first line: `if (benchEnabled() && moment !== 'built_live' && coin) return raiseLine(name, coin.symbol, wallet);`. `offerCoin`'s `moment` type becomes `Exclude<Moment, 'built_live'>` (now includes `streak_five`). In `coinYes`, after the coin entry's `projects` row is made or reused (just before `saveOffer`), under the flag: `UPDATE projects SET raising_at = COALESCE(raising_at, now()) WHERE id = ${projectId}` and a `raising_on` event (`dedupe_key: raising_on:<project_id>:<day>`). The pill follows the launch: it shows only once `token_status = 'launched'`.

`raising.ts`:

```ts
import { sql } from './db';
import { noteEvent } from './events';
import { mainProjectOf } from './main-project';
import { benchStepKey, type BenchAsk } from './bench-reply';

/** v2.13 spec "4. Optional: raise" (R8): the one project's coin, the raising flag on it, the pill. */
export const RAISE_LINES = {
  on: (symbol: string) => `Done. Your page shows you're raising with $${symbol}.`,
  off: "Okay, your page no longer says you're raising. Your coin stays.",
  notLaunched: "Your coin isn't live yet. It shows once it is.",
} as const;
export const showRaisingLine = (symbol: string) => `Want to show you're raising with $${symbol}?`;

/** The one project's launched (or launching) coin entry, newest first. */
export async function raisingCoin(builderId: number) {
  const key = await mainProjectOf(builderId);
  if (!key) return null;
  const [r] = await sql()<{ project_id: number; symbol: string; url: string | null; status: string | null; raising: boolean }[]>`
    SELECT x.id AS project_id, x.token_symbol AS symbol, x.token_url AS url, x.token_status AS status, x.raising_at IS NOT NULL AS raising
    FROM projects x JOIN installations i ON i.id = x.installation_id
    WHERE COALESCE(i.builder_id, i.id) = ${builderId} AND x.token_url IS NOT NULL AND x.token_symbol IS NOT NULL
      AND lower(COALESCE(x.token_repo_key, x.repo_key)) = lower(${key})
    ORDER BY x.coin_launched_at DESC NULLS LAST, x.id DESC LIMIT 1`;
  return r ?? null;
}

export async function setRaising(builderId: number, on: boolean): Promise<{ show_to_builder: string }> {
  const c = await raisingCoin(builderId);
  if (!c) return { show_to_builder: 'Okay.' };
  if (on && c.status !== 'launched') return { show_to_builder: RAISE_LINES.notLaunched };
  if (on) await sql()`UPDATE projects SET raising_at = COALESCE(raising_at, now()) WHERE id = ${c.project_id}`;
  else await sql()`UPDATE projects SET raising_at = NULL WHERE id = ${c.project_id}`;
  const type = on ? 'raising_on' : 'raising_off';
  await noteEvent(type, () => ({ type, dedupe_key: `${type}:${c.project_id}:${new Date().toISOString()}`, installation_id: builderId, project_id: c.project_id }));
  return { show_to_builder: on ? RAISE_LINES.on(c.symbol) : RAISE_LINES.off };
}

export async function pillOf(builderId: number): Promise<{ symbol: string; url: string } | null> {
  const c = await raisingCoin(builderId);
  return c && c.status === 'launched' && c.raising && c.url ? { symbol: c.symbol, url: c.url } : null;
}

/** A coin launched before v2.13 (or before the yes): "Want to show you're raising with $X?", once per project. */
export async function raiseAsk(builderId: number, key: string, seen: { raise_asked?: Record<string, string> }): Promise<BenchAsk | null> {
  if (seen.raise_asked?.[key]) return null;
  const c = await raisingCoin(builderId);
  if (!c || c.status !== 'launched' || c.raising) return null;
  return { kind: 'raise', project_key: key, line: showRaisingLine(c.symbol) };
}
export async function markRaiseAsked(builderId: number, key: string, now: Date): Promise<void> {
  await sql()`UPDATE builder_profiles SET standing_seen = jsonb_set(standing_seen, '{raise_asked}',
    COALESCE(standing_seen->'raise_asked', '{}'::jsonb) || jsonb_build_object(${key}::text, ${now.toISOString()}::text)) WHERE builder_id = ${builderId}`;
}
```

`bench-find.ts coinAskBench(id, p, seen)` becomes, in this order: (1) the project has a launched coin: `raiseAsk(id, p.key, seen)` (and `markRaiseAsked` when the reply uses it); (2) no coin, confirmed and shipped, and the streak is 5 or more and no `coin_moments` row exists for it: `offerCoin(id, p.key, 'streak_five')`; (3) the v2.12.1 moments `first_five` and `has_people` as in T8. `benchMoveUp` answers a `raise` yes with `setRaising(id, true)` and a no by recording the decline (as every kind).

`tokens.ts deployToken`, in the `launched` write: add `coin_tx = ${outcome.tx_hash ?? null}, coin_chain_id = ${CHAIN_IDS[chain]}` to the `UPDATE projects SET token_url = ...` statement (both variants of `launched(withCoin)`). Internal columns, written with the flag off too (nothing reads them there).

and in `tell`: `if (coin) replies.push(coin.raising !== undefined && benchEnabled() ? await setRaising(ctx.installationId, coin.raising) : coin.answer && coin.project_key ? await coinAnswer(ctx, { project_key: coin.project_key, answer: coin.answer, wallet: coin.wallet }) : { show_to_builder: 'Okay.' });` (with the flag off, `raising` is never listed in the MCP schema; a `coin` without `answer` was a parse error before and is "Okay." now, unreachable through MCP). `mcp.ts`: under `benchEnabled()` the `coin` schema adds `raising: z.boolean().optional().describe("true when they say they're raising, false when they say stop raising")` and makes `project_key` and `answer` optional.

/** v2.13 spec R8: "Raising $RECIPE", linking to the coin's tokens.fun page. Factual: the coin's name only. */
export default function RaisingPill({ symbol, url, size = 'md' }: { symbol: string; url: string; size?: 'sm' | 'md' }) {
  const box = size === 'sm' ? 'h-7 px-3 text-xs' : 'h-9 px-4 text-sm';

      Raising ${symbol}
    </a>
  );
}

async function listed(score: number, o: { chains?: string[]; raising?: boolean; streak?: number } = {}): Promise<number> {
  const [{ id }] = await sql()<{ id: number }[]>`INSERT INTO installations (token_hash) VALUES (${`v213-bench-${randomBytes(8).toString('hex')}`}) RETURNING id`;
  const key = `bn${RUN}/p${id}`;
  await sql()`INSERT INTO builder_profiles (builder_id, slug, main_project_key, x_user_id, x_handle, x_name) VALUES (${id}, ${`v213bn-${id}`}, ${key}, ${`bnx${id}`}, ${`bn${RUN}${id}`}, 'Maya')`;
  await sql()`INSERT INTO builder_live (builder_id, score, parts, ranked, shipped, people, formula, calibration, computed_at) VALUES (${id}, ${score}, '{}'::jsonb, true, 1, 0, 'v2', 'v2-launch', now())`;
  await sql()`INSERT INTO builder_projects (builder_id, project_key, name, kind, confirmed, shipped, chains, streak) VALUES (${id}, ${key}, 'Recipe planner', 'app', true, true, ${o.chains ?? []}, ${o.streak ?? 0})`;
  if (o.raising)
    await sql()`INSERT INTO projects (program_id, name, repo_key, token_repo_key, installation_id, token_symbol, token_status, token_url, raising_at)
      VALUES ('earnkit-coin', 'Recipe planner', ${key}, ${key}, ${id}, 'RECIPE', 'launched', ${`https://tokens.fun/c/${id}`}, now())`;
  return id;
}

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`bench-data.ts`:

```ts
import { sql } from './db';
import { chainPools } from './bench-rank';
import { listedRanked } from './person-score';
import { avatarUrl, displayName } from './page-data';
import { BENCH_CHAINS, CHAIN_CHIP_MIN, benchRank, type BenchChain } from './rank';

/** v2.13 spec "The Bench": its rows and its URL filters (R9). */
export type BenchFilter = { chain: BenchChain | null; raising: boolean; weekly: boolean };
export const WEEKLY_STREAK = 4;
export const BENCH_MAX = 100;
const one = (v: string | string[] | undefined) => (typeof v === 'string' ? v : undefined);
export function parseBenchFilter(q: Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>): BenchFilter {
  const c = one(q.chain)?.toLowerCase();
  return { chain: (BENCH_CHAINS as readonly string[]).includes(c ?? '') ? (c as BenchChain) : null, raising: one(q.raising) === '1', weekly: one(q.weekly) === '1' };
}
export function filterHref(f: BenchFilter): string {
  const p = new URLSearchParams();
  if (f.chain) p.set('chain', f.chain);
  if (f.raising) p.set('raising', '1');
  if (f.weekly) p.set('weekly', '1');
  const s = p.toString();
  return s ? `/bench?${s}` : '/bench';
}
export function bestFact(p: { people_week: number; streak: number; weeks_shipping: number; shipped: boolean }): string {
  if (p.people_week > 0) return `${p.people_week.toLocaleString('en-US')} ${p.people_week === 1 ? 'person' : 'people'} this week`;
  if (p.streak >= 2) return `${p.streak} weeks in a row`;
  if (p.weeks_shipping > 0) return `${p.weeks_shipping} ${p.weeks_shipping === 1 ? 'week' : 'weeks'} shipping`;
  return p.shipped ? 'Live' : 'Building';
}
export type BenchRow = { rank: number; line: string | null; handle: string; name: string; avatar: string | null; raising: { symbol: string; url: string } | null; chains: BenchChain[]; project: string; best: string };

export async function benchRows(f: BenchFilter, o: { limit?: number; only?: number[] } = {}) {
  const db = sql();
  const rows = await db<{ score: number; rank: number; count: number; handle: string; x_name: string | null; avatar: string | null; chains: string[]; project: string;
    people_week: number; streak: number; weeks_shipping: number; shipped: boolean; symbol: string | null; url: string | null }[]>`
    WITH ranked AS (
      SELECT x.builder_id, x.score, rank() OVER (ORDER BY x.score DESC)::int AS rank, count(*) OVER ()::int AS count FROM ${listedRanked(db, o.only)} x)
    SELECT r.score, r.rank, r.count, p.x_handle AS handle, p.x_name, p.x_avatar_url AS avatar, b.chains, b.name AS project, b.people_week, b.streak, b.weeks_shipping, b.shipped,
      c.token_symbol AS symbol, c.token_url AS url
    FROM ranked r JOIN builder_profiles p USING (builder_id)
    JOIN builder_projects b ON b.builder_id = r.builder_id AND lower(b.project_key) = lower(p.main_project_key)
    LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT x.token_symbol, x.token_url FROM projects x JOIN installations i ON i.id = x.installation_id
      WHERE COALESCE(i.builder_id, i.id) = r.builder_id AND x.token_status = 'launched' AND x.raising_at IS NOT NULL
        AND lower(COALESCE(x.token_repo_key, x.repo_key)) = lower(p.main_project_key) ORDER BY x.id DESC LIMIT 1) c ON true
    WHERE (${f.chain}::text IS NULL OR ${f.chain} = ANY(b.chains))
      AND (NOT ${f.raising} OR c.token_symbol IS NOT NULL)
      AND (NOT ${f.weekly} OR b.streak >= ${WEEKLY_STREAK})
    ORDER BY r.score DESC, lower(p.x_handle) LIMIT ${Math.min(BENCH_MAX, o.limit ?? BENCH_MAX)}`;
  const [{ total }] = await db<{ total: number }[]>`SELECT count(*)::int AS total FROM ${listedRanked(db, o.only)} x`;
  const pools = await chainPools(o);
  return {
    count: total,
    chips: BENCH_CHAINS.filter((c) => pools[c] >= CHAIN_CHIP_MIN),
    rows: rows.map((r) => ({
      rank: r.rank, line: benchRank({ ranked: true, rank: r.rank, total })?.short.replace(/ on the Bench$/, '') ?? null,
      handle: r.handle, name: displayName(r.x_name, r.handle), avatar: avatarUrl(r.avatar),
      raising: r.symbol && r.url ? { symbol: r.symbol, url: r.url } : null,
      chains: BENCH_CHAINS.filter((c) => r.chains.includes(c)), project: r.project, best: bestFact(r),
    })),
  };
}
```

(The right-hand line is "Top 4%" or "#3 of 12": the Bench words without "on the Bench", since the page is the Bench. Rows outside the top half show the rank number only, `#N`, in the same column.)

`ChainLogo.tsx`:

```tsx
import { CHAIN_LABEL, type BenchChain } from '../../../lib/earnkit/rank';
/** v2.13 R14: a chain as its logo, the name as alt text and tooltip. */
export default function ChainLogo({ chain, size = 20 }: { chain: BenchChain; size?: number }) {
  // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element -- a local SVG
  return <img src={`/images/chains/${chain}.svg`} alt={CHAIN_LABEL[chain]} title={CHAIN_LABEL[chain]} width={size} height={size} className="inline-block shrink-0 rounded-[6px]" />;
}
```

Logos (plan B15): `base.svg` = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><rect width="24" height="24" rx="5" fill="#0000FF"/></svg>`; `arc.svg` and `robinhood.svg` are monograms until the owner's brand files replace them: `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><rect width="24" height="24" rx="12" fill="#1B2A5C"/><text x="12" y="16.5" text-anchor="middle" font-family="sans-serif" font-size="13" font-weight="600" fill="#F6F9FE">A</text></svg>` (`R` with fill `#CCFF00` text on `#0B0B0B` for Robinhood Chain).

`BenchView.tsx` (pure; reuses `Panel`, `Footer`, `Avatar`, `ChainLogo`, `RaisingPill`):
- Exports: `BENCH_TITLE = 'The Bench'`; `benchLine = (n: number) => \`${n.toLocaleString('en-US')} ${n === 1 ? 'builder' : 'builders'} who ship, ranked by what they shipped and who uses it.\``; `BENCH_EMPTY = 'No one here yet.'`; `FILTER_RAISING = 'Raising'`; `FILTER_WEEKLY = 'Shipping every week'`.
- Header `Panel`: h1 `BENCH_TITLE`, `benchLine(count)`.
- Filter bar (a `<nav aria-label="Filters">` of links, never a form): one chip per `chips` chain (a `ChainLogo` link to `filterHref({ ...filter, chain: filter.chain === c ? null : c })`, `aria-pressed` when on), then `FILTER_RAISING` and `FILTER_WEEKLY` links toggling their flag. Selected chips use `bg-ek-blue-bright text-ek-navy-deep`; others `border border-ek-blue-soft/40 text-ek-blue-light`. Sentence case, no all-caps.
- Rows: `<ol>`; each `<li data-row={handle}>` is a grid: `Avatar` (h-11 w-11), then name (link to `pagePath(handle)`, the whole row is the hit area as in `LeaderboardView`), `@handle`, the `RaisingPill size="sm"` when raising, the chain logos; second line the project name and `best`; on the right `line ?? \`#${rank}\`` in `font-funnel-display text-lg text-ek-white tabular-nums`. At phone width the right column stays (grid `grid-cols-[auto_minmax(0,1fr)_auto]`), no horizontal scroll.
- Empty: `BENCH_EMPTY`.

`bench-load.ts`:

```ts
import { unstable_cache } from 'next/cache';
import { PROFILE_TAG } from '../../lib/earnkit/profile-cache';
import { benchRows, filterHref, type BenchFilter } from '../../lib/earnkit/bench-data';
/** One cached read per filter variant (at most 16), 60 seconds; a hide, claim or rename expires them (PROFILE_TAG). */
export const cachedBench = (f: BenchFilter) =>
  unstable_cache(async () => benchRows(f), ['bench-v213', filterHref(f)], { revalidate: 60, tags: [PROFILE_TAG] })();
```

`app/bench/page.tsx`:

```tsx
import { notFound } from 'next/navigation';
import { benchEnabled } from '@/lib/earnkit/builder-score-flag';
import { parseBenchFilter } from '@/lib/earnkit/bench-data';
import BenchView, { BENCH_TITLE } from './BenchView';
import { cachedBench } from './bench-load';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';
export const metadata = { title: `${BENCH_TITLE} · EarnKit`, description: 'Builders who ship every week, ranked by what they shipped and who uses it.' };
type Props = { searchParams: Promise<Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>> };

/** v2.13 spec "The Bench": filters are links (R9). A 404 with the flag off. */
export default async function BenchPage({ searchParams }: Props) {
  if (!benchEnabled()) notFound();
  const filter = parseBenchFilter(await searchParams);
  const { rows, count, chips } = await cachedBench(filter);
  return <BenchView rows={rows} count={count} chips={chips} filter={filter} />;
}
```

`app/sitemap.ts`: when `benchEnabled()`, push `${SITE}/bench` (priority 0.8, daily) and `${SITE}/feed` (0.6, daily).

Spec "Profile (R10, R11, R12)": the header (X photo with an initials fallback, name, @handle, the Raising pill, the Bench line then each chain's line with its logo), the score ring with its four parts, the 52-square shipping grid with the streak, the Ship log tab (long soft-edged cards, newest first, chips alone for weeks with facts and no text, the Started card), the Project tab, the shortened sources line, and the card (photo, name, "Top 4% on the Bench", project name, the streak).

- Modify: `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/claim.ts` (`bindX` keeps the stored picture when X sends none)

- Consumes: `benchEnabled`, `last52`, `weekLabel` (T1); `factChips`, `startedLine`, `startedChips`, `WeekFacts` (T4); `rankLines` (T7); `pillOf` (T9); `ChainLogo`, `UpdateCard` (T6, T10); `RaisingPill` (T9); `MAX_V2` (score-v2.ts); `money`, `peopleUse`, `avatarUrl`, `displayName` (page-data.ts); `earnPrize`, `earnWhen` (stand-view.ts); `listPrograms`, `readFitRepos`, `openCuratedRows`, `repoCanWin` (as `scoreEarn`).

  - `type ProjectTabData = { name: string; what: string | null; live: string | null; repo: string | null; chains: BenchChain[]; people: number | null; money: string | null; coin: { symbol: string; url: string } | null; programs: { title: string; won: boolean }[]; funds: string[]; confirmed: boolean }`

  await sql()`INSERT INTO builder_projects (builder_id, project_key, name, what, kind, confirmed, shipped, streak, ship_weeks) VALUES (${id}, ${key}, 'Recipe planner', 'a recipe planner', 'app', true, true, 2, ${['2026-W41', '2026-W42']})`;
  await sql()`INSERT INTO project_updates (builder_id, project_key, week, text, facts) VALUES

    (${id}, ${key}, '2026-09-21', NULL, '{}'::jsonb)`;
  await sql()`UPDATE project_updates SET hidden = true WHERE project_key = ${key} AND week = '2026-10-05'`;

  assert.equal(d.log[1].started, 'Started tracking Recipe planner.');

  assert.equal(d.grid.filter((g) => g.on).length, 2);
  assert.equal(d.streak, 2);
});

`Avatar.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';

/**
 * v2.12.1 T11, v2.13 R12: a person's X picture (X's own image host only), else (none, or it fails to
 * load: a stale picture after an X change) the first letter of their handle on navy. Decorative.
 */
export default function Avatar({ src, handle, className = 'h-14 w-14 text-xl' }: { src: string | null; handle: string; className?: string }) {
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
  if (src && !failed)
    return (
      // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element -- X's image host; next/image would proxy it through this server
      <img src={src} alt="" referrerPolicy="no-referrer" loading="lazy" decoding="async" onError={() => setFailed(true)}
        className={`shrink-0 rounded-full bg-ek-navy object-cover ring-1 ring-ek-blue-soft/20 ${className}`} />
    );
  return (
    <span aria-hidden="true" className={`inline-flex shrink-0 select-none items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-ek-navy font-funnel-display font-medium text-ek-blue-light ring-1 ring-ek-blue-soft/20 ${className}`}>
      {handle.replace(/^_+/, '').charAt(0).toUpperCase() || '?'}
    </span>
  );
}
```

(Flag off: the same markup as today for both branches; `onError` only changes what happens after a failed load. The v2.12.1 page keeps using it.)

`claim.ts bindX`: `x_avatar_url = ${user.avatar}` becomes `x_avatar_url = COALESCE(${user.avatar}, x_avatar_url)`.

`bench-data.ts profileData(builderId, now)`:
- The claimed profile (`x_handle`, `x_name`, `x_avatar_url`; null without a claim), `main_project_key`, the main `builder_projects` row (`name, what, link, confirmed, chains, people, money_usd, streak, ship_weeks`), `builder_live` (`score`, `parts`, `pending`), `rankLines(builderId)`, `pillOf(builderId)`.
- `parts`: `[['Shipped', 'shipped'], ['Used', 'used'], ['Earned', 'earned'], ['Consistency', 'consistency']]` mapped to `{ label, points: Math.round(parts[k]?.points ?? 0), max: MAX_V2[k] }`; `score` only when the rank line reads (`bench !== null`), else null (v2.12.1: never a bare ring).
- `grid`: `last52(now).map((week) => ({ week, on: ship_weeks.includes(week) }))`.
- `log`: `SELECT week::text, text, facts FROM project_updates WHERE builder_id = $1 AND lower(project_key) = lower(main) AND NOT hidden ORDER BY week DESC LIMIT 60`; each row gives a card `{ week, text, chips: factChips(facts, { raising: pill?.symbol ?? null }) }` when `text` or `hasFacts(facts)`; a row with `facts.started` also gives the Started card `{ week, text: null, chips: startedChips(facts.started), started: startedLine(name) }` placed after that week's card (so it is the oldest); rows with neither are skipped.
- `project`: `{ name, what, live: link (a site), repo: main key is a GitHub repo ? \`https://github.com/${key}\` : null, chains, people: confirmed && !held ? people : null, money: confirmed && money_usd > 1 ? money(money_usd) : null, coin: <the launched coin's symbol and url>, programs: <as pageCards: entries matched by the main key, won by announcements>, funds: <the scoreEarn-style fit lines for the main key: fitsLine(...) without the project name, i.e. \`${title} pays ${prize}; ${when words}.\`>, confirmed }`.
- Private-repo rule (v2.12.1 `shownProjects`): when the main key is a repo whose newest reading is not public or is over a week old, `repo` is null and the name falls back to the agent's own name or "Private project".

`BenchProfileView.tsx` (pure server component; `Panel`, `Footer`, `Avatar`, `ScoreRing`, `ChainLogo`, `RaisingPill`, `UpdateCard`, `ShipGrid`, `ScoreParts`, `ProjectTab`):
- Exports: `TAB_LOG = 'Ship log'`, `TAB_PROJECT = 'Project'`, `BENCH_SOURCES_LINE = 'Facts come from public data EarnKit checks: GitHub, the live site, packages and public transactions.'`, `LOG_EMPTY = 'Updates show here every week.'`, `CONFIRMING_LINE = "This project shows here once it's confirmed as theirs."`, `streakWords = (n) => (n >= 1 ? \`${n} ${n === 1 ? 'week' : 'weeks'} in a row\` : null)`.
- Header panel: `Avatar` with a ring (`ring-2 ring-ek-score-orange/60`, h-16 w-16, md:h-20 md:w-20), the name (`font-funnel-display text-2xl md:text-4xl`), `@handle`, the `RaisingPill` when `raising`; under it one line: the Bench line, then for each chain line ` · ` + `ChainLogo` + the line.

import { Panel } from '../common/Panel';
export const BENCH_HOW_TITLE = 'How it works';
export const BENCH_STEPS = [
  'Add EarnKit to your agent and connect X.',
  'Your project goes on the Bench and your agent posts what you ship each week.',
  'Get funded: grants that fit, or raise with your own coin.',
];
/** v2.13 spec "Landing page" 5: three steps, as HowItWorks lays them out, no step about a bonus. */
export default function BenchHowItWorks() {
  return (
    <Panel label={BENCH_HOW_TITLE} className="py-12 md:py-16">
      <h2 className="font-funnel-display text-3xl font-medium text-ek-white md:text-5xl">{BENCH_HOW_TITLE}</h2>
      <ol className="mt-8 grid gap-4 md:mt-10 md:grid-cols-3">
        {BENCH_STEPS.map((s, i) => (
          <li key={s} className="rounded-[32px] bg-ek-navy px-7 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
            <p className="font-funnel-display text-4xl font-medium text-ek-blue-bright md:text-5xl">{i + 1}</p>
            <p className="mt-6 text-pretty font-funnel-display text-xl font-medium leading-tight text-ek-white md:text-2xl">{s}</p>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ol>
    </Panel>
  );
}

```ts
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import * as React from 'react';
import { renderToStaticMarkup } from 'react-dom/server';
import { bonusOn, bookingFor } from './bonus-rounds';
import { feeTable } from './policy';
import { TOOL_DESCRIPTIONS, toolDescriptions } from './mcp';
import TermsPage from '../../app/terms/page';
import { GET as llms } from '../../app/llms.txt/route';

(globalThis as { React?: typeof React }).React = React;
const on = () => { process.env.EARNKIT_BUILDER_SCORE = 'on'; process.env.EARNKIT_BENCH = 'on'; };
const off = () => { delete process.env.EARNKIT_BENCH; };

test('13.1 bonusOn is false under the flag: every program, so every deposit books to the Safe; flag off: today', () => {
  const p = { status: 'active', builder_bonus: true, campaign: null, starts_at: new Date('2026-10-01'), ends_at: new Date('2026-12-01') };
  off();
  assert.equal(bonusOn(p), true);
  on();
  try {
    assert.equal(bonusOn(p), false);
    assert.deepEqual(bookingFor(p as never, new Date('2026-10-15')), { destination: 'safe', reason: 'builder bonus off' });
  } finally { off(); }
});

test('13.2 the terms\' fee table: 40 / 10 / 50 under the flag, no builder bonus row; flag off: today\'s', () => {
  off();
  assert.ok(feeTable().some(([k]) => k === 'Builder bonus'));
  on();
  try {
    assert.deepEqual(feeTable(), [['Your project', '40%'], ['MINI buyback', '10%'], ['EarnKit', '50%']]);
    const terms = renderToStaticMarkup(React.createElement(TermsPage));
    assert.doesNotMatch(terms, /builder bonus/i);
  } finally { off(); }
});

test('13.3 no tool description or llms.txt line mentions the bonus under the flag; flag off llms.txt is byte for byte today\'s', async () => {
  on();
  try {
    for (const d of Object.values(toolDescriptions(true, true))) assert.doesNotMatch(d, /bonus/i, d);
    const body = await (await llms()).text();
    assert.doesNotMatch(body, /bonus/i);
    assert.match(body, /40% of trading fees/);
  } finally { off(); }
  assert.equal(await (await llms()).text(), readFileSync(new URL('./llms/v212.txt', import.meta.url), 'utf8'));
  assert.match(TOOL_DESCRIPTIONS.confirm, /./);
});

test('13.4 the bonus pages are 404 under the flag; the sitemap lists no round', async () => {
  for (const f of ['../../app/bonus/[round]/page.tsx', '../../app/admin/bonus/[round]/page.tsx'])
    assert.match(readFileSync(new URL(f, import.meta.url), 'utf8'), /if \(benchEnabled\(\)\) notFound\(\)/, f);
  assert.match(readFileSync(new URL('../../app/sitemap.ts', import.meta.url), 'utf8'), /!benchEnabled\(\)/);
});

test('13.5 sponsor campaigns are untouched: no campaign file imports the Bench switch', () => {
  for (const f of ['./campaigns.ts', './sponsor-rules.ts', './escrow.ts'])
    assert.doesNotMatch(readFileSync(new URL(f, import.meta.url), 'utf8'), /benchEnabled/, f);
});
```

`bonus-ledger.test.ts` (append, beside the existing `feeRecipients` cases and their fake `rpc.rewardInfo`):

```ts
test('13.6 fee recipients are read from both EarnKit splits: v2.5 (4 slots) and v2.13 (3 slots: Safe 50, creator 40, MINI 10)', async () => {
  const coin = `0x${'7'.repeat(40)}` as Hex;
  const creator = `0x${'8'.repeat(40)}`;
  const mini = `0x${'9'.repeat(40)}`;
  const chain = { lockers: [`0x${'1'.repeat(40)}`] } as never;
  const rpc = (bps: number[]) => ({ rewardInfo: async () => ({ recipients: [`0x${'5'.repeat(40)}`, creator, mini, ...(bps.length === 4 ? [`0x${'6'.repeat(40)}`] : [])], bps }) }) as never;
  assert.deepEqual(await feeRecipients(coin, { rpc: rpc([5000, 4000, 1000]), chain }), { creator, mini });
  assert.deepEqual(await feeRecipients(coin, { rpc: rpc([3000, 4000, 1000, 2000]), chain }), { creator, mini });
  const unread: Hex[] = [];
  assert.equal((await feeRecipients(coin, { rpc: rpc([5000, 3000, 2000]), chain, unread })).creator, null, 'any other split is unread');
  assert.deepEqual(unread, [coin]);
});
```

(Seed and clean any `projects.fee_recipients` cache row as the file's other `feeRecipients` cases do.)

`cron.test.ts` (append):

```ts
test('13.7 under the flag the cron runs the ledger without rounds and no traction scoring; flag off: both', async () => {
  // As the file's ledger cases: deps.bonus and deps.traction set to recording fakes.
  const calls: string[] = [];
  const deps = { bonus: { ...LEDGER_FAKE, rounds: undefined }, traction: { score: async () => { calls.push('traction'); return { scored: 0 }; } } } as never;
  process.env.EARNKIT_BUILDER_SCORE = 'on';
  process.env.EARNKIT_BENCH = 'on';
  try {
    const r = await runCron({ now: new Date(), budgetMs: 5_000, deps, only: [] });
    assert.ok(!calls.includes('traction'));
    assert.equal((r as { bonus?: { rounds?: unknown } }).bonus?.rounds, undefined);
  } finally {
    delete process.env.EARNKIT_BENCH;
  }
});
```

(`LEDGER_FAKE` is the file's existing fake `BonusDeps`; the traction fake follows the shape `scoreOpenRounds` takes through `deps.traction`. Adjust the field names to the file's own fakes.)

import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { benchEnabled } from '@/lib/earnkit/builder-score-flag';
export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';
/** v2.13: today's llms.txt with the flag off (byte for byte), the Bench's with it on. */
export async function GET() {
  const name = benchEnabled() ? 'v213.txt' : 'v212.txt';
  for (const dir of [join(process.cwd(), 'lib/earnkit/llms'), join(process.cwd(), 'apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/llms')]) {
    try {
      return new Response(await readFile(join(dir, name), 'utf8'), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8', 'Cache-Control': 'public, max-age=600' } });
    } catch {
      // Try the next place.
    }
  }
  return new Response('Not found', { status: 404 });
}

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`lib/launch/clankerRewards.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * EarnKit launches (v2.13): platform = EarnKit's Safe 50 / creator 40 / MINI buyback 10, of the
 * post-cut pool. The v2.5 builder bonus slot (20) is gone; its share is the Safe's (the company
 * treasury). Coins launched before keep their 4-slot split, fixed at launch. Index 0 is the Safe:
 * the revenue indexer (lib/fees) must recognize EARNKIT_SAFE as a platform wallet for source =
 * 'earnkit' tokens.
 */
export const EARNKIT_REWARD_BPS = { platform: 5000, creator: 4000, miniBuyback: 1000 } as const;

export function buildEarnKitRewardRecipients(w: { safe: Address; creator: Address; miniBuyback: Address }): ClankerRewardRecipient[] {
  const slot = (recipient: Address, admin: Address, bps: number): ClankerRewardRecipient => ({ admin, recipient, bps, token: "Paired" });
  const wallets = [w.safe, w.creator, w.miniBuyback].map((a) => a.toLowerCase());
  if (new Set(wallets).size !== wallets.length) {
    throw new Error("EarnKit reward wallets must be distinct (Safe, creator, MINI buyback)");
  }
  const r = [
    slot(w.safe, w.safe, EARNKIT_REWARD_BPS.platform),
    slot(w.creator, w.creator, EARNKIT_REWARD_BPS.creator),
    slot(w.miniBuyback, w.safe, EARNKIT_REWARD_BPS.miniBuyback),
  ];
  if (r.reduce((a, x) => a + x.bps, 0) !== 10000) throw new Error("EarnKit reward bps must sum to 10000");
  return r;
}
```

(Keep `EARNKIT_ROLE_LABELS` and `earnkitRewardRoleLabel` with a comment: "the v2.5 coins' 4 slots; v2.13 coins use the default labels".)

`app/api/v1/deploy/route.ts`:
- Comment block at line ~60: "EarnKit's split (v2.13): the Safe that stands in for "platform" on EarnKit coins. EARNKIT_BONUS_WALLET (v2.5's builder bonus wallet) is optional: when set it is only kept out of the creator slot."
- The first 503 check: `if (caller === "earnkit" && (!EARNKIT_SAFE || !isAddress(EARNKIT_SAFE)))`.
- The distinct check: `distinctWallets([EARNKIT_SAFE, ...(BUYBACK_WALLET ? [BUYBACK_WALLET] : [])])`.
- The creator check: `const bonusWallet = EARNKIT_BONUS_WALLET && isAddress(EARNKIT_BONUS_WALLET) ? [EARNKIT_BONUS_WALLET] : [];` then `distinctWallets([body.creatorWallet, EARNKIT_SAFE, ...bonusWallet, ...(BUYBACK_WALLET ? [BUYBACK_WALLET] : [])])` with the error `creatorWallet can't be EarnKit's Safe or the MINI buyback wallet`.
- The split block: the comment becomes "EarnKit callers (v2.13) get platform (EarnKit's Safe) 50 / creator 40 / MINI buyback 10"; `buildEarnKitRewardRecipients({ safe: EARNKIT_SAFE, creator: body.creatorWallet as Address, miniBuyback: BUYBACK_WALLET as Address })`; `platformPct = EARNKIT_REWARD_BPS.platform / 100`, `creatorPct`, `effectiveMiniPct` as before; `bonusPct` and its declaration and the `...(bonusPct !== undefined ? { bonus: bonusPct } : {})` line in the response are removed.
- `EARNKIT_BONUS_WALLET` stays a module-level read like the other env values (14.5 calls `vi.resetModules()` before each post, so the route sees the changed env).

### Task 16: The journey: acceptance steps 1 to 7 on fakes

One test file walks the spec's "Acceptance test" through the real flag-on code paths in its own throwaway database, so a regression in any task breaks a step the owner will run.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/v213-journey.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: every task; the journey scaffold of `v2121-journey.test.ts` (its own database from `db/schema.sql`, every real program file through `ProgramFile` and `upsertProgram`, the env block, the fetch that refuses every outside host and notes it, the fake X `XOAuthDeps`, `claimGet`, `claimPost`, `xCallback`).
- Produces: `v213-journey.test.ts` cases 16.1 to 16.8.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the journey**

Copy the scaffold of `v2121-journey.test.ts` (lines 1 to the end of its `before`/`after` hooks) with `journeyDb = earnkit_v213_journey_${process.pid}`, `ENV` adding `EARNKIT_BENCH: 'on'`, and fakes for: GitHub by-name GraphQL (`reposQuery` answers for `maya/recipes` with `commit_counts` from three consecutive weeks; the fake asserts it is never sent `PERSON_GITHUB_QUERY` or `OWNED_REPOS_QUERY`), GitHub REST (the proof-line pusher's events and contents reads answering that `maya` pushed the line `tokenLineFor(id, 'https://github.com/maya/recipes')`; `/users/maya` as a 400-day-old User with a public repo), the site probe (`https://recipes.app` up), tokens.fun (`/api/check-ticker` free; `/api/v1/deploy` answering `{ tokenAddress, txHash: '0x' + 'c'.repeat(64), chainId: 8453 }`), and X (`me` returns `{ id: 'x-maya', username: 'maya_makes', name: 'Maya Chen', avatar: 'https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1/m_400x400.jpg' }`). `ctx` for every tool call: `{ installationId: id, readerDeps: FAKE_READER, tokenDeps: FAKE_TOKENS, freshDeps: FRESH_ON }`.

```ts
test('16.1 acceptance 1, with a project: the first reply names it and carries the claim link and the proof; after X and the proof line it is on the Bench; the page shows the photo, the Started card and the Project tab', db, async () => {
  const id = await newInstall();
  const r1 = await tools.find({ identity: { github_login: 'maya', project: { name: 'Recipe planner', what: 'a recipe planner', repo: 'https://github.com/maya/recipes.git', live_url: 'https://recipes.app' } } }, ctx(id)) as Reply;
  assert.equal(r1.show_to_builder.split('\n')[1], confirmingLine('Recipe planner'));
  const link = r1.claim!.link;
  await claimThroughX(link); // claimGet, claimPost, xCallback with the fake X
  // The agent pushed the line: the next reply (the refresh may run after 60 s while confirming, B4).
  await advanceRefreshGuard(id, 61_000);
  const r2 = await tools.find({}, ctx(id)) as Reply;
  assert.match(r2.show_to_builder, /^Recipe planner is on the Bench(: [^,]+)?, 3 weeks shipping\.$/m);
  assert.ok(r2.show_to_builder.endsWith(UPDATES_ASK));
  const page = await renderProfile('maya_makes', 'log');
  assert.match(page, /src="https:\/\/pbs\.twimg\.com\/profile_images\/1\/m_400x400\.jpg"/);
  assert.ok(page.includes('Started tracking Recipe planner.'));
  assert.ok((await renderProfile('maya_makes', 'project')).includes('https://recipes.app'));
});

test('16.2 acceptance 2, without a project: ways to get funded and one build offer; the build becomes the one project', db, async () => {
  const id = await newInstall();
  const r = await tools.find({ agent_can_build: true }, ctx(id)) as Reply;
  assert.match(r.show_to_builder, /^You're not on the Bench yet\./m);
  assert.ok(r.show_to_builder.endsWith(buildAsk('a tip jar on Arc')));
  await runStarterBuild(id, r.build_offer!.program_id); // the v2.11 journey's build steps with the fakes: prepare build start, twist, deploy, host
  assert.equal(await mainProjectOf(id), 'maya2/tipjar');
});

test('16.3 acceptance 3, the weekly update: yes, then "post this week\'s update now": a long card with chips; with a fact it reaches /feed', db, async () => {
  const id = MAYA.id;
  const yes = await tools.tell({ move_up: { kind: 'updates', project_key: 'maya/recipes', answer: 'yes' } }, ctx(id)) as Reply;
  assert.ok(yes.schedule_weekly_update);
  const posted = await tools.tell({ update: { text: 'Added fridge photo import, so it plans a week of meals from a picture.' } }, ctx(id)) as Reply;
  assert.equal(posted.show_to_builder, UPDATE_LINES.postedAt('earnkit.com/maya_makes'));
  assert.match(await renderProfile('maya_makes', 'log'), /data-shape="long"[\s\S]*commits/);
  // The week finishes: the Monday step and the daily rank, then the feed.
  const monday = nextMonday15(NOW);
  await runCron({ now: monday, deps: { builderScore: { only: [id], reader: FAKE_READER } }, only: [] });
  const feed = await feedWeeks(monday, { only: [id] });
  assert.ok(feed[0].cards.some((c) => c.text?.startsWith('Added fridge photo import')));
});

test('16.4 acceptance 4, Bench filters: /bench?chain=base lists only Base projects with the Base logo; the profile shows the Base line (#N of M under 20)', db, async () => {
  await seedBaseBuilders(10); // 10 listed builders whose one project has an EarnKit deploy on Base
  const base = await benchRows(parseBenchFilter({ chain: 'base' }));
  assert.ok(base.rows.length >= 10 && base.rows.every((r) => r.chains.includes('base')));
  assert.match(renderToStaticMarkup(React.createElement(BenchView, { ...base, filter: parseBenchFilter({ chain: 'base' }) })), /alt="Base"/);
  assert.match(await renderProfile(BASE_HANDLE, 'log'), /#\d+ of \d+ on Base|Top \d+(\.\d)?% on Base/);
});

test('16.5 acceptance 5, raise: the yes launches the coin, the pill shows on the profile, the Bench row and the card; stop raising removes it', db, async () => {
  const id = MAYA.id;
  await giveFivePeople(id); // five counted callers on the project's tracked contract: the first_five moment
  const offer = await tools.find({}, ctx(id)) as Reply;
  assert.match(offer.show_to_builder, /Want to raise for Recipe planner\? I'll launch \$\w+ on tokens\.fun and you get 40% of its trading fees/);
  const yes = await tools.tell({ coin: { project_key: 'maya/recipes', answer: 'yes', wallet: MAYA.wallet } }, ctx(id)) as Reply & { project_id: number };
  await tools.confirm({ project_id: yes.project_id, yes: true, wallet: MAYA.wallet }, ctx(id));
  assert.ok(await pillOf(id));
  assert.ok((await renderProfile('maya_makes', 'log')).includes('Raising $'));
  assert.ok((await benchRows(parseBenchFilter({ raising: '1' }))).rows.some((r) => r.handle === 'maya_makes'));
  const [p] = await sql()<{ coin_tx: string; coin_chain_id: number }[]>`SELECT coin_tx, coin_chain_id FROM projects WHERE id = ${yes.project_id}`;
  assert.deepEqual(p, { coin_tx: `0x${'c'.repeat(64)}`, coin_chain_id: 8453 });
  await tools.tell({ coin: { raising: false } }, ctx(id));
  assert.equal(await pillOf(id), null);
});

test('16.6 acceptance 6, the landing page: the Bench top 10 and the square cards', db, async () => {
  const html = renderToStaticMarkup(React.createElement(HomeView, { landing: await readLanding(nextMonday15(NOW)) }));
  assert.ok(html.includes('Ship every week. Get funded.') && html.includes('The Bench') && html.includes('This week on the Bench'));
  assert.match(html, /data-shape="square"/);
});

test('16.7 acceptance 7, no builder bonus text anywhere a builder can see', db, async () => {
  const texts = [
    (await tools.find({}, ctx(MAYA.id)) as Reply).show_to_builder,
    renderToStaticMarkup(React.createElement(HomeView, { landing: await readLanding(NOW) })),
    renderToStaticMarkup(React.createElement(TermsPage)),
    await (await llms()).text(),
    ...Object.values(toolDescriptions(true, true)),
  ];
  for (const t of texts) assert.doesNotMatch(t, /bonus/i);
});

test('16.8 no network: every outside read went through a fake, and no person query was sent', db, () => {
  assert.deepEqual(blocked, []);
  assert.ok(!graphqlSent.some((q) => q === PERSON_GITHUB_QUERY || q === OWNED_REPOS_QUERY));
});
```

The helpers are written in this file, each from the existing journey's pieces: `newInstall` (`saveInstallation`, `touchInstallation`), `claimThroughX(link)` (v2121 journey's claim block), `advanceRefreshGuard(id, ms)` (`UPDATE builder_live SET read_at = read_at - interval`), `renderProfile(handle, tab)` (`HandleView({ handle, path: '/' + handle, tab })` resolved and rendered with `renderToStaticMarkup`), `runStarterBuild` (the v2.11 journey's build steps 13.1 to 13.4 with the fakes, repo `maya2/tipjar`), `nextMonday15(now)` (the next Monday at 15:30 UTC), `seedBaseBuilders(n)` (listed people whose main project has a `contract_deploys` row on Base, then `rescorePerson` for each), `giveFivePeople(id)` (five Blockscout callers on a tracked contract of the project, then `refreshPerson`), `MAYA` (the 16.1 person's id and wallet, set by 16.1), `BASE_HANDLE` (the highest of `seedBaseBuilders`).

- [ ] **Step 2: Run it**

Run: `DATABASE_URL=postgres://postgres@127.0.0.1:55442/postgres?sslmode=disable pnpm exec tsx --test --import ./apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/fixtures/test-guard.ts apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/v213-journey.test.ts`
Expected: PASS once T1 to T13 have landed (fix the task whose step fails; never the journey's expectation, unless the spec says otherwise).

- [ ] **Step 3: The whole suite**

Run (DB URL): `pnpm -s test`, `pnpm db:check`, `pnpm lint`, `pnpm build`. Expected: PASS.

- [ ] **Step 4: Commit**

```bash
git add apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/v213-journey.test.ts
git commit -m "test(v2.13): the journey through acceptance steps 1 to 7

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>"
```

## Self-review

- **Spec coverage:** every section of the spec maps to a task (table below). Where the spec is silent or conflicts with the code, rulings B1 to B18 decide, each with its test.
- **Placeholder scan:** no "TBD", no "handle edge cases", no "similar to Task N". Where a test reuses an existing helper (`launchedThroughFake`, `claimedPerson`, `LEDGER_FAKE`, the v2121 and v211 journey scaffolds), the step names the file and says to extract it if it is inlined.
- **Type consistency:** `BenchChain`, `BENCH_CHAINS`, `CHAIN_LABEL` live in `rank.ts` (T4) and are imported by `person-score.ts`, `bench-rank.ts`, `bench-data.ts`, `ChainLogo`. `WeekFacts` (T4) is the shape of `project_updates.facts` everywhere (T5 writes it, T6 ranks it, T11 renders it). `BenchAsk`/`BenchAskKind` (T8) are what `raising.ts raiseAsk` (T9) returns. `mondayOf` keys rows; `isoWeekOf` keys events and `ship_weeks`. `benchRank(...).short` is the page and Bench text, `.phrase` the reply's. `FeedCard` (T6) and `ProfileCard` (T11) both fit `UpdateCard`'s `card` prop.
- **Review focus:** each of the five is pinned by a named test in its owning task (listed in "Review Focus").
- **Flag-off parity:** every changed function either checks `benchEnabled()` before its new behaviour or only adds internal columns no flag-off path reads (`chains`, `streak`, `weeks_shipping`, `ship_weeks`, `coin_tx`, `coin_chain_id`, `commit_counts`); the only always-on changes are `Avatar`'s `onError` (same markup), `bindX`'s keep-the-picture (no visible change until X sends no picture) and `feeRecipients` reading a split that does not exist until T14 ships.
